01699         write_phase('r03-second', second)
01700         assert win32.closed.count(target) == 1, \
01701             'the read end was closed a second time'
01702         assert reader.handle is None
01703     finally:
01704         for handle in list(win32.live):
01705             RecordingWin32.close_handle(win32, handle)
01706 
01707 
01708 # -- N10-R04: the aggregate finds the partial channel ---------------------
01709 
01710 def test_n10_r04_the_launch_aggregate_finds_the_partial_channel():
01711     """N10-R04 (M07): the partial channel has an owner.
01712 
01713     Obligation: the channel of a launch registers itself with the
01714     **aggregate** of the launch before it builds anything, so that a
01715     channel whose reader thread did not start is found by the outer
01716     owner of the launch and never through a local variable that is
01717     ``None`` at that moment.  The process helper never closes the
01718     standard input and the standard output of a process whose channel
01719     took them: the channel is coordinated first and the files of the
01720     process are closed by the channel alone.  Facts that are not
01721     complete keep the original owner.
01722 
01723     Entry: ``StreamChannel.__init__``, ``_LaunchAggregate.retain``,
01724     ``_LaunchAggregate.release`` and ``_adopt_broker_process``.
01725     Trigger: the reader thread of the channel really starts and the
01726     start of the thread does not return, so that the construction of the
01727     channel fails with the channel already half built.  Oracle: the
01728     aggregate found the partial channel, the files of the process were
01729     closed by the channel and never by the process helper.  Negative:
01730     the process helper never closes the files of a process whose
01731     channel took them.  Returned state: the facts of the aggregate
01732     name what it released.  Salvage: the owner of the test releases the
01733     handles that the product kept.
01734 
01735     RECONCILIATION (N1 correction 12): the **ownership premise** of the
01736     group is built before the cleanup is expected.  The original
01737     process is bound to the **same** aggregate and registered with the
01738     **same** table of the launcher through the production adoption -
01739     ``_adopt_broker_process`` - and the channel is built by the
01740     production ``channel_factory`` of that adoption.  The process of the
01741     launch is therefore coordinated by the aggregate that owns it and
01742     not by a stand-in: the release of the aggregate coordinates the
01743     channel **before** the process, the raw files of the process are
01744     never closed under a live channel endpoint and the incomplete start
01745     keeps its owner.
01746 
01747     The **recovery** is the same owner: the reader of the channel really
01748     ends and the second release of the table coordinates the channel
01749     again.  The confirmed close happens exactly once, the process is
01750     never terminated or waited for a second time and the table of the
01751     launcher is empty only when every item of the launch is really
01752     released.
01753     """
01754     order = []
01755     read_release = threading.Event()
01756     # The event of the worker that is really inside its read.  It is
01757     # published **before** the wait of the primitive and it is the
01758     # barrier of the group: a start that returned does not prove that a
01759     # read primitive is still active.
01760     worker_inside = threading.Event()
01761 
01762     class Process:
01763         pid = 4242
01764         returncode = None
01765 
01766         def __init__(self):
01767             self.terminated = 0
01768             self.waited = 0
01769             self.closed = []
01770             self.stdin = type('S', (), {'close': self._close})()
01771             self.stdout = type('S', (), {'close': self._close})()
01772 
01773         def _close(self):
01774             self.closed.append('stream')
01775             order.append('process_stream')
01776 
01777         def terminate(self):
01778             self.terminated += 1
01779             order.append('terminate')
01780 
01781         def wait(self, timeout=None):
01782             self.waited += 1
01783             order.append('wait')
01784             return 0
01785 
01786     class Api:
01787         def duplicate_current_thread_handle(self):
01788             return 701
01789 
01790         def cancel_synchronous_io(self, handle):
01791             return 'REQUESTED'
01792 
01793         def close_handle(self, handle):
01794             return None
01795 
01796         def read_stream(self, handle, size):
01797             # The reader of the chain stays inside its read: the
01798             # channel is partial and its close can never confirm the
01799             # read end while the reader is alive.
01800             # The event is published **before** the wait of the
01801             # primitive: the group waits for it before it injects the
01802             # interruption of the start.
01803             worker_inside.set()
01804             read_release.wait(20.0)
01805             return b''
01806 
01807         def write_stream(self, handle, data):
01808             return len(data)
01809 
01810     class CountingFile:
01811         """A file of the channel that counts its own closes."""
01812 
01813         def __init__(self, name):
01814             self.name = name
01815             self.closes = 0
01816 
01817         def close(self):
01818             self.closes += 1
01819             order.append('channel-' + self.name)
01820             return True
01821 
01822     clock = ManualClock(1000.0)
01823     custody = _LauncherCustody()
01824     aggregate = _LaunchAggregate(custody=custody)
01825     process = Process()
01826     read_file = CountingFile('read')
01827     write_file = CountingFile('write')
01828     original_start = threading.Thread.start
01829     # The ONE context of the round: the bounded join of the reader of
01830     # the channel is bounded by it and the join never waits longer than
01831     # the round allows.
01832     context = _RoundContext(clock=clock)
01833     context.bind_absolute_deadline(clock.value + 0.2)
01834 
01835     def failing_start(self):
01836         # The thread of the reader of the channel really starts and its
01837         # start does not return: the channel is half built and the
01838         # aggregate is its owner.
01839         original_start(self)
01840         if self.name == 'lvw-native-custody-channel':
01841             # RECONCILIATION (N1 correction 13): the interruption is
01842             # injected **after** the worker of the read really entered
01843             # its read primitive.  A start that returned does not prove
01844             # that a read is still active: the reader of the channel
01845             # may end before the cleanup of the launch and it may then
01846             # release the channel correctly.  The group therefore waits
01847             # for the explicit event of the worker before it injects the
01848             # interruption.
01849             assert worker_inside.wait(20.0), \
01850                 'the worker of the read never entered its read primitive'
01851             raise RuntimeError('thread_start_failed_after_start')
01852 
01853     def channel_factory():
01854         """Build the **partial** channel of the launch.
01855 
01856         The channel registers itself with the aggregate of the launch
01857         before it builds anything and the start of its reader thread
01858         does not return: the channel is half built and the aggregate is
01859         its owner.
01860         """
01861         read_endpoint = _WindowsPipeEndpoint(
01862             file_object=read_file, name='channel-read', api=Api(),
01863             clock=clock, handle=900, context=context)
01864         write_endpoint = _WindowsPipeEndpoint(
01865             file_object=write_file, name='channel-write', api=Api(),
01866             clock=clock, handle=901, context=context)
01867         return StreamChannel(None, None, read_endpoint=read_endpoint,
01868                              write_endpoint=write_endpoint,
01869                              custody=aggregate, clock=clock, context=context)
01870 
01871     # CORRECTION 20 (M3): the release of the gate of the reader is
01872     # guarded: a cancellation of it never skips the join that follows it
01873     # and it never replaces the failure that is already unwinding.
01874     launch_cleanup_errors = []
01875     launch_interrupts = []
01876     try:
01877         with unittest.mock.patch.object(threading.Thread, 'start',
01878                                         failing_start):
01879             with pytest.raises(RuntimeError):
01880                 # The production adoption binds the **original process**
01881                 # to the aggregate and registers it with the table of
01882                 # the launcher **before** the channel of the launch is
01883                 # built.
01884                 _adopt_broker_process(process, channel_factory=channel_factory,
01885                                       executable='C:/python.exe',
01886                                       executable_sha256='a' * 64,
01887                                       aggregate=aggregate, custody=custody)
01888         # The partial channel is found **through the aggregate** and not
01889         # through a local variable of the adoption.
01890         channel = aggregate.channel
01891         assert channel is not None, \
01892             'the partial channel lost its owner'
01893         facts = {'aggregate_channel': channel is not None,
01894                  'read_closes': read_file.closes,
01895                  'write_closes': write_file.closes,
01896                  'process_closed': list(process.closed),
01897                  'terminated': process.terminated,
01898                  'waited': process.waited,
01899                  'order': list(order),
01900                  'custody': [(item['kind'], repr(item['resource']))
01901                              for item in custody.items()]}
01902         write_phase('r04-partial', facts)
01903         # The channel of the launch was coordinated **before** the
01904         # process: the files of the process belong to the channel.
01905         assert order and order[0] == 'channel-write', \
01906             'the channel of the launch was not coordinated before the process'
01907         assert order == ['channel-write', 'terminate', 'wait'], \
01908             'the release of the launch did not follow the dependency order'
01909         # The process of the launch was coordinated by the aggregate that
01910         # owns it: it was terminated and it was waited for.
01911         assert process.terminated == 1
01912         assert process.waited == 1
01913         # The files of the process were not closed by the process
01914         # helper: the channel owns them.
01915         assert process.closed == [], \
01916             'the process helper closed the files of the channel'
01917         # The read end of the channel is never closed under a live
01918         # reader: the incomplete start keeps its owner.
01919         assert read_file.closes == 0, \
01920             'the read end was closed under a live reader'
01921         assert write_file.closes == 1
01922         assert aggregate.channel is channel, \
01923             'the incomplete start lost its owner'
01924         assert any(item['kind'] == 'channel' for item in custody.items()), \
01925             'the incomplete channel lost its owner in the table'
01926 
01927         # The **same** owner coordinates the launch again: the reader of
01928         # the channel really ends and the second release of the table
01929         # closes the read end exactly once.
01930         read_release.set()
01931         reader_thread = getattr(channel, '_thread', None)
01932         if reader_thread is not None:
01933             reader_thread.join(10.0)
01934             assert not reader_thread.is_alive(), \
01935                 'the reader thread of the channel was left behind'
01936         report = custody.release()
01937         facts = {'report': report,
01938                  'read_closes': read_file.closes,
01939                  'write_closes': write_file.closes,
01940                  'process_closed': list(process.closed),
01941                  'terminated': process.terminated,
01942                  'waited': process.waited,
01943                  'order': list(order),
01944                  'custody': [(item['kind'], repr(item['resource']))
01945                              for item in custody.items()]}
01946         write_phase('r04-release', facts)
01947         assert 'channel' in (report.get('released') or []), \
01948             'the same owner did not coordinate the channel again'
01949         assert read_file.closes == 1, \
01950             'the read end of the channel was not closed by its owner'
01951         assert write_file.closes == 1, \
01952             'the write end of the channel was closed a second time'
01953         # The process was never terminated or waited for a second time.
01954         assert process.terminated == 1, \
01955             'the recovery terminated the process of the launch a second time'
01956         assert process.waited == 1, \
01957             'the recovery waited for the process of the launch a second time'
01958         assert process.closed == []
01959         # The table of the launcher is empty only when every item of the
01960         # launch is really released.
01961         assert custody.items() == [], \
01962             'the table of the launcher kept an item of a released launch'
01963     finally:
01964         # The salvage of the owner of the test: the thread that the
01965         # channel really started is joined with a bounded wait.
01966         #
01967         # CORRECTION 20 (M3): the release of the gate is guarded and the
01968         # exact object of a cancellation of it is retained.
01969         _release_gate(read_release.set, launch_cleanup_errors,
01970                       launch_interrupts)
01971         partial = aggregate.channel
01972         thread = getattr(partial, '_thread', None)
01973         if thread is not None:
01974             thread.join(10.0)
01975             # the **exact** cancellation wins over the ordinary assertion
01976             # of the join that follows it.
01977             _raise_first_interruption(launch_interrupts)
01978             assert not thread.is_alive(), \
01979                 'the reader thread of the channel was left behind'

01982 # -- N10-R05: an incomplete channel keeps its owner ------------------------
01983 
01984 def test_n10_r05_an_incomplete_channel_keeps_its_owner_and_the_cancel(tmp_path):
01985     """N10-R05 (M07): a close that returned is not a release.
01986 
01987     Obligation: the close of the channel of a launch is a release only
01988     when **every** fact of it is closed - the reader, the two ends, the
01989     workers of the endpoints and the original handles of their threads.
01990     A close that returned incomplete facts keeps the channel with its
01991     original reference and its owner, and the same launch coordinates
01992     the channel **before** the process.  The safe steps of the release
01993     run before the original cancellation of the launch propagates: the
01994     original object is the one that leaves, the cancellation of the
01995     recovery stays reachable next to it and the recovery of the launcher
01996     cancels in the same way.
01997 
01998     Entry: ``_LaunchAggregate.release``, ``_adopt_broker_process`` and
01999     ``_LauncherCustody.release``.  Trigger: the close of the channel
02000     returns without a confirmed end and it is cancelled by the
02001     recovery.  Oracle: the process was terminated and waited **after**
02002     the channel was coordinated, the channel kept its owner, the
02003     original failure object is the one that propagated and the
02004     cancellation of the recovery is reachable next to it.  Negative: a
02005     returned close is never taken for a release.  Returned state: the
02006     facts of the aggregate name what it kept.  Salvage: the recovery of
02007     the launcher releases what it may.
02008 
02009     CORRECTION 12 (M07-A): the retry of an unresolved **original raw
02010     stream** of a launch is cancelled: the original cancellation is the
02011     error that leaves and the stream keeps its owner.  A cancelled
02012     retry never forgets a stream and it never replaces the failure that
02013     it could not confirm.
02014     """
02015     order = []
02016 
02017     class Process:
02018         pid = 4242
02019         returncode = None
02020 
02021         def __init__(self):
02022             self.terminated = 0
02023             self.waited = 0
02024 
02025         def terminate(self):
02026             self.terminated += 1
02027             order.append('terminate')
02028 
02029         def wait(self, timeout=None):
02030             self.waited += 1
02031             order.append('wait')
02032             return 0
02033 
02034     class CancellingChannel:
02035         """A channel whose close returns without a confirmed end and
02036         whose close is cancelled by the recovery."""
02037 
02038         reader_finished = True
02039 
02040         def __init__(self):
02041             self.closes = 0
02042 
02043         def close(self):
02044             self.closes += 1
02045             order.append('channel')
02046             raise KeyboardInterrupt('channel_close_cancelled')
02047 
02048     class FailingBrokerProcess:
02049         def __init__(self, *args, **kwargs):
02050             raise RuntimeError('broker_process_construction_failed')
02051 
02052     process = Process()
02053     channel = CancellingChannel()
02054     custody = _LauncherCustody()
02055     aggregate = _LaunchAggregate(custody=custody)
02056     original = broker_module.BrokerProcess
02057     broker_module.BrokerProcess = FailingBrokerProcess
02058     try:
02059         with pytest.raises(RuntimeError) as info:
02060             _adopt_broker_process(process, channel_factory=lambda: channel,
02061                                   executable='C:/python.exe',
02062                                   executable_sha256='a' * 64,
02063                                   aggregate=aggregate, custody=custody)
02064     finally:
02065         broker_module.BrokerProcess = original
02066     facts = {'channel_closes': channel.closes,
02067              'terminated': process.terminated,
02068              'waited': process.waited,
02069              'order': list(order),
02070              'aggregate_channel': aggregate.channel is channel,
02071              'custody': [(item['kind'], repr(item['resource']))
02072                          for item in custody.items()],
02073              'error_type': type(info.value).__name__,
02074              'error_detail': getattr(info.value, 'detail', None)}
02075     write_phase('r05-adoption', facts)
02076     # The original failure object is the one that propagated.
02077     assert str(info.value) == 'broker_process_construction_failed'
02078     # The safe steps of the release ran before the failure left: the
02079     # channel was coordinated **before** the process.
02080     assert channel.closes == 1
02081     assert order == ['channel', 'terminate', 'wait'], \
02082         'the channel of the launch was not coordinated before the process'
02083     assert process.terminated == 1
02084     assert process.waited == 1
02085     # The cancellation of the recovery is reachable next to the original
02086     # failure: the primary failure and the cancellation coexist.
02087     cancellation = getattr(info.value, 'lvw_cancelled', None)
02088     assert isinstance(cancellation, KeyboardInterrupt), \
02089         'the cancellation of the recovery was swallowed'
02090     assert str(cancellation) == 'channel_close_cancelled'
02091     # The incomplete facts kept the channel with its owner.
02092     assert aggregate.channel is channel, \
02093         'an incomplete channel lost its owner'
02094     assert any(item['kind'] == 'channel' for item in custody.items())
02095 
02096     # The recovery of the launcher cancels in the same way: the safe
02097     # steps run first and the cancellation of the recovery is the error
02098     # that leaves.  The recovery runs on a **fresh** launch and on a
02099     # fresh table of the launcher, so that the order that is observed
02100     # belongs to that launch alone.
02101     order[:] = []
02102     process2 = Process()
02103     channel2 = CancellingChannel()
02104     custody2 = _LauncherCustody()
02105 
02106     class FailingBrokerProcess2:
02107         def __init__(self, *args, **kwargs):
02108             raise RuntimeError('broker_process_construction_failed')
02109 
02110     original = broker_module.BrokerProcess
02111     broker_module.BrokerProcess = FailingBrokerProcess2
02112     try:
02113         with pytest.raises(RuntimeError):
02114             _adopt_broker_process(process2, channel_factory=lambda: channel2,
02115                                   executable='C:/python.exe',
02116                                   executable_sha256='a' * 64,
02117                                   custody=custody2)
02118     finally:
02119         broker_module.BrokerProcess = original
02120     order[:] = []
02121     with pytest.raises(KeyboardInterrupt) as recovery:
02122         custody2.release()
02123     facts = {'order': list(order),
02124              'terminated': process2.terminated,
02125              'waited': process2.waited,
02126              'error_type': type(recovery.value).__name__,
02127              'custody': [(item['kind'], repr(item['resource']))
02128                          for item in custody2.items()]}
02129     write_phase('r05-recovery', facts)
02130     assert str(recovery.value) == 'channel_close_cancelled'
02131     # RECONCILIATION (N1 correction 11): the process of this launch was
02132     # **already** released by the adoption phase: its exit was really
02133     # confirmed and its original handle was released with it.  The
02134     # recovery of the launcher therefore coordinates the channel only
02135     # and it never repeats the termination and the wait of a process
02136     # that was already proved released.  The old expectation that the
02137     # recovery repeats them contradicted the ownership of the process
02138     # and it is replaced by the boundary that really holds.
02139     assert order == ['channel'], \
02140         'the recovery of the launcher did not coordinate the channel only'
02141     assert process2.terminated == 1, \
02142         'the recovery terminated the process of the launch a second time'
02143     assert process2.waited == 1, \
02144         'the recovery waited for the process of the launch a second time'
02145     # The channel that could not be released keeps its owner.
02146     assert any(item['kind'] == 'channel' for item in custody2.items())
02147 
02148     # -- PHASE THREE: a launch whose **original handle** was still
02149     #    retained is coordinated **again** by the recovery of the
02150     #    launcher.  The recovery retries the incomplete parts and it
02151     #    never opens a budget of its own.
02152     order[:] = []
02153 
02154     class RetainedProcess:
02155         """A process whose first wait failed: it keeps its owner."""
02156 
02157         pid = 4243
02158         returncode = None
02159 
02160         def __init__(self):
02161             self.terminated = 0
02162             self.waited = 0
02163 
02164         def terminate(self):
02165             self.terminated += 1
02166             order.append('terminate')
02167 
02168         def wait(self, timeout=None):
02169             self.waited += 1
02170             order.append('wait')
02171             if self.waited == 1:
02172                 raise RuntimeError('wait_failed')
02173             return 0
02174 
02175     class FailingBrokerProcess3:
02176         def __init__(self, *args, **kwargs):
02177             raise RuntimeError('broker_process_construction_failed')
02178 
02179     process3 = RetainedProcess()
02180     channel3 = CancellingChannel()
02181     custody3 = _LauncherCustody()
02182 
02183     original = broker_module.BrokerProcess
02184     broker_module.BrokerProcess = FailingBrokerProcess3
02185     try:
02186         with pytest.raises(RuntimeError):
02187             _adopt_broker_process(process3, channel_factory=lambda: channel3,
02188                                   executable='C:/python.exe',
02189                                   executable_sha256='a' * 64,
02190                                   custody=custody3)
02191     finally:
02192         broker_module.BrokerProcess = original
02193     # The adoption could not release the process: it keeps its owner.
02194     assert process3.waited == 1
02195     kept = [item for item in custody3.items() if item['kind'] == 'process']
02196     assert kept, 'the process that could not be released lost its owner'
02197     order[:] = []
02198     with pytest.raises(KeyboardInterrupt):
02199         custody3.release()
02200     facts = {'order': list(order),
02201              'terminated': process3.terminated,
02202              'waited': process3.waited,
02203              'custody': [(item['kind'], repr(item['resource']))
02204                          for item in custody3.items()]}
02205     write_phase('r05-retained-recovery', facts)
02206     # The channel is coordinated first and the **retained** process is
02207     # coordinated again: the recovery really retries the incomplete
02208     # part and it never releases the channel alone.
02209     assert order == ['channel', 'terminate', 'wait'], \
02210         'the recovery did not coordinate the retained process again'
02211     assert process3.terminated == 2
02212     assert process3.waited == 2
02213 
02214     # -- N1 correction 12 (M07-A): a **cancelled** retry of an
02215     #    unresolved original raw stream keeps the owner of the stream
02216     #    and the original cancellation is the error that leaves.
02217     #    RECONCILIATION (N1 correction 13): the phase runs on a
02218     #    **scoped** table of the launcher.  The table of the launcher
02219     #    of this package is shared by the whole batch and the context
02220     #    that another check of the batch bound to it is **not** the
02221     #    context of this launch: the bound of another round would
02222     #    refuse the retry of the raw stream before it starts and the
02223     #    cancellation would never be observed at all.  The production
02224     #    table is used and it is never emptied by hand.
02225     scoped = _LauncherCustody()
02226     with unittest.mock.patch.object(broker_module, '_LAUNCHER_CUSTODY',
02227                                     scoped):
02228         holder = m07a_interrupted_launch(tmp_path, sealed_plan(),
02229                                          failures=1, cancel=True)
02230         stdout = holder['stdout']
02231         aggregate = holder['aggregate'][0] if holder['aggregate'] else None
02232         assert aggregate is not None, 'the interrupted launch lost its owner'
02233         release_retained_launches()
02234         assert stdout.attempts == 1, \
02235             'the raw stream was not closed by the first release'
02236         assert stdout.confirmed == 0
02237         with pytest.raises(KeyboardInterrupt):
02238             release_retained_launches()
02239         assert stdout.attempts == 2, \
02240             'the same owner did not retry the raw stream of the launch'
02241         assert stdout.confirmed == 0, \
02242             'the cancelled retry of the raw stream was read as a close'
02243         owned = [item for item in _launcher_custody_items()
02244                  if item['kind'] == 'stdout' and item['resource'] is stdout]
02245         assert owned, 'the cancelled retry forgot the owner of the raw stream'
02246         assert [item['name'] for item in aggregate.raw_streams] == ['stdout'], \
02247             'the launch forgot a raw stream that was never closed'
02248         # The **final** recovery of the **same** owner after the test
02249         # released its fault seam: the stream is really closed, the
02250         # owner of it is released and the table of the launcher is
02251         # empty again.  The recovery is the same owner and it never
02252         # opens a budget of its own.
02253         stdout.cancel = False
02254         release_retained_launches()
02255         facts = {'attempts': stdout.attempts, 'confirmed': stdout.confirmed,
02256                  'owned': len([item for item in _launcher_custody_items()
02257                                if item['kind'] == 'stdout'
02258                                and item['resource'] is stdout]),
02259                  'raw_streams': [item['name']
02260                                  for item in aggregate.raw_streams],
02261                  'table': [(item['kind'], repr(item['resource']))
02262                            for item in _launcher_custody_items()]}
02263         write_phase('r05-final-recovery', facts)
02264         assert stdout.confirmed == 1, \
02265             'the final recovery did not close the raw stream of the launch'
02266         assert stdout.closed is True
02267         assert not [item for item in _launcher_custody_items()
02268                     if item['kind'] == 'stdout'
02269                     and item['resource'] is stdout], \
02270             'the table of the launcher kept the closed raw stream'
02271         assert aggregate.raw_streams == [], \
02272             'the launch still owns a raw stream that was really closed'
02273 
02274     # -- N1 correction 13 (M07-A.3): the **current** minimum of the
02275     #    original round is read again before **each** initial raw close.
02276     #    A context that was tightened by the wait of the process or by a
02277     #    previous close of an original refuses the next close: the
02278     #    original keeps its owner and its identity, no new close is
02279     #    started and no fresh cleanup lease is opened.
02280     class BoundStream:
02281         """One original raw file that counts its own closes.
02282 
02283         The file carries two named seams: an **ordinary** failure that
02284         happens once and a callback that the group uses to tighten the
02285         original round between two closes.  The ordinary failure is the
02286         fault seam of the group: it is released by its own first
02287         attempt and the second attempt of the **same** owner really
02288         closes the original.
02289         """
02290 
02291         def __init__(self, name, on_close=None, failure=None):
02292             self.name = name
02293             self.attempts = 0
02294             self.closed = False
02295             self.on_close = on_close
02296             self._failure = failure
02297             # The **original** fault object of the seam: it is kept so
02298             # that the identity of the failure is a fact and not a
02299             # label.
02300             self.fault = None
02301 
02302         def close(self):
02303             self.attempts += 1
02304             if self._failure is not None:
02305                 error = self._failure
02306                 self._failure = None
02307                 self.fault = error
02308                 raise error
02309             if self.on_close is not None:
02310                 callback = self.on_close
02311                 self.on_close = None
02312                 callback()
02313             self.closed = True
02314             return True
02315 
02316     class BoundProcess:
02317         """A process whose wait may tighten the original round."""
02318 
02319         pid = 4246
02320         returncode = 0
02321 
02322         def __init__(self, *, stdin, stdout, on_wait=None):
02323             self.stdin = stdin
02324             self.stdout = stdout
02325             self.terminated = 0
02326             self.waited = 0
02327             self.on_wait = on_wait
02328 
02329         def terminate(self):
02330             self.terminated += 1
02331 
02332         def wait(self, timeout=None):
02333             self.waited += 1
02334             if self.on_wait is not None:
02335                 callback = self.on_wait
02336                 self.on_wait = None
02337                 callback()
02338             return 0
02339 
02340     def bound_launch(context, process):
02341         """One no-channel launch that the owner of the table coordinates."""
02342         custody = _LauncherCustody()
02343         aggregate = _LaunchAggregate(custody=custody, context=context)
02344         aggregate.bind_process(process)
02345         custody.retain('aggregate', aggregate, 'launch')
02346         custody.retain('process', process, 'popened', launch=aggregate)
02347         return custody, aggregate
02348 
02349     # -- the case one: the round is tightened **during the wait** of the
02350     #    process.  The initial raw closes are refused and the originals
02351     #    keep their identity.
02352     clock_one = ManualClock(1000.0)
02353     context_one = _RoundContext(clock=clock_one)
02354     context_one.bind_absolute_deadline(clock_one.value + 10.0)
02355     stdin_one = BoundStream('stdin')
02356     stdout_one = BoundStream('stdout')
02357     process_one = BoundProcess(
02358         stdin=stdin_one, stdout=stdout_one,
02359         on_wait=lambda: context_one.bind_absolute_deadline(clock_one.value - 1.0))
02360     custody_one, aggregate_one = bound_launch(context_one, process_one)
02361     report_one = custody_one.release()
02362     facts = {'report': report_one,
02363              'stdin_attempts': stdin_one.attempts,
02364              'stdout_attempts': stdout_one.attempts,
02365              'raw_streams': [item['name']
02366                              for item in aggregate_one.raw_streams],
02367              'owned': [(item['kind'], repr(item['resource']))
02368                        for item in custody_one.items()],
02369              'terminated': process_one.terminated,
02370              'waited': process_one.waited}
02371     write_phase('r05-bound-during-wait', facts)
02372     assert stdin_one.attempts == 0, \
02373         'the input was closed although the round had expired'
02374     assert stdout_one.attempts == 0, \
02375         'the output was closed although the round had expired'
02376     assert [item['name'] for item in aggregate_one.raw_streams] == \
02377         ['stdin', 'stdout'], \
02378         'the launch lost an original that it may not close any more'
02379     owned_one = [item for item in custody_one.items()
02380                  if item['kind'] in ('stdin', 'stdout')]
02381     assert len(owned_one) == 2, \
02382         'the table of the launcher lost the identity of an original'
02383 
02384     # -- the case two: the round is tightened **between** two initial
02385     #    raw closes.  The close that follows the tightening is refused.
02386     clock_two = ManualClock(1000.0)
02387     context_two = _RoundContext(clock=clock_two)
02388     context_two.bind_absolute_deadline(clock_two.value + 10.0)
02389     stdin_two = BoundStream(
02390         'stdin',
02391         on_close=lambda: context_two.bind_absolute_deadline(
02392             clock_two.value - 1.0))
02393     stdout_two = BoundStream('stdout')
02394     process_two = BoundProcess(stdin=stdin_two, stdout=stdout_two)
02395     custody_two, aggregate_two = bound_launch(context_two, process_two)
02396     report_two = custody_two.release()
02397     facts = {'report': report_two,
02398              'stdin_attempts': stdin_two.attempts,
02399              'stdout_attempts': stdout_two.attempts,
02400              'raw_streams': [item['name']
02401                              for item in aggregate_two.raw_streams],
02402              'owned': [(item['kind'], repr(item['resource']))
02403                        for item in custody_two.items()]}
02404     write_phase('r05-bound-between-closes', facts)
02405     assert stdin_two.attempts == 1, \
02406         'the input was not closed although the round was still positive'
02407     assert stdin_two.closed is True
02408     assert stdout_two.attempts == 0, \
02409         'the output was closed although the round had expired'
02410     assert [item['name'] for item in aggregate_two.raw_streams] == ['stdout'], \
02411         'the launch owns an original whose close was confirmed'
02412 
02413     # -- the case three: the positive case.  Every initial close of a
02414     #    launch with a positive remaining is really attempted.
02415     clock_three = ManualClock(1000.0)
02416     context_three = _RoundContext(clock=clock_three)
02417     context_three.bind_absolute_deadline(clock_three.value + 10.0)
02418     stdin_three = BoundStream('stdin')
02419     stdout_three = BoundStream('stdout')
02420     process_three = BoundProcess(stdin=stdin_three, stdout=stdout_three)
02421     custody_three, aggregate_three = bound_launch(context_three, process_three)
02422     report_three = custody_three.release()
02423     facts = {'report': report_three,
02424              'stdin_attempts': stdin_three.attempts,
02425              'stdout_attempts': stdout_three.attempts,
02426              'raw_streams': [item['name']
02427                              for item in aggregate_three.raw_streams]}
02428     write_phase('r05-bound-positive', facts)
02429     assert stdin_three.attempts == 1
02430     assert stdout_three.attempts == 1
02431     assert aggregate_three.raw_streams == [], \
02432         'the launch owns an original whose close was confirmed'
02433 
02434     # -- the round of the first case is **expired** and the deadline of
02435     #    a round is **one way**: it may only be shortened.  A repeated
02436     #    release of the **same** owner therefore starts **no** new close,
02437     #    the exact originals keep their owner and the current bound of
02438     #    the round is the expired one.  The group never renews a lease,
02439     #    it never replaces the context of the round and it never rewinds
02440     #    a clock.
02441     repeated_one = custody_one.release()
02442     facts = {'repeated': repeated_one,
02443              'stdin_attempts': stdin_one.attempts,
02444              'stdout_attempts': stdout_one.attempts,
02445              'stdin_closed': stdin_one.closed,
02446              'stdout_closed': stdout_one.closed,
02447              'raw_streams': [item['name']
02448                              for item in aggregate_one.raw_streams],
02449              'owned': [(item['kind'], repr(item['resource']))
02450                        for item in custody_one.items()],
02451              'absolute_deadline': context_one.absolute_deadline}
02452     write_phase('r05-bound-expired-repeat', facts)
02453     assert stdin_one.attempts == 0, \
02454         'the expired round started a new close of the input'
02455     assert stdout_one.attempts == 0, \
02456         'the expired round started a new close of the output'
02457     assert stdin_one.closed is False
02458     assert stdout_one.closed is False
02459     assert [item['name'] for item in aggregate_one.raw_streams] == \
02460         ['stdin', 'stdout'], \
02461         'the expired round lost the identity of an original'
02462     assert context_one.absolute_deadline == clock_one.value - 1.0, \
02463         'the expired round renewed its own deadline'
02464 
02465     # -- the successful unresolved-only retry: it happens in a
02466     #    **separate** original round that is still **positive** and it
02467     #    uses the **same** owner, the **same** original context and the
02468     #    **same** original deadline for both attempts.  The ordinary
02469     #    failure of the close is the fault seam of the group: the first
02470     #    attempt retains the original with its identity and the second
02471     #    attempt of the same owner really closes it.
02472     clock_four = ManualClock(1000.0)
02473     context_four = _RoundContext(clock=clock_four)
02474     context_four.bind_absolute_deadline(clock_four.value + 10.0)
02475     stdin_four = BoundStream('stdin')
02476     stdout_four = BoundStream(
02477         'stdout', failure=RuntimeError('stdout_close_failed'))
02478     process_four = BoundProcess(stdin=stdin_four, stdout=stdout_four)
02479     custody_four, aggregate_four = bound_launch(context_four, process_four)
02480     report_four = custody_four.release()
02481     facts = {'report': report_four,
02482              'stdin_attempts': stdin_four.attempts,
02483              'stdout_attempts': stdout_four.attempts,
02484              'stdout_closed': stdout_four.closed,
02485              'raw_streams': [item['name']
02486                              for item in aggregate_four.raw_streams],
02487              'owned': [(item['kind'], repr(item['resource']))
02488                        for item in custody_four.items()],
02489              'absolute_deadline': context_four.absolute_deadline}
02490     write_phase('r05-owned-retry', facts)
02491     assert stdout_four.attempts == 1, \
02492         'the output of the launch was not closed by the first release'
02493     assert stdout_four.closed is False, \
02494         'the failed close of the output was read as a close'
02495     # The **original** fault object of the seam is the failure that the
02496     # product names and the identity of it is kept.
02497     assert stdout_four.fault is not None, \
02498         'the ordinary failure of the close never happened'
02499     assert aggregate_four.facts.get('failures', {}).get('stdout') \
02500         == 'RuntimeError', \
02501         'the ordinary failure of the close was not named by the product'
02502     assert [item['name'] for item in aggregate_four.raw_streams] == \
02503         ['stdout'], \
02504         'the launch lost the unresolved original of its round'
02505     owned_four = [item for item in custody_four.items()
02506                   if item['kind'] == 'stdout']
02507     assert owned_four, \
02508         'the unresolved original of the round lost its owner'
02509     # The fault seam of the group is released by its own first attempt:
02510     # the **same** owner retries the unresolved original under the
02511     # **same** original context and the **same** original deadline.
02512     recovered_four = custody_four.release()
02513     facts = {'recovered': recovered_four,
02514              'stdin_attempts': stdin_four.attempts,
02515              'stdout_attempts': stdout_four.attempts,
02516              'stdout_closed': stdout_four.closed,
02517              'raw_streams': [item['name']
02518                              for item in aggregate_four.raw_streams],
02519              'owned': [(item['kind'], repr(item['resource']))
02520                        for item in custody_four.items()],
02521              'absolute_deadline': context_four.absolute_deadline}
02522     write_phase('r05-owned-retry-recovery', facts)
02523     assert stdout_four.attempts == 2, \
02524         'the same owner did not retry the unresolved original'
02525     assert stdout_four.closed is True, \
02526         'the same owner did not really close the unresolved original'
02527     assert aggregate_four.raw_streams == [], \
02528         'the launch still owns an original that was really closed'
02529     assert custody_four.items() == [], \
02530         'the table of the launcher kept an item of the launch'
02531     # The deadline of the round was never renewed and the context of
02532     # the round is the one of the first attempt.
02533     assert context_four.absolute_deadline == clock_four.value + 10.0, \
02534         'the round renewed its own deadline'

02537 # -- N10-R06: the admission of a generation -------------------------------
02538 
02539 def test_n10_r06_an_expired_or_cancelled_call_performs_no_primitive_call():
02540     """N10-R06 (M08): the admission comes before the side effect.
02541 
02542     Obligation: a read or a write of an endpoint that enters when the
02543     round is already expired or already cancelled is refused **before**
02544     the worker of the endpoint is started, before the request is
02545     submitted and before the primitive is called: the number of the
02546     real primitive calls of such a call is zero.  The check and the
02547     admission of the generation are one atomic step and a cancellation
02548     is never a remedy that is applied after the submission.
02549 
02550     Entry: ``_WindowsPipeEndpoint.read``,
02551     ``_WindowsPipeEndpoint.write`` and
02552     ``_WindowsPipeEndpoint._admission``.  Trigger: the call enters with
02553     an expired deadline and with a cancel flag that is already set, for
02554     the read and for the write.  Oracle: the refusal is named and no
02555     worker was started.  Negative: the endpoint never starts a worker
02556     for a refused call.  Returned state: the facts of the endpoint name
02557     no worker.  Salvage: the owner of the test closes the endpoint at
02558     the end.
02559     """
02560     clock = ManualClock(1000.0)
02561     calls = []
02562 
02563     class Api:
02564         def duplicate_current_thread_handle(self):
02565             return 701
02566 
02567         def cancel_synchronous_io(self, handle):
02568             calls.append('cancel')
02569             return 'REQUESTED'
02570 
02571         def close_handle(self, handle):
02572             calls.append('close_handle')
02573             return None
02574 
02575         def read_stream(self, handle, size):
02576             calls.append('read')
02577             return b''
02578 
02579         def write_stream(self, handle, data):
02580             calls.append('write')
02581             return len(data)
02582 
02583     endpoint = _WindowsPipeEndpoint(file_object=_FakeFile('read'),
02584                                     name='inprocess', api=Api(), clock=clock,
02585                                     handle=702)
02586     try:
02587         with pytest.raises(BrokerError) as info:
02588             endpoint.read(clock.value - 1.0, None)
02589         assert info.value.code == 'BROKER_PIPE_TIMEOUT'
02590         assert calls == [], 'a primitive was called by a refused read'
02591         assert endpoint._reader is None, \
02592             'the refused read started a worker of the endpoint'
02593         assert endpoint.facts.get('retained_workers', 0) == 0
02594 
02595         cancel = _ChannelCancel()
02596         cancel.set()
02597         with pytest.raises(BrokerError) as info:
02598             endpoint.read(None, cancel)
02599         assert info.value.code == 'BROKER_PIPE_CANCELLED'
02600         assert calls == [], 'a primitive was called by a refused read'
02601         assert endpoint._reader is None, \
02602             'the refused read started a worker of the endpoint'
02603 
02604         with pytest.raises(BrokerError) as info:
02605             endpoint.write(b'abc', clock.value - 1.0, None)
02606         assert info.value.code == 'BROKER_PIPE_TIMEOUT'
02607         assert calls == [], 'a primitive was called by a refused write'
02608         assert endpoint._writer is None, \
02609             'the refused write started a worker of the endpoint'
02610 
02611         with pytest.raises(BrokerError) as info:
02612             endpoint.write(b'abc', None, cancel)
02613         assert info.value.code == 'BROKER_PIPE_CANCELLED'
02614         assert calls == [], 'a primitive was called by a refused write'
02615         assert endpoint._writer is None, \
02616             'the refused write started a worker of the endpoint'
02617         write_phase('r06-admission', {'calls': list(calls),
02618                                       'reader': endpoint._reader is not None,
02619                                       'writer': endpoint._writer is not None})
02620 
02621         # -- the linearization point of the admission (N1 correction
02622         #    11, M08): the request is admitted **before** the worker of
02623         #    the endpoint is started and it is admitted **again** after
02624         #    the worker is ready and immediately before the request is
02625         #    submitted.  A cancellation or an expiry that is published
02626         #    **before** that final admission causes **zero** primitive
02627         #    calls: the request never reaches the worker.
02628         endpoint2 = _WindowsPipeEndpoint(file_object=_FakeFile('read2'),
02629                                          name='inprocess-2', api=Api(),
02630                                          clock=clock, handle=703)
02631         try:
02632             cancel2 = _ChannelCancel()
02633             # The initial admission of the generation succeeds: the
02634             # round is neither expired nor cancelled.
02635             endpoint2._admission(None, None)
02636             worker = endpoint2._start('read', endpoint2._read_block, None)
02637             assert worker.ready.is_set(), 'the worker was never ready'
02638             # The cancellation is published after the readiness of the
02639             # worker and before the dispatch of the request.
02640             cancel2.set()
02641             with pytest.raises(BrokerError) as info:
02642                 endpoint2._await(worker, None, None, cancel2)
02643             assert info.value.code == 'BROKER_PIPE_CANCELLED'
02644             assert calls == [], \
02645                 'a primitive was called by a request that was not admitted'
02646             assert worker._submitted == 0, \
02647                 'the request was submitted although the admission was refused'
02648             write_phase('r06-linearization', {
02649                 'calls': list(calls),
02650                 'submitted': worker._submitted,
02651                 'code': info.value.code})
02652         finally:
02653             endpoint2.close()
02654 
02655         # The same boundary with an **expiry**: the deadline of the
02656         # round arrives after the readiness of the worker and before the
02657         # dispatch of the request.
02658         endpoint3 = _WindowsPipeEndpoint(file_object=_FakeFile('read3'),
02659                                          name='inprocess-3', api=Api(),
02660                                          clock=clock, handle=704)
02661         try:
02662             before = list(calls)
02663             endpoint3._admission(None, None)
02664             worker3 = endpoint3._start('read', endpoint3._read_block, None)
02665             assert worker3.ready.is_set(), 'the worker was never ready'
02666             # The deadline of the round arrives before the dispatch.
02667             clock.value += 1000.0
02668             with pytest.raises(BrokerError) as info:
02669                 endpoint3._await(worker3, None, clock.value - 1.0, None)
02670             assert info.value.code == 'BROKER_PIPE_TIMEOUT'
02671             assert calls == before, \
02672                 'a primitive was called by a request that was not admitted'
02673             assert worker3._submitted == 0, \
02674                 'the request was submitted although the admission was refused'
02675             write_phase('r06-expiry', {
02676                 'calls': list(calls),
02677                 'submitted': worker3._submitted,
02678                 'code': info.value.code})
02679         finally:
02680             endpoint3.close()
02681     finally:
02682         endpoint.close()
02683 
02684 
02685 # -- N10-R07: the context reaches every layer -----------------------------
02686 
02687 def test_n10_r07_the_context_of_the_round_reaches_every_layer(tmp_path):
02688     """N10-R07 (M08): the context of the round is one context.
02689 
02690     Obligation: the parent hands the **one** context of the round to
02691     the launcher of the broker and the launcher holds it **before** the
02692     first side effect of the launch - the creation of the process, the
02693     reading of the executable, the channel and its endpoints.  The
02694     channel, the endpoints, the workers of the endpoints and the API of
02695     the primitives read the **same** context.  In the child the STOP of
02696     the round publishes the ONE cleanup deadline and the transport of
02697     the round - the write of the terminal reply, the partial write and
02698     the join of the reader - uses the earlier of the lease and the
02699     cleanup: there is no unbound window and no new budget of its own.
02700 
02701     The group drives the **production** launcher of the round: the
02702     default launcher of the session calls the production ``launch_broker``
02703     and the only seam that is replaced is the creation of the process:
02704     an in-process fake of ``subprocess.Popen`` that never calls the
02705     original and that never starts an operating system process.  The
02706     executable that is hashed is a **bound synthetic fixture** of the
02707     round and the endpoints of the channel are the production Windows
02708     endpoints with the synthetic primitives of the round.
02709 
02710     Entry: ``NativeCustodySession._call_launcher``,
02711     ``NativeCustodySession.start``, ``_default_launcher``,
02712     ``launch_broker``, ``_LaunchAggregate``, ``StreamChannel``,
02713     ``_WindowsPipeEndpoint``, ``_WindowsPipeWorker``,
02714     ``WindowsApi.bind_context``, ``BrokerCore.__init__`` and
02715     ``BrokerLoop._stop``.  Trigger: the round publishes its cleanup
02716     deadline and the terminal reply is written.  Oracle: the context of
02717     the session was already the context of the round when the process
02718     was created, when the executable was hashed and when the channel and
02719     its endpoints were built; every layer of the child reads the
02720     published cleanup deadline.  Negative: no layer keeps a deadline of
02721     its own.  Returned state: the evidence of the session names the
02722     propagation of the context.  Salvage: the owner of the test closes
02723     the round in every case.
02724 
02725     RECONCILIATION (N1 correction 14, F3): the group drove the parent
02726     of the round with the clock of the session while the channel of the
02727     round and its two endpoints kept the **default** clock of the host
02728     (``time.monotonic``).  A deadline of the round was then compared
02729     with a clock of **another** domain: the admission of the read of
02730     the endpoint refused the round, the reader of the channel failed,
02731     the pump of the parent ended at the end of the stream and the
02732     group observed **no** admission at all (the phase document of the
02733     failed batch of the correction 13 recorded two empty lists).
02734 
02735     The group now builds the channel of the round with the clock of
02736     the round - the production class is used unchanged and only its
02737     supported clock argument is bound - so that the channel, its two
02738     endpoints, their workers, the pump of the parent, the context of
02739     the round and the aggregate of the launch read every deadline of
02740     the round in ONE domain.  The group then proves the **liveness** of
02741     the pump of the parent before it tightens the bound (the next real
02742     read of the broker is held at a transparent seam), publishes the
02743     **original** cleanup deadline of the round and releases the
02744     admission: the original queue method really executes and the
02745     recorded argument is the argument that the production really passed
02746     to it.  The reply of the parent is bounded by the **published**
02747     cleanup deadline of the original context and not by the timeout of
02748     the call.  The positive admission and the zero expiry stay two
02749     distinct real owner schedules with the identity of the original
02750     context, the production is not changed and no assertion of the
02751     group is skipped.
02752 
02753     RECONCILIATION (N1 correction 15, M1): the oracle of the **zero
02754     expiry** was a helper that the group built itself: an **unstarted**
02755     and **unattached** ``_ChannelPump`` that was called directly after
02756     the pump of the round had already ended.  That proved the helper
02757     and not the public chain of the parent.
02758 
02759     The oracle of the zero expiry is now a **public** chain: a round
02760     that is really started through the PRODUCTION default launcher,
02761     with the ORIGINAL context of **that** round, with ONE clock domain
02762     and with the **attached** pump of that round - the pump that the
02763     production session really started and really owns.  The admission
02764     of the pump is held at a transparent gate, the **zero expiry** is
02765     published on the ORIGINAL context **before** the next current bound
02766     of the round is calculated, the reply of the parent is really
02767     waited for with a zero timeout and the attached pump really asks
02768     the queue of the round for nothing.  The pump is held while the
02769     reply is waited for, so its end of stream and its error can never
02770     preempt the reply that the round owes.  The direct helper of the
02771     group stays a **supplement** of the public chain and it is never
02772     the oracle of the zero expiry.  The production is not changed, no
02773     permissive fallback is added to it and no assertion of the group is
02774     skipped.
02775 
02776     RECONCILIATION (N1 correction 15, M3): the owners that the round
02777     really starts are registered **immediately** and they are released
02778     **unconditionally**.  The close of the transport of the child runs
02779     in an inner ``finally`` and it runs also when an assertion of the
02780     group failed.  The stop of the product keeps its own deadline and
02781     its own returned state; the salvage of the test owner is
02782     **separate** from it: it joins every owner that really started with
02783     an explicitly bounded wait, it renews no deadline of the round, it
02784     erases no retained resource of the product and it never claims that
02785     the product released it.  Two subcases fail **on purpose** at an
02786     established checkpoint of a round - after the parent admission and
02787     after the start of the transport of the child - and they catch the
02788     **test sentinel** of the group again: they are the proof of the
02789     negative path of the fixture.
02790 
02791     RECONCILIATION (N1 correction 16, M1): the oracle of the zero
02792     expiry is the **public** start of the round itself and it is no
02793     longer a second private read after a completed start.  The round is
02794     really started through the PRODUCTION default launcher and the
02795     reply admission of that very start is the admission that observes
02796     the zero expiry: the **attached** pump of the round is held at a
02797     transparent gate that calls the original read of the broker, the
02798     ORIGINAL context of the round is tightened **before** the current
02799     bound of the round is calculated by the admission, the reply of the
02800     parent is really waited for with a zero timeout and the attached
02801     pump really asks the queue of the round for nothing.  The expected
02802     error cause, the returned state and the retention of the round are
02803     read from the **returned** evidence of the public start and they
02804     are never inferred from a previous successful start or from the
02805     identity of the context.  The direct helper of the group stays a
02806     **supplement** and it is never the oracle.
02807 
02808     RECONCILIATION (N1 correction 16, M3): the fixture owns a
02809     **registry** of the owners that it really starts and every owner is
02810     registered **immediately**: a channel that registers itself with
02811     its custody before it builds anything, an endpoint that is built
02812     and a session that is created.  A drain thread whose start took
02813     effect and then failed is therefore an owner that the fixture
02814     joins.  The start state of an owner is one of ``STARTED``,
02815     ``NEVER_STARTED`` or ``UNKNOWN`` and an **unknown** owner fails the
02816     proof of the cleanup: it is never taken for an owner that never
02817     started.  Every gate of the fixture - the primitive of the round
02818     included - is released in the **outermost** ``finally``, the close
02819     of the transport of the child never prevents the cleanup of the
02820     other endpoint, every bounded salvage join and every liveness check
02821     **runs while an ordinary failure is unwinding** and the returned
02822     state of the public stop of the round is captured **before** the
02823     salvage.  Five subcases fail on purpose: the **test sentinel**, an
02824     **ordinary** assertion, a close of the transport of the child that
02825     fails and a construction of the transport of the child that fails
02826     **after** its drain thread was really started.
02827     """
02828     document = _live_plan_document()
02829     plan = validate_launch_plan(document, now=time.time())
02830     clock = ManualClock(1000.0)
02831     observations = {'events': []}

02833     class Api:
02834         """The synthetic primitives of the round."""
02835 
02836         def __init__(self):
02837             self.lock = threading.Lock()
02838             self.frames = []
02839             self.thread_handles = []
02840             self.next_handle = 900
02841             # The explicit notification that the round published a
02842             # frame: the reader of the round waits for it and it never
02843             # ends the stream on a timeout.
02844             self.frames_ready = threading.Event()
02845             self.final = b''
02846 
02847         def duplicate_current_thread_handle(self):
02848             with self.lock:
02849                 self.next_handle += 1
02850                 self.thread_handles.append(self.next_handle)
02851                 return self.next_handle
02852 
02853         def cancel_synchronous_io(self, handle):
02854             # RECONCILIATION (N1 correction 14, F3): the round is really
02855             # alive now and the reader of the channel is really inside
02856             # its read when the round is closed.  The cancellation
02857             # releases that read: the reader really ends and no thread
02858             # of the round is left behind after the test.
02859             self.frames_ready.set()
02860             return 'REQUESTED'
02861 
02862         def close_handle(self, handle):
02863             return None
02864 
02865         def read_stream(self, handle, size):
02866             with self.lock:
02867                 if self.frames:
02868                     return self.frames.pop(0)
02869             # The reader of the round waits for the explicit frame of
02870             # the round; the wait is bounded and it never decides the
02871             # end of the stream.
02872             self.frames_ready.wait(30.0)
02873             with self.lock:
02874                 if self.frames:
02875                     return self.frames.pop(0)
02876             return b''
02877 
02878         def write_stream(self, handle, data):
02879             # The terminal frame of the round is published by the write
02880             # of the stop and by nothing else.
02881             with self.lock:
02882                 final = self.final
02883                 self.final = b''
02884             if final:
02885                 with self.lock:
02886                     self.frames.append(final)
02887                 self.frames_ready.set()
02888             return len(data)
02889 
02890     api = Api()
02891     api.frames = [_reply_bytes('READY', 'ARMED', {}, plan, NONCE, 1),
02892                   _reply_bytes('STATUS', 'RUNNING', {}, plan, NONCE, 2)]
02893     api.final = _reply_bytes('STOPPED', 'RELEASED', {}, plan, NONCE, 3)

02895     class FakeFile:
02896         """A file of the round that counts its own closes."""
02897 
02898         def __init__(self, name):
02899             self.name = name
02900             self.closes = 0
02901 
02902         def close(self):
02903             self.closes += 1
02904             return True

02909         def __init__(self):
02910             self.closed = 0
02911 
02912         def Close(self):
02913             self.closed += 1
02914             return True

02916     class FakeProcess:
02917         """The process of the round: it is never an operating system one."""
02918 
02919         pid = 4242
02920         returncode = 0
02921 
02922         def __init__(self):
02923             self.stdin = FakeFile('stdin')
02924             self.stdout = FakeFile('stdout')
02925             self.stderr = FakeFile('stderr')
02926             self._handle = FakeHandle()
02927             self.terminated = 0
02928             self.waited = 0
02929 
02930         def wait(self, timeout=None):
02931             self.waited += 1
02932             return 0
02933 
02934         def terminate(self):
02935             self.terminated += 1
02936             return None

02938     # The bound synthetic executable of the round: a local file whose
02939     # bytes are the bytes that the launcher hashes.  No arbitrary path
02940     # and no network is ever read.
02941     synthetic_executable = tmp_path / 'synthetic-python.exe'
02942     synthetic_executable.write_bytes(b'# the synthetic executable of the round\n')
02943     synthetic_digest = hashlib.sha256(
02944         synthetic_executable.read_bytes()).hexdigest()

02946     def fake_popen(command, **kwargs):
02947         """The in-process seam of the creation of the process.
02948 
02949         The fake never calls the original ``Popen`` and it never
02950         starts an operating system process.  It observes the state of
02951         the launch **at the moment of the creation of the process**: the
02952         aggregate of the launch is already with its owner and the
02953         context of the launch is already the context of the session.
02954         """
02955         observations['events'].append('popen')
02956         observations['command'] = list(command)
02957         aggregates = [item['resource'] for item in _launcher_custody_items()
02958                       if item['kind'] == 'aggregate']
02959         observations['aggregate_at_popen'] = aggregates[-1] if aggregates else None
02960         observations['context_at_popen'] = (aggregates[-1].context
02961                                            if aggregates else None)
02962         return FakeProcess()

02971 
02972         name = 'nt'
02973

02984             return getattr(subprocess, item)

02996     def fake_make_endpoint(file_object, name, *, api=None, clock=None,
02997                            context=None):
02998         """The endpoints of the round: the production ones.
02999 
03000         The endpoint is the production Windows endpoint and the only
03001         thing that is injected is the **synthetic primitive table** of
03002         the round: no native library is loaded and no operating system
03003         call is made.
03004 
03005         N1 correction 16 (M3): the endpoint is registered in the
03006         registry of the fixture **immediately**: the workers of the
03007         endpoint are started later and the product may drop its
03008         reference to them when it closes the endpoint.
03009         """
03010         handles['next'] += 1
03011         endpoint = _WindowsPipeEndpoint(
03012             file_object=file_object, name=name,
03013             api=api if api is not None else api_table['value'],
03014             clock=clock,
03015             handle=handles['next'], context=context)
03016         registry = round_registry['value']
03017         if registry is not None:
03018             registry.register('endpoint:%s' % name, endpoint,
03019                               kind='endpoint', seam='make_endpoint')
03020         return endpoint

03022     # The table of the primitives of the round that is **started now**.
03023     # It is a holder and not a plain name: every round of the group has
03024     # its own table and the launcher of a round really reads the table
03025     # of **that** round.
03026     api_table = {'value': api}
03027     # RECONCILIATION (N1 correction 14, F3): the ONE clock domain of
03028     # the round.  The channel of the round and its two endpoints are
03029     # built by the **production** launcher and they keep the default
03030     # clock of the host (time.monotonic) when the launcher does not
03031     # name one, while the context of the round carries the deadlines of
03032     # the round in the domain of the clock of the session.  A deadline
03033     # of the round was then compared with a clock of **another** domain:
03034     # the admission of the read of the endpoint refused the round (the
03035     # deadline of the round was in the past for the clock of the host),
03036     # the reader of the channel failed, the pump of the parent ended at
03037     # the end of the stream and **no** admission was observed at all.
03038     #
03039     # The channel of the round is therefore built with the clock of the
03040     # round: the production class is used unchanged and only its
03041     # **supported** clock argument is bound.  The channel, its two
03042     # endpoints, the pump of the parent, the context of the round and
03043     # the aggregate of the launch then read every deadline of the
03044     # round in ONE domain.  The production is not changed.
03045     real_stream_channel = broker_module.StreamChannel
03046     # The clock of the round that is **started now**.  The channel of
03047     # the round and its two endpoints read the ONE domain of that
03048     # round: the wrapper below binds it and it never decides a deadline
03049     # of its own.
03050     round_clock = {'value': clock}
03051     # N1 correction 16 (M3): the registry of the owners that the
03052     # fixture really starts.  The registry of the round that is
03053     # **started now** is the one that the seams below write into: an
03054     # owner is registered **immediately** and it is never discovered
03055     # later from a product that may have dropped it.
03056     round_registry = {'value': None}
03057     # The recorder of the queue of the round that is **started now**.
03058     # It is armed only for the round that observes the zero expiry and
03059     # it is installed on the queue of the round at the moment of the
03060     # construction of its channel.
03061     round_queue_recorder = {'value': None}
03062 
03063     def clocked_stream_channel(*args, **kwargs):
03064         """The production channel of the round with the clock of the round.
03065 
03066         The wrapper calls the **original** production constructor and it
03067         only binds the clock of the round: it never replaces a method,
03068         never decides a deadline and never changes the wiring of the
03069         launch.
03070 
03071         N1 correction 16 (M3): the custody of the launch is the
03072         **supported** seam of the production and the wrapper replaces it
03073         with the custody seam of the fixture: a channel registers itself
03074         with its custody **before** it builds anything, so the registry
03075         of the fixture holds the channel even when its construction
03076         failed after its drain thread was started.  The original
03077         custody of the round receives every call unchanged.
03078         """
03079         kwargs.setdefault('clock', round_clock['value'])
03080         registry = round_registry['value']
03081         if registry is not None and kwargs.get('custody') is not None:
03082             kwargs['custody'] = _RegistryCustody(registry, 'launch',
03083                                                  kwargs['custody'])
03084         channel = real_stream_channel(*args, **kwargs)
03085         recorder = round_queue_recorder['value']
03086         if recorder is not None:
03087             recorder(channel)
03088         return channel

03090     def start_round(round_session, round_api, round_clock_value, registry):
03091         """Start one round through the PRODUCTION default launcher.
03092 
03093         The round is started by the **production** session, with the
03094         **production** default launcher and with the **production**
03095         ``launch_broker``.  The only seams are the creation of the
03096         process, the table of the primitives of the round and the clock
03097         of the round: the launcher of the round itself is never
03098         replaced by a stand-in.
03099 
03100         N1 correction 16 (M3): the session of the round is registered
03101         in the registry of the fixture **immediately**: the background
03102         threads and the closer threads of the round are the owners that
03103         the session really started and the product may drop its
03104         reference to them.
03105 
03106         N1 correction 17 (M1): the **returned** state of the public
03107         start is the value that this helper returns.  The public
03108         ``NativeCustodySession.start`` returns the evidence of the round
03109         and never raises for an ordinary failure: the caller reads the
03110         value that the public operation really returned and it never
03111         reads a later evidence object instead of it.
03112         """
03113         previous_api_table = api_table['value']
03114         previous_clock = round_clock['value']
03115         previous_registry = round_registry['value']
03116         api_table['value'] = round_api
03117         round_clock['value'] = round_clock_value
03118         round_registry['value'] = registry
03119         registry.register('session', round_session, kind='session',
03120                           seam='fixture')
03121         try:
03122             with unittest.mock.patch.object(broker_module, 'os', _OsView()), \
03123                  unittest.mock.patch.object(broker_module, 'subprocess',
03124                                             _SubprocessView()), \
03125                  unittest.mock.patch.object(broker_module, 'sys', _SysView()), \
03126                  unittest.mock.patch.object(broker_module, '_make_endpoint',
03127                                             fake_make_endpoint), \
03128                  unittest.mock.patch.object(broker_module, 'StreamChannel',
03129                                             clocked_stream_channel):
03130                 start_return = round_session.start()
03131         finally:
03132             api_table['value'] = previous_api_table
03133             round_clock['value'] = previous_clock
03134             round_registry['value'] = previous_registry
03135         return start_return
03136 
03137     session = NativeCustodySession(plan=document, run_root=tmp_path,
03138                                    parent_child_remaining_seconds=21600,
03139                                    launcher=None, clock=clock,
03140                                    wall=time.time, nonce=NONCE)
03141     parent_salvage = []
03142     child_transport = None
03143     # N1 correction 16 (M3): the registry of the owners that the
03144     # fixture really starts.  Every owner of the parent and of the
03145     # child is registered **immediately**.
03146     parent_registry = _OwnerRegistry()
03147     # CORRECTION 17 (M3): the observer of the starts of the threads of
03148     # the round of the parent.  It is armed before the round is started
03149     # and it is disarmed only after the salvage of that round: a thread
03150     # that the product starts during the stop is an owner of the round.
03151     parent_observer = _StartObserver(parent_registry)
03152     parent_observer_patch = parent_observer.install()
03153     parent_observer_patch.start()
03154     parent_observer.arm()
03155     # The failures of the close of the transport of the child and of
03156     # its two endpoints: they are **recorded** and never discarded.
03157     child_close_failures = []
03158     # CORRECTION 18 (M3): the **exact** objects of the interruptions
03159     # that the cleanup of the round of the parent met.
03160     parent_interrupts = []
03161     try:
03162         # -- the parent: the PRODUCTION default launcher and the
03163         #    PRODUCTION launch_broker, with the creation of the process
03164         #    replaced by the in-process fake -------------------------
03165         parent_start_return = start_round(session, api, clock,
03166                                           parent_registry)
03167         facts = {'context_at_popen':
03168                  observations['context_at_popen'] is session._context,
03169                  'command': observations.get('command'),
03170                  'executable_sha256': getattr(session.broker,
03171                                               'executable_sha256', None),
03172                  'channel_context':
03173                  session.broker._channel._context is session._context,
03174                  'reader_context':
03175                  session.broker._channel._reader._context is session._context,
03176                  'writer_context':
03177                  session.broker._channel._writer._context is session._context,
03178                  'broker_context':
03179                  session.broker._context is session._context,
03180                  'events': list(observations['events'])}
03181         write_phase('r07-launcher', facts)
03182         # The context of the round was **already** the context of the
03183         # session when the process of the round was created.
03184         assert observations.get('context_at_popen') is session._context, \
03185             'the launcher did not hold the context of the session before ' \
03186             'the creation of the process'
03187         # The executable that was hashed is the bound synthetic fixture
03188         # of the round and the hash is the one that the round carries.
03189         assert observations.get('command', [None])[0] \
03190             == str(synthetic_executable), \
03191             'the launcher did not use the bound synthetic executable'
03192         assert session.broker.executable_sha256 == synthetic_digest, \
03193             'the launcher did not hash the bound synthetic executable'
03194         # The channel of the launch and its endpoints carry the same
03195         # context.
03196         assert session.broker._channel._context is session._context
03197         assert session.broker._channel._reader._context is session._context
03198         assert session.broker._channel._writer._context is session._context
03199         assert session.broker._context is session._context
03200         assert session.evidence()['launcher_context_propagated'] is True
03201
