03202         # -- N1 correction 13 (PF13-03) and N1 correction 14 (F3): the
03203         #    **real** parent pump reads the channel of the round through
03204         #    the BrokerProcess and the queue of the channel, and the
03205         #    parent waits for its replies on the event of the round.
03206         #    The recorders are transparent: they call the original owner
03207         #    of the wait and they record the **actual** argument of it.
03208         #    The original context of the round is tightened before the
03209         #    next admission and the next requested argument is the
03210         #    **current** minimum; the zero expiry is included.
03211         #
03212         #    RECONCILIATION (F3): the previous fixture of the group
03213         #    installed the recorder on a pump that was **already dead**:
03214         #    the reader of the channel had failed at the admission of
03215         #    its read (the deadline of the round was in the domain of
03216         #    the facade and the clock of the endpoint was the clock of
03217         #    the host), the channel was at the end of its stream and
03218         #    **no** admission of the parent was observed at all.  The
03219         #    group now proves the **liveness** of the owner of the read
03220         #    before it tightens the bound: the next real read of the
03221         #    broker is **held** at the seam of the BrokerProcess, the
03222         #    transparent recorder is installed, the ORIGINAL cleanup
03223         #    deadline of the round is published and the admission is
03224         #    released.  The original queue method really executes and
03225         #    the recorded argument is the argument that the production
03226         #    really passed to it.
03227         queue_waits = []
03228         original_get = session.broker._channel._queue.get
03229 
03230         def recording_get(block=True, timeout=None):
03231             queue_waits.append(timeout)
03232             return original_get(block=block, timeout=timeout)
03233 
03234         session.broker._channel._queue.get = recording_get
03235         reply_waits = []
03236         original_waiter = custody_module._ReplyWaiter
03237 
03238         class RecordingWaiter(original_waiter):
03239             """A waiter of a reply that records its actual wait."""
03240 
03241             def __init__(self, kinds):
03242                 super().__init__(kinds)
03243                 event = self.event
03244                 original_wait = event.wait
03245 
03246                 def recording_wait(timeout=None):
03247                     reply_waits.append(timeout)
03248                     return original_wait(timeout)
03249 
03250                 event.wait = recording_wait
03251 
03252         # The gate of the admission: the next real read of the broker
03253         # is held **before** the bound of the round is published.  The
03254         # gate calls the original read of the broker: it never decides
03255         # a deadline, it never replaces the decision of the production
03256         # and it never fabricates an admission.
03257         admission_gate = threading.Event()
03258         admission_held = threading.Event()
03259         original_broker_read = session.broker.read
03260 
03261         def gated_broker_read(timeout):
03262             admission_held.set()
03263             admission_gate.wait(15.0)
03264             return original_broker_read(timeout)
03265 
03266         session.broker.read = gated_broker_read
03267         # CORRECTION 20 (M3): the release of the gate of the admission is
03268         # guarded: a cancellation of it never replaces the failure that
03269         # is already unwinding and the **exact** object of the
03270         # cancellation is retained.
03271         admission_cleanup_errors = []
03272         admission_interrupts = []
03273         try:
03274             # -- the pump of the parent is really alive and it reaches
03275             #    its next admission: the liveness of the owner of the
03276             #    read is a **fact** of the round and not an assumption.
03277             assert admission_held.wait(15.0), \
03278                 'the parent pump never reached its next admission'
03279             queue_waits[:] = []
03280             reply_waits[:] = []
03281             # The **published** cleanup deadline of the round shortens
03282             # the next read of the parent: the round is not over yet.
03283             session._context.publish_cleanup_deadline(clock.value + 0.05)
03284             # The admission is released: the production reads the
03285             # **current** minimum of the ORIGINAL context.
03286             admission_gate.set()
03287             deadline = time.monotonic() + 15.0
03288             while time.monotonic() < deadline:
03289                 if any(value is not None and 0.0 < value <= 0.05 + 1e-9
03290                        for value in queue_waits):
03291                     break
03292                 time.sleep(0.005)
03293             # The reply of the parent is bounded by the **published**
03294             # cleanup deadline of the ORIGINAL context: the timeout of
03295             # the call is longer and it may not shorten it.
03296             with unittest.mock.patch.object(custody_module, '_ReplyWaiter',
03297                                             RecordingWaiter):
03298                 with pytest.raises(CustodyError):
03299                     session._read_reply(('READY',), timeout=10.0)
03300             # The **actual** remaining of the round at the admission: it
03301             # is read from the ORIGINAL context and from the clock of
03302             # the round and it is never a nominal number.
03303             remaining = session._context.effective_deadline() - clock()
03304             facts = {'queue_waits': list(queue_waits),
03305                      'reply_waits': list(reply_waits),
03306                      'cleanup': clock.value + 0.05,
03307                      'remaining_at_admission': remaining,
03308                      'pump_alive': bool(session._pump is not None
03309                                         and not session._pump.finished),
03310                      'pump_error': (repr(session._pump.error)
03311                                     if session._pump is not None else None),
03312                      'session_state': session.state}
03313             write_phase('r07-pump-current-minimum', facts)
03314             assert queue_waits, \
03315                 'the parent pump never waited for a frame of the round'
03316             assert 0.0 < queue_waits[-1] <= 0.05 + 1e-9, \
03317                 'the read of the parent pump ignored the current minimum'
03318             assert queue_waits[-1] == pytest.approx(remaining), \
03319                 'the read of the parent pump did not use the actual remaining'
03320             assert reply_waits, \
03321                 'the parent never waited for a reply of the round'
03322             assert 0.0 < reply_waits[-1] <= 0.05 + 1e-9, \
03323                 'the reply event of the parent ignored the current minimum'
03324             assert reply_waits[-1] == pytest.approx(remaining), \
03325                 'the reply event of the parent did not use the actual remaining'
03326         finally:
03327             # The gate of the owner of the test is released
03328             # **unconditionally**: no admission of the parent stays
03329             # blocked behind the test and no thread of the test is left
03330             # behind.
03331             #
03332             # CORRECTION 20 (M3): the release is guarded and the exact
03333             # object of a cancellation of it is retained: it never
03334             # replaces the failure that is already unwinding.
03335             _release_gate(admission_gate.set, admission_cleanup_errors,
03336                           admission_interrupts)
03337         # CORRECTION 20 (M3): the priority of the failures of this round
03338         # is fixed: the failure that is already unwinding wins, then the
03339         # **exact** interruption of the cleanup and only then an ordinary
03340         # assertion.
03341         _raise_first_interruption(admission_interrupts)
03342         # -- N1 correction 16 (M1): the zero expiry on a **public**
03343         #    operation that is really in flight.
03344         #
03345         #    The oracle of the zero expiry is **not** a helper that the
03346         #    group builds itself and it is **not** a second private read
03347         #    after a completed start.  It is the **public** start of the
03348         #    round itself: the round is really started through the
03349         #    PRODUCTION default launcher and the reply admission of that
03350         #    very start is the admission that observes the zero expiry.
03351         #
03352         #    The schedule of the round is bounded at every step:
03353         #
03354         #      1. the round is really started through the PRODUCTION
03355         #         default launcher with the ORIGINAL context of **that**
03356         #         round and with ONE clock domain;
03357         #      2. the **attached** pump of the round - the pump that the
03358         #         production session really started and really owns -
03359         #         reaches its next admission and the admission is
03360         #         **held** at a transparent gate that calls the original
03361         #         read of the broker;
03362         #      3. the reply admission of the **public** start tightens
03363         #         the ORIGINAL context of the round **before** the
03364         #         current bound of the round is calculated by the
03365         #         admission;
03366         #      4. the reply of the parent is really waited for and it
03367         #         asks for **nothing**: the event of the reply is
03368         #         really waited for with a zero timeout;
03369         #      5. the admission is released and the **attached** pump
03370         #         really performs its next read: the queue of the round
03371         #         is really asked for **nothing**.
03372         #
03373         #    The pump of the round is held at the gate while the reply
03374         #    is waited for: the end of the stream of the pump and its
03375         #    error can therefore never preempt the reply that the round
03376         #    owes.  The gate is released only **after** the wait of the
03377         #    admission returned.
03378         zero_api = Api()
03379         zero_clock = ManualClock(2000.0)
03380         zero_document = plan_document(lease_expires_at=time.time() + 120.0)
03381         zero_plan = validate_launch_plan(zero_document, now=time.time())
03382         zero_api.frames = [
03383             _reply_bytes('READY', 'ARMED', {}, zero_plan, NONCE, 1),
03384             _reply_bytes('STATUS', 'RUNNING', {}, zero_plan, NONCE, 2)]
03385         zero_api.final = _reply_bytes('STOPPED', 'RELEASED', {}, zero_plan,
03386                                       NONCE, 3)
03387         zero_session = NativeCustodySession(
03388             plan=zero_document, run_root=tmp_path / 'zero-round',
03389             parent_child_remaining_seconds=21600, launcher=None,
03390             clock=zero_clock, wall=time.time, nonce=NONCE)
03391         zero_registry = _OwnerRegistry()
03392         zero_observer = _StartObserver(zero_registry)
03393         # CORRECTION 18 (M3): the **exact** objects of the interruptions
03394         # that the cleanup of the round of zero met.
03395         zero_interrupts = []
03396         zero_queue_waits = []
03397         zero_reply_waits = []
03398         zero_gate = threading.Event()
03399         zero_held = threading.Event()
03400         zero_gate_facts = []
03401         zero_premise = {}
03402         zero_salvage = []
03403         zero_returned = {}
03404         zero_channel = None
03405         zero_broker = None
03406         zero_pump = None
03407         zero_start_return = {}
03408         zero_trace = []
03409         zero_original_broker_read = broker_module.BrokerProcess.read
03410         zero_original_waiter = custody_module._ReplyWaiter
03411 
03412         def zero_record(event, **facts):
03413             """Record one event of the order of the zero round."""
03414             item = {'event': event, 'at': time.monotonic()}
03415             item.update(facts)
03416             zero_trace.append(item)
03417             return item
03418 
03419         def zero_install_queue_recorder(channel):
03420             """The recorder of the queue of the round of the zero expiry.
03421 
03422             The recorder is installed on the queue of the round at the
03423             moment of the construction of the channel - **before** the
03424             pump of the round is started - and it calls the **original**
03425             method of the queue: it records the **actual** argument
03426             that the production really passed to it.
03427             """
03428             original_get = channel._queue.get
03429 
03430             def zero_recording_get(block=True, timeout=None):
03431                 zero_queue_waits.append(timeout)
03432                 zero_record('QUEUE_GET_ENTERED', timeout=timeout)
03433                 return original_get(block=block, timeout=timeout)
03434 
03435             channel._queue.get = zero_recording_get
03436 
03437         def zero_gated_broker_read(self, timeout):
03438             """The read of the broker of the round, held at the gate.
03439 
03440             The gate belongs to the round of the zero expiry only: the
03441             context of the handle decides that.  It forwards the
03442             original signature and the original result and it decides
03443             only the schedule.
03444             """
03445             if getattr(self, '_context', None) is not zero_session._context:
03446                 return zero_original_broker_read(self, timeout)
03447             zero_record('PUMP_READ_ENTERED', timeout=timeout)
03448             zero_held.set()
03449             released = zero_gate.wait(ZERO_GATE_SECONDS)
03450             zero_gate_facts.append({'released': bool(released),
03451                                     'timeout': ZERO_GATE_SECONDS})
03452             zero_record('GATE_RELEASED', released=bool(released),
03453                         timeout=ZERO_GATE_SECONDS)
03454             return zero_original_broker_read(self, timeout)
03455 
03456         class ZeroTighteningWaiter(zero_original_waiter):
03457             """The waiter of the reply that tightens the ORIGINAL context.
03458 
03459             The waiter of the reply is built by the admission of the
03460             **public** start of the round and it is built **before**
03461             the admission calculates the current bound of the round.
03462             The waiter tightens the ORIGINAL context of the round at
03463             that exact moment and it records the actual wait of the
03464             reply.  It forwards the original signature and the original
03465             result and it decides only the schedule.
03466             """
03467 
03468             def __init__(self, kinds):
03469                 super().__init__(kinds)
03470                 # The premise of the schedule: the attached pump of the
03471                 # round is really inside its read and it is really held
03472                 # there.  A schedule that did not reach the admission
03473                 # fails the premise of the test.
03474                 zero_premise['pump_at_admission'] = bool(
03475                     zero_held.wait(ZERO_ADMISSION_SECONDS))
03476                 pump = zero_session._pump
03477                 zero_premise['pump_attached'] = pump is not None
03478                 zero_premise['pump_eof_at_admission'] = bool(
03479                     pump is not None and pump._eof)
03480                 # CORRECTION 17 (M1): the **actual** error of the pump
03481                 # and its display form are two different facts: the
03482                 # premise asserts the actual value and the display form
03483                 # is never an oracle.
03484                 error = pump.error if pump is not None else None
03485                 zero_premise['pump_error_is_none'] = error is None
03486                 zero_premise['pump_error_type'] = (
03487                     type(error).__name__ if error is not None else None)
03488                 zero_premise['pump_error_repr'] = (
03489                     repr(error) if error is not None else None)
03490                 # The ORIGINAL context of the round is tightened
03491                 # **before** the admission calculates the current bound
03492                 # of the round.
03493                 zero_session._context.bind_absolute_deadline(
03494                     zero_clock.value)
03495                 zero_premise['tightened_at'] = time.monotonic()
03496                 zero_premise['effective_at_admission'] = \
03497                     zero_session._context.effective_deadline()
03498                 event = self.event
03499                 original_wait = event.wait
03500 
03501                 def recording_wait(timeout=None):
03502                     zero_reply_waits.append(timeout)
03503                     zero_record('REPLY_WAIT_ENTERED', timeout=timeout)
03504                     result = original_wait(timeout)
03505                     zero_record('REPLY_WAIT_RETURNED', timeout=timeout,
03506                                 result=bool(result))
03507                     # The pump is released **after** the wait of the
03508                     # admission returned: the end of the stream of the
03509                     # pump and its error can never preempt the reply
03510                     # that the round owes.
03511                     zero_gate.set()
03512                     return result
03513 
03514                 event.wait = recording_wait
03515 
03516         with zero_observer.install():
03517             zero_observer.arm()
03518             try:
03519                 with unittest.mock.patch.object(
03520                         broker_module.BrokerProcess, 'read',
03521                         zero_gated_broker_read), \
03522                      unittest.mock.patch.object(custody_module, '_ReplyWaiter',
03523                                                 ZeroTighteningWaiter):
03524                     round_queue_recorder['value'] = zero_install_queue_recorder
03525                     try:
03526                         zero_record('START_CALLED')
03527                         # CORRECTION 17 (M1): the **returned** state of
03528                         # the public start is the value that the public
03529                         # operation really returned.
03530                         zero_start_return = start_round(zero_session, zero_api,
03531                                                        zero_clock,
03532                                                        zero_registry)
03533                     finally:
03534                         round_queue_recorder['value'] = None
03535                 # The **returned** state of the public start of the
03536                 # round is the value that the public start returned: it
03537                 # is read before the salvage of the fixture and the
03538                 # salvage never replaces it.
03539                 zero_returned = zero_start_return
03540                 zero_pump = zero_session._pump
03541                 zero_broker = getattr(zero_session, 'broker', None)
03542                 zero_channel = getattr(zero_broker, '_channel', None)
03543                 # The **attached** pump of the round really performed its
03544                 # read after the gate was released: the queue of the round
03545                 # was really asked for nothing.
03546                 deadline = time.monotonic() + 15.0
03547                 while time.monotonic() < deadline:
03548                     if zero_pump is not None and zero_pump.finished:
03549                         break
03550                     time.sleep(0.005)
03551                 if zero_pump is not None and zero_pump.finished:
03552                     zero_record('PUMP_ENDED')
03553                 zero_pump_error = zero_pump.error if zero_pump is not None else None
03554                 facts = {'zero_queue_waits': list(zero_queue_waits),
03555                          'zero_reply_waits': list(zero_reply_waits),
03556                          'zero_pump_finished': bool(zero_pump is not None
03557                                                     and zero_pump.finished),
03558                          'zero_pump_error_is_none': zero_pump_error is None,
03559                          'zero_pump_error_type': (
03560                              type(zero_pump_error).__name__
03561                              if zero_pump_error is not None else None),
03562                          'zero_pump_error_repr': (
03563                              repr(zero_pump_error)
03564                              if zero_pump_error is not None else None),
03565                          'zero_pump_frames': zero_pump.frames
03566                          if zero_pump is not None else None,
03567                          'zero_state': zero_returned.get('state'),
03568                          'zero_failures': list(zero_returned.get('failures')
03569                                                or []),
03570                          'zero_broker_retained': zero_returned.get(
03571                              'broker_retained'),
03572                          'zero_broker_identity': zero_returned.get('broker'),
03573                          'zero_premise': dict(zero_premise),
03574                          'zero_gate_facts': list(zero_gate_facts),
03575                          'zero_registry': zero_registry.facts(),
03576                          'zero_trace': list(zero_trace),
03577                          'zero_pump_context_is_the_session_context':
03578                              zero_pump is not None
03579                              and zero_pump._context is zero_session._context,
03580                          'zero_channel_context_is_the_session_context':
03581                              zero_channel is not None
03582                              and zero_channel._context is zero_session._context}
03583                 write_phase('r07-zero-public-chain', facts)
03584                 # The premise of the schedule: the attached pump of the
03585                 # round was really inside its read and it was really held
03586                 # there, and the end of its stream never preempted the
03587                 # reply that the round owes.
03588                 assert zero_premise['pump_at_admission'] is True, \
03589                     'the attached pump of the zero round never reached its admission'
03590                 assert zero_premise['pump_eof_at_admission'] is False, \
03591                     'the attached pump of the zero round was at the end of its ' \
03592                     'stream before the admission of the reply'
03593                 assert zero_premise['pump_error_is_none'] is True, \
03594                     'the attached pump of the zero round failed before the ' \
03595                     'admission of the reply'
03596                 assert zero_premise['pump_error_repr'] is None, \
03597                     'the attached pump of the zero round failed before the ' \
03598                     'admission of the reply'
03599                 # The gate was **released** and it never timed out.
03600                 assert zero_gate_facts, \
03601                     'the admission of the zero round never reached the gate'
03602                 assert zero_gate_facts[0]['released'] is True, \
03603                     'the gate of the admission of the zero round timed out'
03604                 assert zero_gate_facts[0]['timeout'] == ZERO_GATE_SECONDS, \
03605                     'the gate of the admission of the zero round was not bounded'
03606                 # The public start of the round really observed the zero
03607                 # expiry: the reply of the parent asked for **nothing**.
03608                 assert zero_reply_waits, \
03609                     'the public start of the zero round never waited for a reply'
03610                 assert zero_reply_waits[-1] == 0.0, \
03611                     'the reply event of the zero round asked for a wait ' \
03612                     'after the expiry'
03613                 # The **attached** pump of the round really asked the queue
03614                 # of the round for **nothing**.
03615                 assert zero_queue_waits, \
03616                     'the attached pump of the zero round never waited for a frame'
03617                 assert zero_queue_waits[-1] == 0.0, \
03618                     'the attached pump of the zero round asked for a wait ' \
03619                     'after the expiry'
03620                 assert facts['zero_pump_finished'] is True, \
03621                     'the attached pump of the zero round did not end'
03622                 assert facts['zero_pump_context_is_the_session_context'] is True, \
03623                     'the attached pump of the zero round has another context'
03624                 assert facts['zero_channel_context_is_the_session_context'] is True, \
03625                     'the channel of the zero round has another context'
03626                 # CORRECTION 17 (M1): the attached pump is the pump of
03627                 # the **session** and it really ended: an identity and a
03628                 # completed lifecycle are stronger than a presence.
03629                 assert zero_pump is zero_session._pump, \
03630                     'the attached pump of the zero round is not the pump of the session'
03631                 # The **expected error cause** of the public start: the
03632                 # admission of the reply of the round really timed out and
03633                 # the round latched its unknown state.
03634                 assert 'CUSTODY_CHANNEL_TIMEOUT' in facts['zero_failures'], \
03635                     'the public start of the zero round did not name the ' \
03636                     'timeout of the admission: %s' % (facts['zero_failures'],)
03637                 assert 'CUSTODY_CHANNEL_EOF' not in facts['zero_failures'], \
03638                     'the round of the zero expiry ended at the end of its ' \
03639                     'stream instead of the timeout of the admission'
03640                 assert facts['zero_state'] == 'UNKNOWN_RETAINED', \
03641                     'the public start of the zero round did not latch its state'
03642                 # CORRECTION 17 (M1): the **retention** of the round is
03643                 # the returned fact of the product: the **value** of the
03644                 # retention, the **identity** of the retained resource
03645                 # and the source of the identity of the broker are the
03646                 # oracle - the presence of a key is not.
03647                 assert facts['zero_broker_retained'] is True, \
03648                     'the public start of the zero round did not retain its broker'
03649                 assert zero_broker is not None, \
03650                     'the round of the zero expiry has no broker'
03651                 assert zero_session.broker is zero_broker, \
03652                     'the retained original resource is not the object of the round'
03653                 assert facts['zero_broker_identity'] is not None, \
03654                     'the returned state of the public start names no identity'
03655                 assert facts['zero_broker_identity']['identity_source'] \
03656                     == 'PARENT_ORIGINAL_HANDLE', \
03657                     'the identity of the broker of the zero round is a claim ' \
03658                     'and not the original handle'
03659                 assert facts['zero_broker_identity']['pid'] == zero_broker.pid, \
03660                     'the identity of the broker of the zero round names ' \
03661                     'another process'
03662                 assert zero_returned['pump_error'] is None, \
03663                     'the pump of the zero round failed before the admission'
03664                 # CORRECTION 17 (M1): the **order** of the start, of the
03665                 # pump, of the reply, of the gate and of the queue is the
03666                 # recorded order and it is the required one.
03667                 def zero_index(event):
03668                     for position, item in enumerate(zero_trace):
03669                         if item['event'] == event:
03670                             return position
03671                     return None
03672 
03673                 order = [zero_index(name) for name in
03674                          ('START_CALLED', 'PUMP_READ_ENTERED',
03675                           'REPLY_WAIT_ENTERED', 'REPLY_WAIT_RETURNED',
03676                           'GATE_RELEASED', 'QUEUE_GET_ENTERED', 'PUMP_ENDED')]
03677                 assert all(item is not None for item in order), \
03678                     'the order of the zero round was not completely observed: ' \
03679                     '%s' % (zero_trace,)
03680                 assert order == sorted(order), \
03681                     'the order of the start, of the pump, of the reply, of ' \
03682                     'the gate and of the queue of the zero round was not ' \
03683                     'the required one: %s' % (zero_trace,)
03684             finally:
03685                 # The fixture of the zero round is released and the owners
03686                 # that it really started are joined with a bounded wait.
03687                 # The gate of the test and the primitive of the round are
03688                 # released **unconditionally**.
03689                 #
03690                 # CORRECTION 20 (M3): every **initial** release of a gate
03691                 # is guarded **on its own**: a cancellation of the first
03692                 # gate never skips the second one and it never skips the
03693                 # stop, the drain, the proof and the evidence that follow
03694                 # it.  It never replaces the failure that is already
03695                 # unwinding either: the **exact** object of the
03696                 # cancellation is retained and it is re-raised with the
03697                 # priority of the cleanup.
03698                 zero_cleanup_errors = []
03699                 _release_gate(zero_gate.set, zero_cleanup_errors,
03700                               zero_interrupts)
03701                 _release_gate(zero_api.frames_ready.set, zero_cleanup_errors,
03702                               zero_interrupts)
03703                 # The **returned** state of the product is captured
03704                 # **before** the salvage: the salvage of the test is
03705                 # separate from it and it never discards it.
03706                 zero_stop = _capture_stop(zero_session,
03707                                            original_broker=zero_broker,
03708                                            interrupts=zero_interrupts)
03709                 # CORRECTION 17 (M3): the owners of the round are drained
03710                 # **boundedly**: the drain repeats until the owners are
03711                 # quiescent and really joined, the quiescence is
03712                 # asserted **after** the drain and the budget of the
03713                 # drain is one overall bound.
03714                 #
03715                 # CORRECTION 18 (M3): the drain and the writing of the
03716                 # evidence are guarded: one exception never skips the
03717                 # owners that follow it and it never certifies the
03718                 # cleanup.
03719                 zero_drain = {'salvage': [], 'attempts': [], 'proof': None,
03720                               'quiescent': False, 'errors': [],
03721                               'all_salvage': []}
03722                 try:
03723                     zero_drain = _drain_owners(
03724                         zero_registry, session=zero_session,
03725                         channel=zero_channel,
03726                         prefix='zero',
03727                         release=(lambda: zero_gate.set(),
03728                                  lambda: zero_api.frames_ready.set()),
03729                         interrupts=zero_interrupts)
03730                 except BaseException as error:  # noqa: BLE001
03731                     zero_cleanup_errors.append(
03732                         {'kind': 'DRAIN', 'type': type(error).__name__,
03733                          'detail': str(error)})
03734                     _note_interruption(zero_interrupts, error)
03735                 zero_salvage = zero_drain['salvage']
03736                 zero_proof = zero_drain['proof']
03737                 # CORRECTION 18 (M3): a stop that raised and a snapshot
03738                 # of the evidence that failed are facts of the cleanup.
03739                 zero_stop_proof = _stop_proof_failure(zero_stop,
03740                                                       'the round of zero')
03741                 if zero_proof is None and zero_stop_proof is not None:
03742                     zero_proof = zero_stop_proof
03743                 if zero_proof is None and zero_cleanup_errors:
03744                     zero_proof = ('the round of zero met %s'
03745                                   % (zero_cleanup_errors,))
03746                 # CORRECTION 19 (M3): the **quiescence** of the round of
03747                 # zero is part of its proof: an owner that the final
03748                 # resnapshot of the drain found and that no join
03749                 # released fails the round of zero and it fails the test
03750                 # with it.
03751                 if zero_proof is None and zero_drain['quiescent'] is not True:
03752                     zero_proof = 'the round of zero was not quiescent'
03753                 facts = {'salvage': zero_salvage,
03754                          'all_salvage': zero_drain.get('all_salvage', []),
03755                          'stop': zero_stop,
03756                          'drain': zero_drain['attempts'],
03757                          'quiescent': zero_drain['quiescent'],
03758                          'proof_failure': zero_proof,
03759                          'stop_proof_failure': zero_stop_proof,
03760                          'cleanup_errors': zero_cleanup_errors,
03761                          'interrupts': _interruption_facts(zero_interrupts),
03762                          'registry': zero_registry.facts()}
03763                 # the state of the unwinding is read **before** the
03764                 # write: inside the handler of the write the state of the
03765                 # frame is the write error itself and the original
03766                 # failure would never be seen.
03767                 unwinding = sys.exc_info()[0] is not None
03768                 try:
03769                     try:
03770                         write_phase('r07-zero-salvage', facts)
03771                     except BaseException as write_error:  # noqa: BLE001
03772                         zero_cleanup_errors.append(
03773                             {'kind': 'EVIDENCE',
03774                              'type': type(write_error).__name__,
03775                              'detail': str(write_error)})
03776                         interrupted = _note_interruption(zero_interrupts,
03777                                                         write_error)
03778                         if not unwinding:
03779                             if zero_interrupts:
03780                                 # CORRECTION 20 (M3): the **first** exact
03781                                 # pending cancellation of the cleanup wins
03782                                 # over the current one and over the
03783                                 # ordinary assertion of the evidence: a
03784                                 # cancellation of the write is never
03785                                 # preferred to the cancellation that the
03786                                 # cleanup met **before** it.
03787                                 raise zero_interrupts[0]
03788                             if interrupted:
03789                                 # CORRECTION 19 (M3): an **exact**
03790                                 # cancellation of the write is re-raised
03791                                 # as **itself**.
03792                                 raise
03793                             raise AssertionError(
03794                                 'the evidence of the cleanup of the round of '
03795                                 'zero could not be written: %s'
03796                                 % (write_error,))
03797                 finally:
03798                     zero_observer.disarm()
03799                 # CORRECTION 19 (M3): the priority of the failures is
03800                 # fixed: the failure that is already unwinding wins, then
03801                 # the **exact** interruption of the cleanup and only then
03802                 # the ordinary assertion of the proof.
03803                 if zero_interrupts and sys.exc_info()[0] is None:
03804                     raise zero_interrupts[0]
03805                 if zero_proof is not None and sys.exc_info()[0] is None:
03806                     raise AssertionError(zero_proof)
03807                 # CORRECTION 17 (M1): the proof of the cleanup of the
03808                 # round of the zero expiry is **hard**: no owner of the
03809                 # round may have an unknown start state, no owner may
03810                 # have met an error and every owner that really started
03811                 # was really joined and is not alive any more.
03812                 if sys.exc_info()[0] is None:
03813                     _assert_salvage_proved(zero_salvage, 'the round of zero')
03814         # -- N1 correction 15 (M1): the **SUPPLEMENT** of the zero
03815         #    expiry.  The direct helper of the group is kept as a
03816         #    supplement of the public chain above and it is never the
03817         #    oracle of the zero expiry: the acceptance oracle is the
03818         #    attached pump of the round that the production really
03819         #    started.
03820         # The **zero expiry**: the absolute deadline of the round is
03821         # shortened and every next wait of the parent asks for nothing.
03822         # The pump of the round ends when the bound of the round
03823         # arrived: it never spins on a round that is over.  The queue
03824         # wait of the **same** production chain - the BrokerProcess and
03825         # the channel of the round - is therefore observed through the
03826         # handle of the broker.
03827         session._context.bind_absolute_deadline(clock.value)
03828         queue_waits[:] = []
03829         reply_waits[:] = []
03830         session.broker.read(CHANNEL_POLL_SECONDS)
03831         deadline = time.monotonic() + 15.0
03832         while time.monotonic() < deadline:
03833             if session._pump is not None and session._pump.finished:
03834                 break
03835             time.sleep(0.005)
03836         # The reply event of the parent is observed on a **fresh** pump
03837         # of the same production owner with the same original context:
03838         # the pump of the round is over and it never waits again.
03839         zero_pump = custody_module._ChannelPump(
03840             session.broker, decode=session._decode_reply, clock=clock,
03841             context=session._context)
03842         try:
03843             with unittest.mock.patch.object(custody_module, '_ReplyWaiter',
03844                                             RecordingWaiter):
03845                 with pytest.raises(CustodyError):
03846                     zero_pump.take(('READY',), clock.value + 0.05)
03847         finally:
03848             zero_pump.stop()
03849         facts = {'queue_waits': list(queue_waits),
03850                  'reply_waits': list(reply_waits),
03851                  'pump_finished': bool(session._pump is not None
03852                                        and session._pump.finished)}
03853         write_phase('r07-pump-zero-expiry', facts)
03854         assert queue_waits, \
03855             'the parent never waited for a frame of the round'
03856         assert queue_waits[-1] == 0.0, \
03857             'the read of the parent asked for a wait after the expiry'
03858         assert reply_waits, \
03859             'the parent never waited for a reply of the round'
03860         assert reply_waits[-1] == 0.0, \
03861             'the reply event of the parent asked for a wait after the expiry'
03862         assert facts['pump_finished'] is True, \
03863             'the pump of the round did not end after the bound arrived'
03864 
03865         # -- the child: the core, the loop and the transport share one
03866         #    context and the cleanup deadline of the round -----------
03867         # N1 correction 15 (M3): every owner that the child really
03868         # starts is registered **immediately** and it is released
03869         # **unconditionally**.  The close of the transport of the child
03870         # runs in an inner ``finally`` and it runs also when an
03871         # assertion of the group failed: a failed assertion of the
03872         # group may never leave the drain thread of the transport of the
03873         # child or the workers of its two endpoints behind.
03874         child = None
03875         windows = None
03876         child_read_endpoint = None
03877         child_write_endpoint = None
03878         transport = None
03879         try:
03880             # CORRECTION 18 (M3): the construction of the child is
03881             # **inside** the guard of the round: a construction that
03882             # failed half way still owns its teardown and its proof.
03883             kernel32 = SyntheticKernel32()
03884             windows = WindowsApi(table=synthetic_table(kernel32))
03885             child = BrokerCore(win32=windows, plan=sealed_plan(),
03886                                run_root=tmp_path, clock=clock)
03887             assert windows._context is child.context, \
03888                 'the facade of the primitives has no context of the round'
03889             child.bind_absolute_deadline(2000.0)
03890             # N1 correction 16 (M3): the endpoints and the transport of
03891             # the child are registered **immediately**.  A construction
03892             # that failed half way still owns its endpoints and its
03893             # drain thread, and the product may drop its reference to
03894             # them when it closes them.
03895             child_read_endpoint = _WindowsPipeEndpoint(
03896                 file_object=_FakeFile('read'), name='transport-read',
03897                 api=api, clock=clock, handle=920, context=child.context)
03898             parent_registry.register('child:read-endpoint',
03899                                      child_read_endpoint,
03900                                      kind='endpoint', seam='fixture')
03901             child_write_endpoint = _WindowsPipeEndpoint(
03902                 file_object=_FakeFile('write'), name='transport-write',
03903                 api=api, clock=clock, handle=921, context=child.context)
03904             parent_registry.register('child:write-endpoint',
03905                                      child_write_endpoint,
03906                                      kind='endpoint', seam='fixture')
03907             transport = StreamChannel(
03908                 None, None,
03909                 read_endpoint=child_read_endpoint,
03910                 write_endpoint=child_write_endpoint,
03911                 clock=clock, context=child.context,
03912                 custody=_RegistryCustody(parent_registry, 'child'))
03913             child_transport = transport
03914             transport.bind_deadline(2000.0)
03915             loop = BrokerLoop(core=child, transport=transport, plan=child.plan,
03916                               nonce=NONCE, clock=clock, wall=time.time)
03917             loop._deadline_mono = 2000.0
03918             # The STOP of the round: the core publishes the ONE cleanup
03919             # deadline and the terminal reply is written under it.
03920             loop._stop('STOP_COMMAND', 0)
03921             cleanup = child.context.cleanup_deadline_value()
03922             facts = {'cleanup': cleanup,
03923                      'transport': transport._effective_deadline(),
03924                      'write_endpoint': transport._writer._effective_deadline(),
03925                      'write_deadline': transport._writer._write_deadline,
03926                      'read_endpoint': transport._reader._effective_deadline(),
03927                      'api': windows._effective_deadline()}
03928             write_phase('r07-child', facts)
03929             assert cleanup is not None, 'the round published no cleanup deadline'
03930             assert transport._effective_deadline() == cleanup, \
03931                 'the transport kept the long lease of the round'
03932             assert transport._writer._effective_deadline() == cleanup, \
03933                 'the endpoint of the transport kept the long lease'
03934             assert transport._writer._write_deadline == cleanup, \
03935                 'the partial write of the transport kept the long lease'
03936             assert transport._reader._effective_deadline() == cleanup, \
03937                 'the join of the reader kept the long lease'
03938             assert windows._effective_deadline() == cleanup, \
03939                 'the facade of the primitives kept the long lease'
03940         finally:
03941             # The close of the transport of the child is
03942             # **unconditional**: it runs also when an assertion of the
03943             # group failed.  It is the close of the **product** of the
03944             # child and it is not the salvage of the test owner.
03945             #
03946             # N1 correction 16 (M3): a close that failed never
03947             # prevents the cleanup of the **other** endpoint: every
03948             # close of the fixture runs in its own ``finally`` and every
03949             # failure of a close is **recorded** and never discarded.
03950             #
03951             # CORRECTION 19 (M3): the close of the transport and the
03952             # close of **each** endpoint are ``BaseException`` aware: a
03953             # cancellation of the first close never skips the close of
03954             # the second endpoint.  The **exact** object of the
03955             # cancellation is retained and it is re-raised by the final
03956             # salvage of the parent.
03957             if transport is not None:
03958                 try:
03959                     transport.close()
03960                 except BaseException as error:  # noqa: BLE001
03961                     child_close_failures.append(
03962                         {'owner': 'child:transport',
03963                          'type': type(error).__name__,
03964                          'detail': str(error)})
03965                     _note_interruption(parent_interrupts, error)
03966                 finally:
03967                     # A **partial** construction of the transport of the
03968                     # child still owns its two endpoints: they are closed
03969                     # here and they are never forgotten.
03970                     for endpoint in (child_read_endpoint, child_write_endpoint):
03971                         if endpoint is None:
03972                             continue
03973                         try:
03974                             endpoint.close()
03975                         except BaseException as error:  # noqa: BLE001
03976                             child_close_failures.append(
03977                                 {'owner': getattr(endpoint, '_name', 'endpoint'),
03978                                  'type': type(error).__name__,
03979                                  'detail': str(error)})
03980                             _note_interruption(parent_interrupts, error)
03981             else:
03982                 # A **partial** construction of the transport of the
03983                 # child still owns its two endpoints: they are closed
03984                 # here and they are never forgotten.
03985                 for endpoint in (child_read_endpoint, child_write_endpoint):
03986                     if endpoint is None:
03987                         continue
03988                     try:
03989                         endpoint.close()
03990                     except BaseException as error:  # noqa: BLE001
03991                         child_close_failures.append(
03992                             {'owner': getattr(endpoint, '_name', 'endpoint'),
03993                              'type': type(error).__name__,
03994                              'detail': str(error)})
03995                         _note_interruption(parent_interrupts, error)
03996     finally:
03997         # N1 correction 16 (M3): the fixture of the test releases
03998         # **every** gate of the test - the primitive of the round
03999         # included - and joins **every** owner that really started.
04000         # The stop of the product keeps its own deadline and its own
04001         # returned state; the salvage of the test is **separate** from
04002         # it: it renews no deadline of the round, it erases no retained
04003         # resource of the product and it never claims that the product
04004         # released it.
04005         #
04006         # The **returned** state of the product is captured **before**
04007         # the salvage, and the proof of the cleanup is **run** while an
04008         # ordinary failure is already unwinding: it never replaces the
04009         # failure that is unwinding and it is never discarded.
04010         # CORRECTION 20 (M3): the **initial** release of the gate of the
04011         # parent is guarded **on its own**: a cancellation of it never
04012         # skips the stop, the drain, the proof and the evidence that
04013         # follow it and it never replaces the failure that is already
04014         # unwinding.
04015         parent_cleanup_errors = []
04016         _release_gate(api.frames_ready.set, parent_cleanup_errors,
04017                       parent_interrupts)
04018         parent_broker = getattr(session, 'broker', None)
04019         parent_stop = _capture_stop(session, original_broker=parent_broker,
04020                                     interrupts=parent_interrupts)
04021         parent_channel = getattr(parent_broker, '_channel', None)
04022         # CORRECTION 17 (M3): the owners of the round of the parent are
04023         # drained **boundedly**: the drain repeats until the owners are
04024         # quiescent and really joined, the quiescence is asserted
04025         # **after** the drain and the budget of the drain is one overall
04026         # bound that is never reset.
04027         #
04028         # CORRECTION 18 (M3): the drain is guarded: one exception never
04029         # skips the owners that follow it and it never skips the
04030         # evidence of the cleanup.
04031         parent_drain = {'salvage': [], 'attempts': [], 'proof': None,
04032                         'quiescent': False, 'errors': [], 'all_salvage': []}
04033         try:
04034             parent_drain = _drain_owners(
04035                 parent_registry, session=session, channel=parent_channel,
04036                 prefix='parent', release=lambda: api.frames_ready.set(),
04037                 interrupts=parent_interrupts)
04038         except BaseException as error:  # noqa: BLE001
04039             parent_cleanup_errors.append(
04040                 {'kind': 'DRAIN', 'type': type(error).__name__,
04041                  'detail': str(error)})
04042             _note_interruption(parent_interrupts, error)
04043         parent_salvage = parent_drain['salvage']
04044         parent_proof = parent_drain['proof']
04045         # CORRECTION 18 (M3): a stop that raised and a snapshot of the
04046         # evidence that failed are facts of the cleanup: they never
04047         # certify it.
04048         parent_stop_proof = _stop_proof_failure(parent_stop,
04049                                                 'the round of the parent')
04050         if parent_proof is None and parent_stop_proof is not None:
04051             parent_proof = parent_stop_proof
04052         if parent_proof is None and parent_cleanup_errors:
04053             parent_proof = ('the round of the parent met %s'
04054                             % (parent_cleanup_errors,))
04055         # CORRECTION 19 (M3): the **quiescence** of the round of the
04056         # parent is part of its proof: an owner that the final
04057         # resnapshot of the drain found and that no join released fails
04058         # the round of the parent and it fails the test with it.
04059         if parent_proof is None and parent_drain['quiescent'] is not True:
04060             parent_proof = ('the round of the parent was not quiescent')
04061         # the state of the unwinding is read **before** the write: inside
04062         # the handler of the write the state of the frame is the write
04063         # error itself and the original failure would never be seen.
04064         unwinding = sys.exc_info()[0] is not None
04065         try:
04066             try:
04067                 write_phase('r07-parent-salvage',
04068                             {'salvage': parent_salvage,
04069                              'all_salvage':
04070                                  parent_drain.get('all_salvage', []),
04071                              'stop': parent_stop,
04072                              'drain': parent_drain['attempts'],
04073                              'quiescent': parent_drain['quiescent'],
04074                              'proof_failure': parent_proof,
04075                              'stop_proof_failure': parent_stop_proof,
04076                              'cleanup_errors': parent_cleanup_errors,
04077                              'child_close_failures': child_close_failures,
04078                              'interrupts': _interruption_facts(
04079                                  parent_interrupts),
04080                              'registry': parent_registry.facts()})
04081             except BaseException as write_error:  # noqa: BLE001
04082                 parent_cleanup_errors.append(
04083                     {'kind': 'EVIDENCE',
04084                      'type': type(write_error).__name__,
04085                      'detail': str(write_error)})
04086                 interrupted = _note_interruption(parent_interrupts,
04087                                                 write_error)
04088                 if not unwinding:
04089                     if parent_interrupts:
04090                         # CORRECTION 20 (M3): the **first** exact pending
04091                         # cancellation of the cleanup wins over the
04092                         # current one and over the ordinary assertion of
04093                         # the evidence.
04094                         raise parent_interrupts[0]
04095                     if interrupted:
04096                         # CORRECTION 19 (M3): an **exact** cancellation
04097                         # of the write is re-raised as **itself**: it is
04098                         # never converted into an ordinary assertion.
04099                         raise
04100                     raise AssertionError(
04101                         'the evidence of the cleanup of the parent could not '
04102                         'be written: %s' % (write_error,))
04103         finally:
04104             parent_observer.disarm()
04105             parent_observer_patch.stop()
04106         # CORRECTION 19 (M3): the priority of the failures is fixed: the
04107         # failure that is already unwinding wins, then the **exact**
04108         # interruption of the cleanup and only then the ordinary
04109         # assertion of the proof.
04110         if parent_interrupts and sys.exc_info()[0] is None:
04111             raise parent_interrupts[0]
04112         if parent_proof is not None and sys.exc_info()[0] is None:
04113             raise AssertionError(parent_proof)
04114     # CORRECTION 17 (M3): no unrelated failure of a close of the child
04115     # is tolerated next to the intended ones: every failure of a close
04116     # of the child fails the round of the parent.
04117     assert child_close_failures == [], \
04118         'the close of the transport of the child met %s' \
04119         % (child_close_failures,)
04120 
04121     # -- N1 correction 12 (M08-A): the admission of a generation is
04122     #    decided at the **dispatch** of the worker and it consults the
04123     #    **current** original context of the round.  The captured
04124     #    request of the call is never the authority of the decision.
04125     class DispatchApi:
04126         """The primitives of a worker whose dispatch is observed."""
04127 
04128         def __init__(self):
04129             self.calls = []
04130 
04131         def duplicate_current_thread_handle(self):
04132             return 701
04133 
04134         def cancel_synchronous_io(self, handle):
04135             self.calls.append('cancel')
04136             return 'REQUESTED'
04137 
04138         def close_handle(self, handle):
04139             return None
04140 
04141         def read_stream(self, handle, size):
04142             # The primitive of the round: it is never called when the
04143             # admission of the generation was refused.
04144             self.calls.append('read')
04145             return b''
04146 
04147     class PausedQueue:
04148         """The queue of a worker whose dispatch the owner pauses.
04149 
04150         The worker takes the request of the round **after** the owner
04151         of the test released it: the admission of the generation is
04152         therefore decided after the cancellation or the expiry of the
04153         original context was published.
04154         """
04155 
04156         def __init__(self, real):
04157             self._real = real
04158             self.release = threading.Event()
04159 
04160         def put(self, item, timeout=None):
04161             return self._real.put(item, timeout=timeout)
04162 
04163         def get(self, timeout=None):
04164             while not self.release.is_set():
04165                 time.sleep(0.005)
04166             return self._real.get(timeout=timeout)
04167 
04168     for mode in ('cancel', 'expire'):
04169         api = DispatchApi()
04170         context = _RoundContext()
04171         context.bind_absolute_deadline(time.monotonic() + 60.0)
04172         worker = _WindowsPipeWorker(api, 702, kind='read',
04173                                     action=lambda request: b'',
04174                                     name='lvw-n10-r07-dispatch',
04175                                     context=context)
04176         paused = PausedQueue(worker._requests)
04177         worker._requests = paused
04178         worker.start()
04179         # CORRECTION 20 (M3): the release of the paused queue of the
04180         # teardown is guarded: a cancellation of it never replaces the
04181         # failure that is already unwinding and the **exact** object of
04182         # the cancellation is retained.
04183         dispatch_cleanup_errors = []
04184         dispatch_interrupts = []
04185         try:
04186             assert worker.wait_ready(5.0), 'the worker never came up'
04187             # The request is really submitted and the dispatch of the
04188             # worker is paused: the flag of the call and the flag of
04189             # the channel are **not** set.
04190             generation = worker.submit(None)
04191             # The original context of the round is cancelled or it
04192             # expires **before** the decision of the dispatch.
04193             if mode == 'cancel':
04194                 context.request_cancel()
04195             else:
04196                 context.cleanup_deadline(time.monotonic() - 1.0)
04197             paused.release.set()
04198             assert worker.wait_done(generation, 10.0), \
04199                 'the refused generation never completed'
04200             outcome = worker.outcome(generation)
04201             assert outcome is not None, 'the outcome of the generation is gone'
04202             facts = {'mode': mode, 'generation': generation,
04203                      'error': outcome['error'],
04204                      'calls': list(api.calls)}
04205             write_phase('r07-dispatch-%s' % mode, facts)
04206             # The primitive of the round was never called: the
04207             # admission of the generation was refused before it.
04208             assert api.calls == [], \
04209                 'the primitive of the round ran after a refused admission'
04210             if mode == 'cancel':
04211                 assert outcome['error'] == 'BROKER_PIPE_CANCELLED'
04212             else:
04213                 assert outcome['error'] == 'BROKER_PIPE_TIMEOUT'
04214             assert outcome['generation'] == generation, \
04215                 'the outcome of another generation was read'
04216         finally:
04217             # CORRECTION 20 (M3): the release of the paused queue is
04218             # guarded and the exact object of a cancellation of it is
04219             # retained: it never replaces the failure that is already
04220             # unwinding.
04221             _release_gate(paused.release.set, dispatch_cleanup_errors,
04222                           dispatch_interrupts)
04223             worker.stop_accepting()
04224             worker.join_bounded(10.0)
04225             # the **exact** cancellation wins over the ordinary assertion
04226             # of the join that follows it.
04227             _raise_first_interruption(dispatch_interrupts)
04228             assert not worker.is_alive(), 'the worker was left behind'
04229         # CORRECTION 20 (M3): the priority of the failures of this
04230         # round is fixed: the failure that is already unwinding wins,
04231         # then the **exact** interruption of the cleanup.
04232         _raise_first_interruption(dispatch_interrupts)
04233 
04234     # -- N1 correction 12 (M08-A): the await of the endpoint notices
04235     #    the cancellation of the **original** context of the round and
04236     #    not only the flag of the call.
04237     released = threading.Event()
04238 
04239     class AwaitApi:
04240         """The primitives of an endpoint whose read is released."""
04241 
04242         def __init__(self):
04243             self.calls = []
04244 
04245         def duplicate_current_thread_handle(self):
04246             return 703
04247 
04248         def cancel_synchronous_io(self, handle):
04249             self.calls.append('cancel')
04250             released.set()
04251             return 'REQUESTED'
04252 
04253         def close_handle(self, handle):
04254             return None
04255 
04256         def read_stream(self, handle, size):
04257             self.calls.append('read')
04258             released.wait(10.0)
04259             return b''
04260 
04261     await_context = _RoundContext()
04262     await_context.bind_absolute_deadline(time.monotonic() + 60.0)
04263     endpoint = _WindowsPipeEndpoint(file_object=_FakeFile('await'),
04264                                     name='await', api=AwaitApi(),
04265                                     clock=time.monotonic, handle=704,
04266                                     context=await_context)
04267     holder = {}
04268 
04269     def run_read():
04270         try:
04271             endpoint.read(None, None)
04272         except BaseException as error:  # noqa: BLE001 - the failure stays visible
04273             holder['error'] = error
04274 
04275     reader = threading.Thread(target=run_read)
04276     reader.start()
04277     # CORRECTION 20 (M3): the release of the gate of the await is
04278     # guarded: a cancellation of it never skips the join and the close
04279     # that follow it and it never replaces the failure that is already
04280     # unwinding.
04281     await_cleanup_errors = []
04282     await_interrupts = []
04283     try:
04284         deadline = time.monotonic() + 10.0
04285         while endpoint._reader is None and time.monotonic() < deadline:
04286             time.sleep(0.005)
04287         assert endpoint._reader is not None, 'the worker never started'
04288         # The original context of the round is cancelled while the read
04289         # is really in flight.
04290         await_context.request_cancel()
04291         reader.join(10.0)
04292         assert not reader.is_alive(), 'the read of the round was left behind'
04293         error = holder.get('error')
04294         facts = {'error': type(error).__name__,
04295                  'code': getattr(error, 'code', None),
04296                  'calls': endpoint._api.calls}
04297         write_phase('r07-await-context', facts)
04298         assert isinstance(error, BrokerError), \
04299             'the await of the endpoint did not name the cancellation'
04300         assert error.code == 'BROKER_PIPE_CANCELLED', \
04301             'the await of the endpoint did not notice the original context'
04302     finally:
04303         # CORRECTION 20 (M3): the release of the gate is guarded and the
04304         # exact object of a cancellation of it is retained.
04305         _release_gate(released.set, await_cleanup_errors, await_interrupts)
04306         reader.join(10.0)
04307         endpoint.close()
04308     # the **exact** cancellation of the cleanup wins over an ordinary
04309     # assertion and it is re-raised as **itself**.
04310     _raise_first_interruption(await_interrupts)
04311 
04312     # -- N1 correction 15 (M3): the fixture of the group releases
04313     # -- N1 correction 16 (M3): the fixture of the group releases
04314     #    **every** gate of the test and joins **every** owner that
04315     #    really started - also when an assertion of the group fails.
04316     #    The subcases below fail **on purpose** at a meaningful
04317     #    checkpoint of a round and they catch **their own** exception
04318     #    again: the distinctive sentinel of the group, an **ordinary**
04319     #    assertion of the group, a close of the transport of the child
04320     #    that fails and a construction of the transport of the child
04321     #    that fails **after** its drain thread was really started.
04322     #    They are the proof of the negative path of the fixture: a
04323     #    failed assertion of the group may never leave a gate of the
04324     #    test blocked and it may never leave an owner of the round
04325     #    behind.  A receipt of the happy path alone cannot prove the
04326     #    negative path.
04327     def controlled_failure(checkpoint):
04328         """Fail one round of the fixture on purpose at a checkpoint.
04329 
04330         The round is really started through the PRODUCTION default
04331         launcher and the failure of the fixture of the test is raised
04332         at the requested checkpoint of the round.  The ``finally`` of
04333         the subcase releases every gate of the test, closes the
04334         transport of the child where it was constructed, stops the
04335         round, joins every owner that really started and **runs the
04336         proof of the cleanup** - also while the failure of the subcase
04337         is already unwinding.
04338 
04339         Every failure of the subcase is caught by **its own type**: it
04340         is never a broad ``Exception`` and it never swallows a real
04341         failure of the production.
04342 
04343         N1 correction 16 (M3): the owners of the round are registered
04344         **immediately** in the registry of the fixture, so the drain
04345         thread of a transport whose construction failed is still an
04346         owner that the fixture joins.
04347 
04348         N1 correction 17 (M3): the cleanup region is moved **outward**
04349         by one level: it encloses the start of the round **and** the
04350         assertion of the state of the round, so a failure of either is
04351         cleaned up, joined and proved.  The deliberate failure is
04352         **raised inside** the cleanup region and it is **caught outside**
04353         of it: the cleanup demonstrably runs while the failure is
04354         unwinding and that fact is recorded.  The ``CLOSE_FAILURE``
04355         checkpoint is the one case whose failure is produced **by the
04356         cleanup itself**: it is recorded with its exact type and its
04357         exact message and it never propagates.
04358         """
04359         controlled_api = Api()
04360         controlled_clock = ManualClock(3000.0)
04361         controlled_document = plan_document(lease_expires_at=time.time()
04362                                             + 120.0)
04363         controlled_plan = validate_launch_plan(controlled_document,
04364                                                 now=time.time())
04365         controlled_api.frames = [
04366             _reply_bytes('READY', 'ARMED', {}, controlled_plan, NONCE, 1),
04367             _reply_bytes('STATUS', 'RUNNING', {}, controlled_plan, NONCE, 2)]
04368         controlled_api.final = _reply_bytes('STOPPED', 'RELEASED', {},
04369                                             controlled_plan, NONCE, 3)
04370         controlled_session = NativeCustodySession(
04371             plan=controlled_document,
04372             run_root=tmp_path / ('controlled-' + checkpoint.lower()),
04373             parent_child_remaining_seconds=21600, launcher=None,
04374             clock=controlled_clock, wall=time.time, nonce=NONCE)
04375         controlled_registry = _OwnerRegistry()
04376         # CORRECTION 17 (M3): the observer of the starts of the round of
04377         # the subcase.  It is armed before the round is started and it
04378         # is disarmed only after the salvage of that round.
04379         controlled_observer = _StartObserver(controlled_registry)
04380         gate = threading.Event()
04381         held = threading.Event()
04382         transport = None
04383         read_endpoint = None
04384         write_endpoint = None
04385         raised = []
04386         close_failures = []
04387         injected_starts = []
04388         facts = {}
04389         controlled_original_broker = None
04390         # CORRECTION 18 (M3): the errors of the cleanup of this subcase
04391         # are **lists** and they are never overwritten: one exception
04392         # never skips the owners that follow it.
04393         controlled_cleanup_errors = []
04394         # CORRECTION 18 (M3): the **exact** objects of the interruptions
04395         # that the cleanup of this subcase met.
04396         controlled_interrupts = []
04397 
04398         def write_facts():
04399             """Write the facts of the subcase of the group."""
04400             write_phase('r07-controlled-failure-%s' % checkpoint.lower(),
04401                         facts)
04402 
04403         controlled_observer_patch = controlled_observer.install()
04404         controlled_observer_patch.start()
04405         controlled_observer.arm()
04406         try:
04407             # (A) The OUTER region: the deliberate failures are caught
04408             #     **here**, outside of the entire cleanup region.
04409             try:
04410                 # (B) The cleanup region: the start of the round and the
04411                 #     assertion of its state are **inside** it.
04412                 controlled_start_return = start_round(
04413                     controlled_session, controlled_api, controlled_clock,
04414                     controlled_registry)
04415                 controlled_original_broker = getattr(controlled_session,
04416                                                       'broker', None)
04417                 assert controlled_start_return['state'] == 'RUNNING', \
04418                     'the round of the controlled failure did not really start'
04419                 controlled_original_read = controlled_session.broker.read
04420 
04421                 def controlled_gated_read(timeout):
04422                     held.set()
04423                     gate.wait(30.0)
04424                     return controlled_original_read(timeout)
04425 
04426                 controlled_session.broker.read = controlled_gated_read
04427                 # The **established checkpoint** of the parent: the
04428                 # attached pump of the round really reached its next
04429                 # admission and the admission is really held.
04430                 assert held.wait(15.0), \
04431                     'the parent of the controlled failure never reached its admission'
04432                 if checkpoint in ('CHILD_TRANSPORT', 'CLOSE_FAILURE',
04433                                   'CONSTRUCTOR_FAILURE'):
04434                     controlled_kernel32 = SyntheticKernel32()
04435                     controlled_windows = WindowsApi(
04436                         table=synthetic_table(controlled_kernel32))
04437                     controlled_core = BrokerCore(
04438                         win32=controlled_windows, plan=sealed_plan(),
04439                         run_root=tmp_path, clock=controlled_clock)
04440                     controlled_core.bind_absolute_deadline(4000.0)
04441                     read_endpoint = _WindowsPipeEndpoint(
04442                         file_object=_FakeFile('controlled-read'),
04443                         name='controlled-read', api=controlled_api,
04444                         clock=controlled_clock, handle=930,
04445                         context=controlled_core.context)
04446                     controlled_registry.register(
04447                         'controlled:read-endpoint', read_endpoint,
04448                         kind='endpoint', seam='fixture')
04449                     write_endpoint = _WindowsPipeEndpoint(
04450                         file_object=_FakeFile('controlled-write'),
04451                         name='controlled-write', api=controlled_api,
04452                         clock=controlled_clock, handle=931,
04453                         context=controlled_core.context)
04454                     controlled_registry.register(
04455                         'controlled:write-endpoint', write_endpoint,
04456                         kind='endpoint', seam='fixture')
04457                     if checkpoint == 'CONSTRUCTOR_FAILURE':
04458                         # CORRECTION 17 (M3): the construction of the
04459                         # transport of the child fails **inside** the
04460                         # constructor: the ORIGINAL ``Thread.start``
04461                         # really takes effect and the exception is
04462                         # raised after it.  The seam targets the
04463                         # **exact** drain object of this child - the
04464                         # thread whose target is the drain of the
04465                         # channel of the **context of the child** -
04466                         # and it never touches a thread of the parent
04467                         # that carries the same name.
04468                         original_thread_start = threading.Thread.start
04469                         # CORRECTION 19 (M3): the channel of the **parent**
04470                         # is captured **before** the child is constructed:
04471                         # the lookup of the child never returns it and the
04472                         # identity of the child is proved against it.  The
04473                         # parent is the channel that the launch of the
04474                         # round already registered as ``launch:channel``.
04475                         controlled_parent_channel = None
04476                         for entry in controlled_registry.entries():
04477                             if entry.get('kind') == 'channel' \
04478                                     and entry.get('name') == 'launch:channel':
04479                                 controlled_parent_channel = entry.get('owner')
04480                                 break
04481                         if controlled_parent_channel is None:
04482                             controlled_parent_channel = getattr(
04483                                 getattr(controlled_session, 'broker', None),
04484                                 '_channel', None)
04485 
04486                         def controlled_custody_channel():
04487                             """The **exact** child channel of the custody.
04488 
04489                             CORRECTION 19 (M3): the lookup selects the
04490                             channel that the **controlled** custody of the
04491                             fixture really retained - the newly registered
04492                             child - and **never** the channel of the
04493                             parent that the launch already registered as
04494                             ``launch:channel``.  The identity of the child
04495                             is proved against the parent and a name that
04496                             is similar never claims the ownership.
04497                             """
04498                             children = [entry['owner']
04499                                         for entry
04500                                         in controlled_registry.entries()
04501                                         if entry['kind'] == 'channel'
04502                                         and entry['name'] == 'controlled:channel']
04503                             if not children:
04504                                 # the fallback is the **newly retained**
04505                                 # owner: the channel of the parent that
04506                                 # the launch already registered is never
04507                                 # the child.
04508                                 children = [entry['owner']
04509                                             for entry
04510                                             in controlled_registry.entries()
04511                                             if entry['kind'] == 'channel'
04512                                             and entry['owner'] is not
04513                                             controlled_parent_channel]
04514                             assert len(children) == 1, \
