04515                                 'the controlled custody did not retain ' \
04516                                 'exactly one child channel: %s' % (children,)
04517                             child = children[0]
04518                             assert child is not controlled_parent_channel, \
04519                                 'the child channel of the controlled ' \
04520                                 'custody is the channel of the parent'
04521                             return child
04522 
04523                         def controlled_start_then_raise(thread, *args,
04524                                                          **kwargs):
04525                             # CORRECTION 18 (M3): the actual attribute of
04526                             # the stdlib thread is ``_target`` - a plain
04527                             # ``threading.Thread`` has **no** ``target``
04528                             # attribute at all and the bound method of
04529                             # the target carries its owner in
04530                             # ``__self__``.
04531                             target = getattr(thread, '_target', None)
04532                             owner = getattr(target, '__self__', None)
04533                             if not isinstance(owner,
04534                                               broker_module.StreamChannel):
04535                                 return original_thread_start(thread, *args,
04536                                                             **kwargs)
04537                             if getattr(owner, '_context', None) \
04538                                     is not controlled_core.context:
04539                                 return original_thread_start(thread, *args,
04540                                                             **kwargs)
04541                             # the **exact** custody-owned child: only the
04542                             # channel that the custody of the fixture
04543                             # really registered is injected into and
04544                             # never a thread of the parent that carries
04545                             # the same name.
04546                             child = controlled_custody_channel()
04547                             if owner is not child:
04548                                 return original_thread_start(thread, *args,
04549                                                             **kwargs)
04550                             injected_starts.append(
04551                                 {'id': id(thread), 'name': thread.name,
04552                                  'owner_id': id(owner),
04553                                  'child_id': id(child),
04554                                  'target_name': getattr(target, '__name__',
04555                                                        None),
04556                                  'target_owner_is_custody_channel': True,
04557                                  'target_owner_is_parent':
04558                                      owner is
04559                                      controlled_parent_channel})
04560                             # the ORIGINAL start really takes effect and
04561                             # the exact failure of the fixture is raised
04562                             # **inside** the constructor of the child.
04563                             original_thread_start(thread, *args, **kwargs)
04564                             raise _R07ConstructorFailure(checkpoint)
04565 
04566                         with unittest.mock.patch.object(
04567                                 threading.Thread, 'start',
04568                                 controlled_start_then_raise):
04569                             transport = broker_module.StreamChannel(
04570                                 None, None, read_endpoint=read_endpoint,
04571                                 write_endpoint=write_endpoint,
04572                                 clock=controlled_clock,
04573                                 context=controlled_core.context,
04574                                 custody=_RegistryCustody(
04575                                     controlled_registry, 'controlled'))
04576                     else:
04577                         transport = StreamChannel(
04578                             None, None, read_endpoint=read_endpoint,
04579                             write_endpoint=write_endpoint,
04580                             clock=controlled_clock,
04581                             context=controlled_core.context,
04582                             custody=_RegistryCustody(
04583                                 controlled_registry, 'controlled'))
04584                         transport.bind_deadline(4000.0)
04585                         if checkpoint == 'CLOSE_FAILURE':
04586                             # The close of the transport of the child
04587                             # fails: the cleanup of the **other**
04588                             # endpoint may never be skipped because of
04589                             # it and the failure of the close is
04590                             # **recorded** with its exact type and its
04591                             # exact message.
04592                             def controlled_failing_close():
04593                                 raise RuntimeError(
04594                                     'controlled_close_failure')
04595 
04596                             transport.close = controlled_failing_close
04597                 # The deliberate failure of the fixture of the test is
04598                 # **raised** here: it propagates through the cleanup
04599                 # region and it is caught **outside** of it.
04600                 if checkpoint == 'ORDINARY_ASSERTION':
04601                     # An **ordinary** assertion of the group: it is a
04602                     # real ``AssertionError`` of the test and it is
04603                     # caught by its own type - never by a broad
04604                     # ``Exception``.
04605                     try:
04606                         assert controlled_session.state == 'NOT_A_STATE', \
04607                             _R07OrdinaryAssertion.TOKEN
04608                     except AssertionError as error:
04609                         # The token of the deliberate assertion is
04610                         # normalised: the exact token of the failure
04611                         # survives the rewriting of the assertions of
04612                         # the test runner.
04613                         raise _R07OrdinaryAssertion(
04614                             _R07OrdinaryAssertion.TOKEN) from error
04615                 elif checkpoint not in ('CONSTRUCTOR_FAILURE',
04616                                         'CLOSE_FAILURE'):
04617                     # CORRECTION 18 (M3): the ``CLOSE_FAILURE``
04618                     # checkpoint is a **cleanup only** case: its failure
04619                     # is produced by the cleanup itself and it never
04620                     # raises the generic sentinel of the group.
04621                     raise _R07Sentinel(checkpoint)
04622             finally:
04623                 # (C) The cleanup region: every gate of the test is
04624                 #     released and every owner of the round is
04625                 #     released with it, **while** the deliberate
04626                 #     failure is unwinding.
04627                 #
04628                 # CORRECTION 18 (M3): every step of the cleanup is
04629                 # guarded and every error is **retained**: an
04630                 # interruption of the first close never skips the close
04631                 # of the second endpoint and it never skips the owners
04632                 # of the round.
04633                 #
04634                 # CORRECTION 20 (M3): every **initial** release of a gate
04635                 # is guarded **on its own**: a cancellation of the first
04636                 # gate never skips the second one, never skips the
04637                 # closes, the stop, the drain, the proof and the evidence
04638                 # that follow it and it never replaces the failure that
04639                 # is already unwinding.
04640                 _release_gate(gate.set, controlled_cleanup_errors,
04641                               controlled_interrupts)
04642                 _release_gate(controlled_api.frames_ready.set,
04643                               controlled_cleanup_errors,
04644                               controlled_interrupts)
04645                 try:
04646                     # The close of the transport of the child runs in
04647                     # its own ``finally``: a close that failed never
04648                     # prevents the cleanup of the **other** endpoint
04649                     # and every failure of a close is **recorded**.
04650                     if transport is not None:
04651                         try:
04652                             transport.close()
04653                         except BaseException as error:  # noqa: BLE001
04654                             close_failures.append(
04655                                 {'owner': 'controlled:transport',
04656                                  'type': type(error).__name__,
04657                                  'detail': str(error)})
04658                             _note_interruption(controlled_interrupts, error)
04659                     for endpoint in (read_endpoint, write_endpoint):
04660                         if endpoint is None:
04661                             continue
04662                         try:
04663                             endpoint.close()
04664                         except BaseException as error:  # noqa: BLE001
04665                             close_failures.append(
04666                                 {'owner': getattr(endpoint, '_name',
04667                                                   'endpoint'),
04668                                  'type': type(error).__name__,
04669                                  'detail': str(error)})
04670                             _note_interruption(controlled_interrupts, error)
04671                 finally:
04672                     # The **returned** state of the product is captured
04673                     # **before** the salvage of the fixture and the
04674                     # proof of the cleanup is **run** while the failure
04675                     # of the subcase is already unwinding.
04676                     controlled_stop = _capture_stop(
04677                         controlled_session,
04678                         original_broker=controlled_original_broker,
04679                         interrupts=controlled_interrupts)
04680                     # CORRECTION 20 (M3): this release is guarded as
04681                     # well: a cancellation of it never skips the drain,
04682                     # the proof and the evidence that follow it.
04683                     _release_gate(controlled_api.frames_ready.set,
04684                                   controlled_cleanup_errors,
04685                                   controlled_interrupts)
04686                     controlled_broker = getattr(controlled_session,
04687                                                 'broker', None)
04688                     controlled_channel = getattr(controlled_broker,
04689                                                  '_channel', None)
04690                     # CORRECTION 17 (M3): the owners of the round are
04691                     # drained **boundedly** and the quiescence is
04692                     # asserted **after** the drain.
04693                     drain = {'salvage': [], 'attempts': [], 'proof': None,
04694                              'quiescent': False, 'errors': [],
04695                              'all_salvage': []}
04696                     try:
04697                         drain = _drain_owners(
04698                             controlled_registry,
04699                             session=controlled_session,
04700                             channel=controlled_channel, prefix='controlled',
04701                             release=(lambda: gate.set(),
04702                                      lambda: controlled_api.frames_ready.set()),
04703                             interrupts=controlled_interrupts)
04704                     except BaseException as error:  # noqa: BLE001
04705                         controlled_cleanup_errors.append(
04706                             {'kind': 'DRAIN',
04707                              'type': type(error).__name__,
04708                              'detail': str(error)})
04709                         _note_interruption(controlled_interrupts, error)
04710                     # CORRECTION 18 (M3): a stop that raised and a
04711                     # snapshot of the evidence that failed are facts of
04712                     # the cleanup: they never certify it.
04713                     stop_proof = _stop_proof_failure(
04714                         controlled_stop,
04715                         'the controlled failure of %s' % checkpoint)
04716                     salvage = drain['salvage']
04717                     proof = drain['proof']
04718                     if proof is None and stop_proof is not None:
04719                         proof = stop_proof
04720                     if proof is None and controlled_cleanup_errors:
04721                         proof = ('the controlled failure of %s met %s'
04722                                  % (checkpoint, controlled_cleanup_errors))
04723                     # CORRECTION 19 (M3): the **quiescence** of the round
04724                     # of the controlled failure is part of its proof: an
04725                     # owner that the final resnapshot of the drain found
04726                     # and that no join released fails the subcase with it.
04727                     if proof is None and drain['quiescent'] is not True:
04728                         proof = ('the owners of the controlled failure of %s '
04729                                  'were not quiescent' % checkpoint)
04730                     facts = {'checkpoint': checkpoint,
04731                              'failure': raised[0] if raised else None,
04732                              'gate_released': bool(gate.is_set()),
04733                              'stop': controlled_stop,
04734                              'close_failures': list(close_failures),
04735                              'cleanup_errors':
04736                                  list(controlled_cleanup_errors),
04737                              'cleanup_ran_while_unwinding':
04738                                  sys.exc_info()[0] is not None,
04739                              'salvage': salvage,
04740                              'all_salvage': drain.get('all_salvage', []),
04741                              'drain': drain['attempts'],
04742                              'quiescent': drain['quiescent'],
04743                              'proof_failure': proof,
04744                              'stop_proof_failure': stop_proof,
04745                              'interrupts': _interruption_facts(
04746                                  controlled_interrupts),
04747                              'injected_starts': list(injected_starts),
04748                              'registry': controlled_registry.facts()}
04749                     unwinding = sys.exc_info()[0] is not None
04750                     try:
04751                         write_facts()
04752                     except BaseException as write_error:  # noqa: BLE001
04753                         controlled_cleanup_errors.append(
04754                             {'kind': 'EVIDENCE',
04755                              'type': type(write_error).__name__,
04756                              'detail': str(write_error)})
04757                         interrupted = _note_interruption(
04758                             controlled_interrupts, write_error)
04759                         if not unwinding:
04760                             if controlled_interrupts:
04761                                 # CORRECTION 20 (M3): the **first** exact
04762                                 # pending cancellation of the cleanup wins
04763                                 # over the current one and over the
04764                                 # ordinary assertion of the evidence.
04765                                 raise controlled_interrupts[0]
04766                             if interrupted:
04767                                 # CORRECTION 19 (M3): an **exact**
04768                                 # cancellation of the write is re-raised
04769                                 # as **itself**.
04770                                 raise
04771                             raise AssertionError(
04772                                 'the evidence of the controlled failure of %s '
04773                                 'could not be written: %s'
04774                                 % (checkpoint, write_error))
04775                     # CORRECTION 19 (M3): the priority of the failures is
04776                     # fixed: the failure that is already unwinding wins,
04777                     # then the **exact** interruption of the cleanup and
04778                     # only then the ordinary assertion of the proof.  A
04779                     # proof assertion never renders the rethrow of the
04780                     # exact interruption unreachable.
04781                     if controlled_interrupts and not unwinding:
04782                         raise controlled_interrupts[0]
04783                     if proof is not None and not unwinding:
04784                         raise AssertionError(proof)
04785         except _R07Sentinel as error:
04786             raised.append({'class': _R07Sentinel,
04787                            'type': type(error).__name__,
04788                            'token': str(error),
04789                            'detail': str(error)})
04790             facts['failure'] = raised[0]
04791             write_facts()
04792         except _R07ConstructorFailure as error:
04793             raised.append({'class': _R07ConstructorFailure,
04794                            'type': type(error).__name__,
04795                            'token': str(error),
04796                            'detail': str(error)})
04797             facts['failure'] = raised[0]
04798             write_facts()
04799         except _R07OrdinaryAssertion as error:
04800             raised.append({'class': _R07OrdinaryAssertion,
04801                            'type': type(error).__name__,
04802                            'token': str(error),
04803                            'detail': str(error),
04804                            'is_assertion_error': isinstance(
04805                                error, AssertionError)})
04806             facts['failure'] = raised[0]
04807             write_facts()
04808         finally:
04809             controlled_observer.disarm()
04810             controlled_observer_patch.stop()
04811             # CORRECTION 20 (M3): this **last** release is guarded as
04812             # well: a cancellation of it never replaces the failure that
04813             # is already unwinding.
04814             _release_gate(controlled_api.frames_ready.set,
04815                           controlled_cleanup_errors, controlled_interrupts)
04816         return facts
04817 
04818     controlled_results = {}
04819     for checkpoint in ('PARENT_ADMISSION', 'CHILD_TRANSPORT',
04820                        'ORDINARY_ASSERTION', 'CLOSE_FAILURE',
04821                        'CONSTRUCTOR_FAILURE'):
04822         controlled = controlled_failure(checkpoint)
04823         controlled_results[checkpoint] = controlled
04824         if checkpoint == 'CLOSE_FAILURE':
04825             # CORRECTION 17 (M3): this checkpoint is a **cleanup only**
04826             # case: the deliberate failure is produced by the cleanup
04827             # itself, it is **recorded**, it never propagates and it is
04828             # the **only** failure that may be recorded.  The physical
04829             # cleanup of the round is proved by the joins and the
04830             # liveness of the owners and not by the injected failure.
04831             assert controlled['failure'] is None, \
04832                 'the close failure of %s was not a cleanup-only failure' \
04833                 % checkpoint
04834             assert controlled['cleanup_ran_while_unwinding'] is False, \
04835                 'the close failure of %s was claimed to unwind' % checkpoint
04836             close_failures = controlled['close_failures']
04837             assert len(close_failures) == 1, \
04838                 'the close failure of %s was not the only failure: %s' \
04839                 % (checkpoint, close_failures)
04840             assert close_failures[0]['owner'] == 'controlled:transport', \
04841                 'the close failure of %s was not the failure of the ' \
04842                 'transport of the child: %s' % (checkpoint, close_failures)
04843             assert close_failures[0]['type'] == 'RuntimeError', \
04844                 'the close failure of %s was not the exact type: %s' \
04845                 % (checkpoint, close_failures)
04846             assert close_failures[0]['detail'] == 'controlled_close_failure', \
04847                 'the close failure of %s was not the exact message: %s' \
04848                 % (checkpoint, close_failures)
04849             assert [item for item in close_failures
04850                     if item['owner'] != 'controlled:transport'] == [], \
04851                 'the close failure of %s was accompanied by another owner: %s' \
04852                 % (checkpoint, close_failures)
04853             assert [item for item in close_failures
04854                     if item['type'] != 'RuntimeError'] == [], \
04855                 'the close failure of %s was accompanied by another type: %s' \
04856                 % (checkpoint, close_failures)
04857         else:
04858             failure = controlled['failure']
04859             assert failure is not None, \
04860                 'the controlled failure of %s did not raise its failure' \
04861                 % checkpoint
04862             # CORRECTION 17 (M3): the **exact** type and the **exact**
04863             # token of the deliberate failure - never a name of a type
04864             # and never a non-``None`` check.
04865             if checkpoint == 'ORDINARY_ASSERTION':
04866                 assert failure['class'] is _R07OrdinaryAssertion, \
04867                     'the controlled failure of %s was not the ordinary ' \
04868                     'assertion of the group: %s' % (checkpoint, failure)
04869                 assert failure['is_assertion_error'] is True, \
04870                     'the controlled failure of %s was not an ' \
04871                     'AssertionError: %s' % (checkpoint, failure)
04872                 assert failure['token'] == _R07OrdinaryAssertion.TOKEN, \
04873                     'the controlled failure of %s was not the exact token: %s' \
04874                     % (checkpoint, failure)
04875             elif checkpoint == 'CONSTRUCTOR_FAILURE':
04876                 assert failure['class'] is _R07ConstructorFailure, \
04877                     'the controlled failure of %s was not the failure of ' \
04878                     'the constructor: %s' % (checkpoint, failure)
04879                 assert failure['token'] == 'CONSTRUCTOR_FAILURE', \
04880                     'the controlled failure of %s was not the exact token: %s' \
04881                     % (checkpoint, failure)
04882                 # the seam really took effect on the **exact** drain of
04883                 # the child and on nothing else
04884                 assert len(controlled['injected_starts']) == 1, \
04885                     'the constructor failure of %s did not take effect on ' \
04886                     'exactly one drain: %s' \
04887                     % (checkpoint, controlled['injected_starts'])
04888                 assert controlled['injected_starts'][0]['name'] \
04889                     == 'lvw-native-custody-channel', \
04890                     'the constructor failure of %s did not take effect on ' \
04891                     'the drain of the child: %s' \
04892                     % (checkpoint, controlled['injected_starts'])
04893                 # CORRECTION 19 (M3): the injection really took effect on
04894                 # the **child** and never on the channel of the parent
04895                 # that the launch already registered.
04896                 assert controlled['injected_starts'][0][
04897                     'target_owner_is_parent'] is False, \
04898                     'the constructor failure of %s was injected into the ' \
04899                     'channel of the parent: %s' \
04900                     % (checkpoint, controlled['injected_starts'])
04901             else:
04902                 assert failure['class'] is _R07Sentinel, \
04903                     'the controlled failure of %s was not the sentinel of ' \
04904                     'the group: %s' % (checkpoint, failure)
04905                 assert failure['type'] == '_R07Sentinel', \
04906                     'the controlled failure of %s was not the exact type: %s' \
04907                     % (checkpoint, failure)
04908                 assert failure['token'] == checkpoint, \
04909                     'the controlled failure of %s was not the exact token: %s' \
04910                     % (checkpoint, failure)
04911             # CORRECTION 18 (M3): **every** non-close controlled
04912             # subcase requires its **own** list of the close failures to
04913             # be empty: an unrelated failure of a close of an endpoint
04914             # fails the subcase instead of being tolerated next to the
04915             # intended failure.  The list of the main round is a
04916             # different list and it supplies no coverage here.
04917             assert controlled['close_failures'] == [], \
04918                 'the controlled failure of %s met an unrelated close ' \
04919                 'failure: %s' % (checkpoint, controlled['close_failures'])
04920             # the cleanup really ran **while** the deliberate failure was
04921             # unwinding: that is the proof of the negative path
04922             assert controlled['cleanup_ran_while_unwinding'] is True, \
04923                 'the cleanup of the controlled failure of %s did not run ' \
04924                 'while the failure was unwinding' % checkpoint
04925         assert controlled['gate_released'] is True, \
04926             'the gate of the controlled failure of %s stayed blocked' \
04927             % checkpoint
04928         assert controlled['proof_failure'] is None, \
04929             'the cleanup of the controlled failure of %s was not proved: %s' \
04930             % (checkpoint, controlled['proof_failure'])
04931         assert controlled['quiescent'] is True, \
04932             'the owners of the controlled failure of %s were not quiescent' \
04933             % checkpoint
04934         _assert_salvage_proved(controlled['salvage'],
04935                                'the controlled failure of %s' % checkpoint)
04936 
04937 # -- N10-R08: the retry of a cancellation re-reads the context ------------
04938 
04939 def test_n10_r08_the_retry_of_a_cancellation_re_reads_the_context():
04940     """N10-R08 (M08): the retry of a cancellation tightens itself.
04941 
04942     Obligation: every round of the retry of a cancellation reads the
04943     **latest** effective context: a cleanup deadline that the round
04944     published while the retry was running shortens the retry and the
04945     pause of the retry is cropped to the time that is really left.  A
04946     retry never waits longer than the context allows and it never falls
04947     back to a fresh budget of its own.
04948 
04949     Entry: ``_WindowsPipeWorker.request_cancel`` and
04950     ``_WindowsPipeWorker._effective_deadline``.  Trigger: the
04951     cancellation reports ``NOT_FOUND`` and the round publishes a
04952     shorter cleanup deadline while the retry runs.  Oracle: the retry
04953     stopped at the shorter deadline and it really repeated.  Negative:
04954     the retry never follows the old absolute deadline beyond the
04955     cleanup.  Returned state: the number of the attempts is named.
04956     Salvage: the owner of the test releases the worker at the end.
04957     """
04958     context = _RoundContext()
04959     context.bind_absolute_deadline(time.monotonic() + 30.0)
04960     release = threading.Event()
04961     attempts = []
04962 
04963     class Api:
04964         def duplicate_current_thread_handle(self):
04965             return 701
04966 
04967         def cancel_synchronous_io(self, handle):
04968             attempts.append(time.monotonic())
04969             if len(attempts) == 1:
04970                 # The round publishes a **shorter** cleanup deadline
04971                 # while the retry of the cancellation is running.
04972                 context.cleanup_deadline(time.monotonic() + 0.05)
04973             return 'NOT_FOUND'
04974 
04975         def close_handle(self, handle):
04976             return None
04977 
04978     worker = _WindowsPipeWorker(Api(), 702, kind='read',
04979                                 action=lambda request: release.wait(10.0),
04980                                 name='lvw-n10-r08', context=context)
04981     worker.start()
04982     # CORRECTION 20 (M3): the release of the gate of the retry is
04983     # guarded: a cancellation of it never skips the join that follows it
04984     # and it never replaces the failure that is already unwinding.
04985     retry_cleanup_errors = []
04986     retry_interrupts = []
04987     try:
04988         assert worker.wait_ready(5.0), 'the worker never came up'
04989         worker.submit(None)
04990         assert worker.inside.wait(5.0), 'the worker never entered its read'
04991         started = time.monotonic()
04992         state = worker.request_cancel(None)
04993         elapsed = time.monotonic() - started
04994         facts = {'state': state, 'attempts': len(attempts),
04995                  'elapsed': elapsed,
04996                  'cleanup': context.cleanup_deadline_value()}
04997         write_phase('r08-retry', facts)
04998         assert len(attempts) >= 2, \
04999             'the cancellation was not really repeated'
05000         assert elapsed < 2.0, \
05001             'the retry ignored the cleanup deadline of the round'
05002         assert context.cleanup_deadline_value() is not None
05003     finally:
05004         # CORRECTION 20 (M3): the release of the gate is guarded and the
05005         # exact object of a cancellation of it is retained.
05006         _release_gate(release.set, retry_cleanup_errors, retry_interrupts)
05007         worker.join(10.0)
05008         # the **exact** cancellation wins over the ordinary assertion of
05009         # the join that follows it.
05010         _raise_first_interruption(retry_interrupts)
05011         assert not worker.is_alive(), 'the worker was left behind'
05012 
05013 
05014 # -- N10-R09: every exit of the startup owner joins its thread ------------
05015 
05016 def test_n10_r09_every_exit_of_the_startup_owner_joins_its_thread(tmp_path):
05017     """N10-R09 (M08): the owner of a startup step joins its thread.
05018 
05019     Obligation: **every** exit of a startup step - the success, the
05020     timeout, the ordinary failure, the cancellation and the
05021     ``SystemExit`` - waits for the **real** exit of the thread of the
05022     step in a ``finally``, bounded by the deadline of the round.  A
05023     thread that did not exit keeps its owner: it stays in the owners
05024     that are in flight and the round keeps the fact.  The original
05025     exception object is the one that leaves and the wait never
05026     publishes the cleanup deadline of the round before the round
05027     failed.
05028 
05029     Entry: ``BrokerLoop._run_startup`` and ``_StartupOwner.settle``.
05030     Trigger: the result of the step is published while the **real**
05031     thread of the step is still alive, and the result is an ordinary
05032     error, a cancellation and a ``SystemExit``.  Oracle: the owner was
05033     not released before the real exit of its thread, the bounded join
05034     ran and the original exception object is the one that propagated.
05035     Negative: the publication of the result is never a proof that the
05036     thread ended.  Returned state: the owners that are in flight are
05037     named.  Salvage: the owner of the test releases the threads that
05038     the product started.
05039 
05040     RECONCILIATION (N1 correction 11): the three phases of the group
05041     shared **one** deadline of the loop, so the second phase inherited
05042     the budget that the first phase had already consumed and it was
05043     refused by the deadline of the round before its result was read.
05044     Every phase is now an **independent bounded scenario** with its own
05045     core, its own loop and its own deadline: no phase borrows the
05046     expired budget of a previous phase, the real result is published
05047     while the real thread of the step is still alive, the join of every
05048     thread is **unconditional** and no sleep ever proves the exit of a
05049     thread.
05050 
05051     RECONCILIATION (N1 correction 14, F4): the **unconditional** join
05052     of the group was also applied to the thread of the step that
05053     **never started**: the seam of that subcase raised before the
05054     original start of the thread and the join of the fixture raised
05055     ``RuntimeError: cannot join thread before it is started``.  The
05056     facts of the start decide now: the start did not return, the
05057     thread has **no identity** and it is not alive, so the fixture
05058     skips **only** that impossible join.  The unconditional bounded
05059     join stays for every thread that really started, an object without
05060     an identity is never read as a physical exit of a thread and the
05061     causal negative of the group (an unproved start is never released)
05062     is unchanged.
05063     """
05064     clock = time.monotonic
05065     release = threading.Event()
05066     owners = []
05067     # CORRECTION 20 (M3): the release of the gate of the startup owner is
05068     # guarded: a cancellation of it never skips the joins that follow it
05069     # and it never replaces the failure that is already unwinding.
05070     startup_cleanup_errors = []
05071     startup_interrupts = []
05072     try:
05073         for error in (BrokerError('BROKER_STARTUP_ORDINARY'),
05074                       KeyboardInterrupt('startup_cancelled'),
05075                       SystemExit(3)):
05076             published = threading.Event()
05077             # RECONCILIATION (N1 correction 11): every phase of the
05078             # group is an **independent bounded scenario**.  It has its
05079             # own core, its own loop and its own deadline: no phase
05080             # borrows the expired budget of a previous phase and no
05081             # sleep ever proves the exit of a thread.
05082             win32 = RecordingWin32()
05083             core = BrokerCore(win32=win32, plan=sealed_plan(), run_root=tmp_path,
05084                               clock=clock)
05085             core.bind_absolute_deadline(clock() + 60.0)
05086             transport = type('T', (), {'bind_deadline': lambda self, value: value,
05087                                        'write': lambda self, raw: None})()
05088             loop = BrokerLoop(core=core, transport=transport, plan=core.plan,
05089                               nonce=NONCE, clock=clock, wall=time.time)
05090             loop._deadline_mono = clock() + 2.0
05091 
05092             def action(error=error, published=published, loop=loop):
05093                 # The result of the step is published and the **real**
05094                 # thread of the step stays alive: the publication of the
05095                 # result is never a proof that the thread ended.
05096                 owner = loop._startup_owner
05097                 owner._results.put_nowait(('error', error))
05098                 published.set()
05099                 release.wait(15.0)
05100 
05101             published.clear()
05102             with pytest.raises(type(error)) as info:
05103                 loop._run_startup('ARM', action)
05104             assert published.is_set(), \
05105                 'the result of the step was never published'
05106             # The original exception object is the one that propagated.
05107             assert info.value is error, \
05108                 'the original exception object was replaced'
05109             owner = loop._startup_owner
05110             owners.append(owner)
05111             # The real thread of the step is still alive: the owner was
05112             # not released before the real exit of its thread.
05113             assert owner.in_flight, \
05114                 'the thread of the step ended while the owner was released'
05115             assert loop._startup_in_flight is owner, \
05116                 'the owner of a step that did not end was released'
05117             assert owner in core.context.inflight(), \
05118                 'the owner of a step that did not end left the flight'
05119             facts = {'error_type': type(error).__name__,
05120                      'in_flight': len(core.context.inflight()),
05121                      'retained': loop._startup_in_flight is owner}
05122             write_phase('r09-%s' % type(error).__name__.lower(), facts)
05123     finally:
05124         # The salvage of the owner of the test: the threads that the
05125         # product started are released and joined with a bounded wait.
05126         # The join is **unconditional**: no phase leaves a thread of the
05127         # round behind.
05128         _release_gate(release.set, startup_cleanup_errors,
05129                       startup_interrupts)
05130         for owner in owners:
05131             thread = owner._thread
05132             if thread is not None:
05133                 thread.join(10.0)
05134                 # the **exact** cancellation wins over the ordinary
05135                 # assertion of the join that follows it.
05136                 _raise_first_interruption(startup_interrupts)
05137                 assert not thread.is_alive(), \
05138                     'the thread of a startup step was left behind'
05139 
05140     # -- N1 correction 12 (M08-C): the wait of the owner of a startup
05141     #    step is cropped to the time that is really left until the
05142     #    **current** effective deadline of the round.  The owner holds
05143     #    the original context and it never keeps a copy of a number.
05144     #    RECONCILIATION (N1 correction 13): the recorder of the queue
05145     #    implements the **whole** signature of ``queue.get``: the owner
05146     #    observes the result that is already published with a non
05147     #    blocking ``get_nowait`` and that observation is **not** a wait.
05148     #    The two are recorded apart and the recorder forwards the
05149     #    arguments of the original call.  The schedule of the group
05150     #    tightens the deadline of the round **between** two waits and
05151     #    the next wait receives the time that is really left.
05152     clock = ManualClock(1000.0)
05153     context = _RoundContext(clock=clock)
05154     context.bind_absolute_deadline(clock.value + 0.5)
05155     owner = _StartupOwner(name='inventory', clock=clock,
05156                           deadline=clock.value + 0.5, context=context)
05157     observed = []
05158     requested = []
05159     first_wait = threading.Event()
05160     original_get = owner._results.get
05161 
05162     def recording_get(block=True, timeout=None):
05163         if block is False:
05164             # The observation of the owner asks for the result that is
05165             # already published and it never waits for one.
05166             observed.append(timeout)
05167             return original_get(block=False)
05168         requested.append(timeout)
05169         if len(requested) == 1:
05170             first_wait.set()
05171         return original_get(block=True, timeout=timeout)
05172 
05173     owner._results.get = recording_get
05174 
05175     def tighten():
05176         # The deadline of the round is tightened **between** two waits
05177         # of the owner: the next wait receives the time that is really
05178         # left and it is cropped to it.  The last step expires the
05179         # round even when the barrier was never reached: the owner
05180         # never waits forever.
05181         first_wait.wait(10.0)
05182         clock.advance(0.49)
05183         time.sleep(0.2)
05184         clock.advance(0.02)
05185         clock.advance(10.0)
05186 
05187     tightener = threading.Thread(target=tighten, daemon=True,
05188                                   name='lvw-n10-r09-tightener')
05189     tightener.start()
05190     try:
05191         with pytest.raises(BrokerError) as info:
05192             owner.await_result()
05193     finally:
05194         tightener.join(10.0)
05195         assert not tightener.is_alive(), \
05196             'the thread that tightens the deadline was left behind'
05197     facts = {'observed': list(observed), 'requested': list(requested),
05198              'code': info.value.code}
05199     write_phase('r09-await-wait', facts)
05200     assert info.value.code == 'BROKER_STARTUP_DEADLINE_EXCEEDED', \
05201         'the owner of the step did not refuse the expired round'
05202     assert observed, 'the owner of the step never observed its result'
05203     assert requested, 'the owner of the step never waited for its result'
05204     assert requested[0] <= 0.05 + 1e-9, \
05205         'the wait of the owner exceeded the poll cap of the package'
05206     assert max(requested) <= 0.5 + 1e-9, \
05207         'the wait of the owner exceeded the remaining of the round'
05208     # The wait that followed the tightening received the time that was
05209     # really left and it was cropped to it.
05210     assert min(requested[1:]) <= 0.01 + 1e-9, \
05211         'the wait of the owner was not cropped to the tightened remaining'
05212 
05213     # -- N1 correction 12 (M08-D): a start that raised **after** the
05214     #    thread of the step really came up is not a release.  The owner
05215     #    joins the thread that it really has, it keeps its owner while
05216     #    the thread is alive and a later settle of the **same** owner
05217     #    converges after the real exit of the thread.
05218     tail_release = threading.Event()
05219     original_start = threading.Thread.start
05220 
05221     def failing_start(self):
05222         original_start(self)
05223         if self.name.startswith('lvw-native-custody-startup-'):
05224             raise KeyboardInterrupt('start_interrupted')
05225 
05226     clock = time.monotonic
05227     core = BrokerCore(win32=RecordingWin32(), plan=sealed_plan(),
05228                       run_root=tmp_path, clock=clock)
05229     core.bind_absolute_deadline(clock() + 60.0)
05230     transport = type('T', (), {'bind_deadline': lambda self, value: value,
05231                                'write': lambda self, raw: None})()
05232     loop = BrokerLoop(core=core, transport=transport, plan=core.plan,
05233                       nonce=NONCE, clock=clock, wall=time.time)
05234     loop._deadline_mono = clock() + 30.0
05235     # CORRECTION 20 (M3): the release of the gate of the interrupted
05236     # start is guarded: a cancellation of it never skips the settle and
05237     # the join that follow it and it never replaces the failure that is
05238     # already unwinding.
05239     interrupted_cleanup_errors = []
05240     interrupted_interrupts = []
05241     try:
05242         with unittest.mock.patch.object(threading.Thread, 'start',
05243                                         failing_start):
05244             with pytest.raises(KeyboardInterrupt):
05245                 loop._run_startup('ARM', lambda: tail_release.wait(15.0))
05246         owner = loop._startup_owner
05247         assert owner is not None, 'the owner of the step was never built'
05248         facts = {'started': owner._started,
05249                  'in_flight': owner.in_flight,
05250                  'retained': loop._startup_in_flight is owner}
05251         write_phase('r09-interrupted-start', facts)
05252         # The facts of the thread decided: the thread of the step really
05253         # came up and the owner does not release it.
05254         assert owner._started is True, \
05255             'the facts of the thread of the step were not consulted'
05256         assert loop._startup_in_flight is owner, \
05257             'the owner of an interrupted start was released'
05258         assert owner.in_flight is True
05259         assert owner in core.context.inflight()
05260         # The thread of the step is really alive: the owner is still
05261         # the owner of it.
05262         tail_release.set()
05263         assert owner.settle(10.0) is True, \
05264             'the later settle of the owner did not converge'
05265         assert owner.in_flight is False
05266     finally:
05267         # CORRECTION 20 (M3): the release of the gate is guarded and the
05268         # exact object of a cancellation of it is retained.
05269         _release_gate(tail_release.set, interrupted_cleanup_errors,
05270                       interrupted_interrupts)
05271         for pending in loop.context.inflight():
05272             pending.settle(5.0)
05273         thread = getattr(loop._startup_owner, '_thread', None)
05274         if thread is not None:
05275             thread.join(10.0)
05276             # the **exact** cancellation wins over the ordinary assertion
05277             # of the join that follows it.
05278             _raise_first_interruption(interrupted_interrupts)
05279             assert not thread.is_alive(), \
05280                 'the thread of a startup step was left behind'
05281 
05282     # -- N1 correction 12 (M08-D): a start that never came up stays
05283     #    **unknown**: the owner is never released and no join of a
05284     #    thread that never started is attempted.
05285     #
05286     #    RECONCILIATION (N1 correction 14, F4): the group joined the
05287     #    thread of the step **unconditionally** and the thread of this
05288     #    subcase never started at all: the seam of the group raised
05289     #    **before** the original start of the thread, so the join of
05290     #    the fixture raised ``RuntimeError: cannot join thread before
05291     #    it is started``.  The facts of the start decide now: the start
05292     #    did not return, the thread has **no identity** and it is not
05293     #    alive, so the fixture skips **only** that impossible join.
05294     #    The unconditional bounded join stays for every thread that
05295     #    really started (the two phases above), an object without an
05296     #    identity is never read as a physical exit of a thread and the
05297     #    causal negative of the group - an unproved start is never
05298     #    released - is unchanged.
05299     owner = _StartupOwner(name='never', clock=time.monotonic,
05300                           deadline=time.monotonic() + 10.0)
05301 
05302     def never_started(self):
05303         raise RuntimeError('start_failed_before_the_thread')
05304 
05305     with unittest.mock.patch.object(threading.Thread, 'start',
05306                                     never_started):
05307         with pytest.raises(RuntimeError):
05308             owner.start(lambda: None)
05309     assert owner.settle(5.0) is False, \
05310         'an unproved start of a step was released'
05311     assert owner.in_flight is False
05312     thread = getattr(owner, '_thread', None)
05313     assert thread is not None, \
05314         'the owner of the step did not keep the thread of the step'
05315     # The **facts** of the start of the thread of the step.
05316     start_facts = {'owner_started': owner._started,
05317                    'ident': getattr(thread, 'ident', None),
05318                    'alive': bool(thread.is_alive()),
05319                    'settle': owner.settle(5.0),
05320                    'in_flight': owner.in_flight}
05321     write_phase('r09-never-started', start_facts)
05322     assert owner._started is False, \
05323         'the owner of the step claims that the start of its thread returned'
05324     assert thread.ident is None, \
05325         'the thread of the step really started'
05326     assert thread.is_alive() is False, \
05327         'the thread of the step is alive although it never started'
05328     if thread.ident is not None or thread.is_alive():
05329         # A thread that really started is joined **unconditionally** and
05330         # with a bounded wait.
05331         thread.join(5.0)
05332         assert not thread.is_alive(), \
05333             'the thread of a startup step was left behind'
05334 
05335 
05336 # -- N10-R10: the outcome of the generation wins --------------------------
05337 
05338 class _BarrierRefusal(Exception):
05339     """The refusal of the publication barrier of the test.
05340 
05341     It is a **premise** of the test and not a failure of the
05342     production: the production catches the ordinary exceptions of the
05343     seam of the round, and the outer test asserts that the premise
05344     failed and that the barrier never reported a success.  An exception
05345     that the production swallowed is never a test oracle.
05346     """
05347 
05348     def __init__(self, reason):
05349         super().__init__(reason)
05350         self.reason = reason
05351 
05352 
05353 class _M09TimeoutApi:
05354     """The primitives of a chain whose fault is published by the owner.
05355 
05356     The chain of the phase is the **real** chain of the round: the
05357     session, the handle of the broker, the channel of the round and the
05358     worker of its read endpoint.  The façade is the only seam and it
05359     does two things and nothing else:
05360 
05361     * it publishes the ordinary failure of the read of the **current**
05362       generation, and it publishes it at the moment of the coordination
05363       of the round - the moment at which the effective timeout of the
05364       round is decided;
05365     * it decides whether the coordination of the round **succeeds**: a
05366       coordination whose worker really ended is a confirmed one and a
05367       coordination whose worker is still alive is a failed one.
05368 
05369     The seam never replaces the selection of the outcome, the history
05370     of the error, the public stop of the round or a fact of the round:
05371     every one of them is the production.
05372     """
05373 
05374     def __init__(self, *, worker_holder=None, confirm_the_worker=False,
05375                  hold_the_worker=False):
05376         self.lock = threading.Lock()
05377         self.frames = []
05378         self.final = b''
05379         self._outcomes = queue.Queue()
05380         self.read_waiting = threading.Event()
05381         self.fault_observed = threading.Event()
05382         self.eof_observed = threading.Event()
05383         self.closed = []
05384         self.close_ledger = []
05385         self.thread_handles = []
05386         self.next_handle = 700
05387         # The ordinary failure of the read and the object of it: the
05388         # owner of the test keeps the **original** error object and it
05389         # compares it with the object that the production really kept.
05390         self.fault = None
05391         self.fault_object = None
05392         self.worker_holder = worker_holder if worker_holder is not None else {}
05393         # Whether the coordination of the round waits for the real exit
05394         # of the worker.  A coordination that waits is a **confirmed**
05395         # one and a coordination that does not wait is a **failed** one
05396         # while the worker is still alive.
05397         self.confirm_the_worker = confirm_the_worker
05398         # The event that holds the worker inside its own release, so
05399         # that the failure of the coordination is deterministic.
05400         self.hold_the_worker = hold_the_worker
05401         self.release_the_worker = threading.Event()
05402         self.cancel_calls = 0
05403         # The **checked** barrier of the publication of the outcome of
05404         # the generation.  The barrier is a **premise** of the test and
05405         # not a fact of the production: it observes the immutable
05406         # outcome of the **named** generation and it refuses the premise
05407         # when the outcome is absent, when it belongs to another
05408         # generation, when it does not carry the expected kind of the
05409         # ordinary failure or when it does not keep the **exact**
05410         # original error object.  A refused premise is never a passed
05411         # barrier and the primitive of the round never returns
05412         # ``REQUESTED`` as if the barrier had passed.
05413         self.barrier_probe = 'EXACT'
05414         self.barrier = {
05415             'probe': 'EXACT',
05416             'expected_generation': None,
05417             'probe_generation': None,
05418             'observed': False,
05419             'observed_generation': None,
05420             'observed_error': None,
05421             'observed_object': None,
05422             'failure': None,
05423             'refused': None,
05424             'passed': False,
05425             'returned': None,
05426             'entered_at': None,
05427             'observed_at': None,
05428             'left_at': None,
05429             'return_state': None,
05430             'generation': None,
05431         }
05432         # CORRECTION 17 (M2): the completion of the barrier is published
05433         # by the seam itself and the facts of the barrier are **durable**:
05434         # the snapshot is persisted as a real phase document when the
05435         # barrier is finalised, on the successful path **and** on the
05436         # refused path.  A shallow in-memory dictionary alone is not
05437         # execution evidence.
05438         self.barrier_completed = threading.Event()
05439         self.barrier_snapshot = None
05440         self.barrier_document = None
05441         self.barrier_expectation = None
05442         self.barrier_write_error = None
05443         # CORRECTION 18 (M2): the **ledger** of the phase and the name of
05444         # the barrier of this phase.  The attempt of the barrier is
05445         # marked **in the seam itself**: an assertion of the phase that
05446         # fails after the seam ran may never relabel the barrier as
05447         # ``NOT_RUN``.
05448         self.barrier_ledger = None
05449         self.barrier_name = None
05450         # CORRECTION 18 (M2): the durable document of the barrier is
05451         # written **before** the completion is published and the fact of
05452         # that write is recorded.
05453         self.barrier_persisted = False
05454         # CORRECTION 19 (M3): the **exact** objects of the interruptions
05455         # that the seam of the round met: the owner of the phase re-raises
05456         # the first one of them when no other failure is unwinding.
05457         self.interrupts = []
05458         # CORRECTION 20 (M3): the **exact** object of a cancellation that
05459         # the durable write of the barrier met: it is never reduced to a
05460         # type and a detail and it is never converted into an ordinary
05461         # assertion of the phase.
05462         self.barrier_interruption = None
05463 
05464     # -- the explicit outcomes of the reader ------------------------------
05465     def publish_read_fault(self):
05466         """Publish the ordinary failure of the read, explicitly."""
05467         with self.lock:
05468             error = self.fault
05469             self.fault = None
05470         if error is not None:
05471             self.publish_outcome('FAULT', error)
05472         return error
05473 
05474     def publish_outcome(self, kind, payload=None):
05475         self._outcomes.put((kind, payload))
05476         return payload
05477 
05478     # -- the primitives ----------------------------------------------------
05479     def duplicate_current_thread_handle(self):
05480         with self.lock:
05481             self.next_handle += 1
05482             self.thread_handles.append(self.next_handle)
05483             return self.next_handle
05484 
05485     def cancel_synchronous_io(self, handle):
05486         self.cancel_calls += 1
05487         # The coordination of the round publishes the ordinary failure
05488         # of the read of the current generation: the fault is really
05489         # published **before** the endpoint consumes the outcome.
05490         self.publish_read_fault()
05491         worker = self.worker_holder.get('worker')
05492         if worker is not None and not self.confirm_the_worker:
05493             # RECONCILIATION (N1 correction 14, F5): the cancel
05494             # primitive of the round enqueued the fault and returned
05495             # **immediately**.  The coordination of the round could then
05496             # reach its selection point **before** the worker had
05497             # published the immutable outcome of its generation: the
05498             # selection point had no published fault and the parallel
05499             # fact of the timeout could not be inferred from a later
05500             # observation of the fault.
05501             #
05502             # The primitive now waits, bounded, for the **immutable
05503             # outcome of that specific generation** before it returns.
05504             # The selection point of the production then really sees the
05505             # published fault of the round.  The wait is bounded, it
05506             # never decides the outcome of the generation and it never
05507             # replaces the error of the round.  The worker of the round
05508             # is held inside its own final close and release tail, so
05509             # the real bounded join of the coordination still FAILS.
05510             #
05511             # N1 correction 15 (M2): the wait is a **checked** barrier
05512             # and it **fails closed**.  The barrier demands the immutable
05513             # outcome of the **exact** generation, the expected kind of
05514             # the ordinary failure and the **exact** original error
05515             # object, and it demands them **before** the cancel
05516             # primitive returns.  A missing outcome, an outcome of
05517             # another generation, another kind of failure and another
05518             # error object are an **explicit refusal** of the premise of
05519             # the test: the primitive never returns ``REQUESTED`` as if
05520             # the barrier had passed and the record of the barrier
05521             # names the refusal.
05522             generation = self.worker_holder.get('generation')
05523             if generation is None:
05524                 generation = worker._submitted
05525             barrier = self.barrier
05526             expected_generation = generation
05527             probe_generation = generation
05528             if self.barrier_probe == 'ABSENT':
05529                 # The negative subcase: the barrier waits for the
05530                 # outcome of a generation that was never published.
05531                 probe_generation = generation + 1000000
05532             elif self.barrier_probe == 'WRONG_GENERATION':
05533                 # The negative subcase: the premise demands the outcome
05534                 # of another generation than the one that the round
05535                 # really published.
05536                 expected_generation = generation + 1
05537             barrier['probe'] = self.barrier_probe
05538             barrier['expected_generation'] = expected_generation
05539             barrier['probe_generation'] = probe_generation
05540             barrier['entered_at'] = time.monotonic()
05541             failure = None
05542             outcome = None
05543             # CORRECTION 18 (M2): the attempt of the barrier is marked
05544             # **here**, in the actual seam of the round and not in the
05545             # phase that waits for it: an assertion of the phase that
05546             # fails after this point may never relabel the barrier as
05547             # ``NOT_RUN`` and it may never overwrite the executed
05548             # barrier with a synthetic one.
05549             if self.barrier_ledger is not None and self.barrier_name:
05550                 self.barrier_ledger.attempt(self.barrier_name)
05551             # CORRECTION 17 (M2): the barrier is **finalised** on the
05552             # successful path **and** on the refused path: the failure,
05553             # the return state and the generation and object facts are
05554             # recorded, the snapshot is persisted and the completion is
05555             # published - always, and never before the facts are there.
05556             try:
05557                 if self.barrier_probe == 'ABSENT':
05558                     outcome = _wait_for_missing_outcome(worker,
05559                                                          probe_generation,
05560                                                          1.0)
05561                 else:
05562                     outcome = _wait_for_outcome(worker, probe_generation, 15.0)
05563                 barrier['observed_at'] = time.monotonic()
05564                 barrier['observed'] = outcome is not None
05565                 barrier['observed_generation'] = (
05566                     outcome.get('generation')
05567                     if outcome is not None else None)
05568                 barrier['observed_error'] = (outcome.get('error')
05569                                              if outcome is not None else None)
05570                 barrier['observed_object'] = (outcome.get('error_object')
05571                                              if outcome is not None else None)
05572                 if outcome is None:
05573                     failure = 'BARRIER_OUTCOME_MISSING'
05574                 elif outcome.get('generation') != expected_generation:
05575                     failure = 'BARRIER_GENERATION_MISMATCH'
05576                 elif outcome.get('error') != 'RuntimeError':
05577                     failure = 'BARRIER_ERROR_KIND'
05578                 elif outcome.get('error_object') is not self.fault_object:
05579                     failure = 'BARRIER_ERROR_OBJECT'
05580                 barrier['failure'] = failure
05581                 self.worker_holder['published_outcome'] = outcome
05582                 if failure is not None:
05583                     # The barrier **fails closed**: the primitive of the
05584                     # round does not return and the premise of the test is
05585                     # refused explicitly.
05586                     barrier['refused'] = failure
05587                     raise _BarrierRefusal(failure)
05588                 barrier['passed'] = True
05589                 barrier['returned'] = 'REQUESTED'
05590             finally:
05591                 # the facts of the barrier are finalised **always**: the
05592                 # return state names the path that was taken, the
05593                 # generation is the one that was demanded, the snapshot
05594                 # is persisted as a real document and the completion is
05595                 # published.
05596                 barrier['left_at'] = time.monotonic()
05597                 barrier['return_state'] = ('REFUSED' if failure is not None
05598                                            else 'PASSED')
05599                 barrier['generation'] = expected_generation
05600                 self.barrier_snapshot = dict(self.barrier)
05601                 # CORRECTION 18 (M2): the durable document of the
05602                 # barrier is written **before** the completion is
05603                 # published: a phase that waits for the completion and
05604                 # that reads the persisted document never reads a
05605                 # snapshot that was not written.
05606                 self._persist_barrier()
05607                 # CORRECTION 20 (M3): the publication of the completion is
05608                 # guarded: a cancellation of it never replaces the
05609                 # original failure and the **exact** object of the
05610                 # cancellation is retained by the owner of the phase.
05611                 _release_gate(self.barrier_completed.set, None, self.interrupts)
05612         if self.confirm_the_worker:
05613             # The coordination waits for the real exit of the worker:
05614             # the bounded join of the coordination is then a confirmed
05615             # one.
05616             if worker is not None:
05617                 worker.join(10.0)
05618         return 'REQUESTED'
05619 
05620     def _persist_barrier(self):
05621         """The **durable** facts of the barrier of this round.
05622 
05623         The facts of the barrier are written as a real phase document:
05624         the identity of the observed object is a **boolean** and the
05625         type and the detail of the failure are readable.  A shallow
05626         in-memory dictionary alone is not execution evidence.
05627 
05628         The failure of the write never replaces the fact of the barrier:
05629         it is recorded and it is visible to the owner of the test.
05630         """
05631         if not self.barrier_document:
05632             return None
05633         snapshot = self.barrier_snapshot or dict(self.barrier)
05634         observed = snapshot.get('observed_object')
05635         document = {
05636             'status': 'EXECUTED',
05637             'barrier': {key: value for key, value in snapshot.items()
05638                         if key != 'observed_object'},
05639             'barrier_observed_object_is_original':
05640                 observed is self.fault_object,
05641             'barrier_observed_object_type': (type(observed).__name__
05642                                              if observed is not None else None),
05643             'barrier_observed_object_detail': (repr(observed)
05644                                                if observed is not None else None),
05645             'barrier_expectation': self.barrier_expectation,
05646             'barrier_probe': self.barrier_probe,
05647             'confirm_the_worker': bool(self.confirm_the_worker),
05648             'hold_the_worker': bool(self.hold_the_worker),
05649             'cancel_calls': self.cancel_calls,
05650             'expected_generation': self.worker_holder.get('generation'),
05651             'published_outcome': bool(
05652                 self.worker_holder.get('published_outcome')),
05653             'barrier_persisted': True,
05654             'state': None,
05655         }
05656         try:
05657             path = write_phase(self.barrier_document, document)
05658         except BaseException as error:  # noqa: BLE001 - it stays visible
05659             self.barrier_write_error = {'type': type(error).__name__,
05660                                         'detail': str(error)}
05661             # CORRECTION 20 (M3): the **exact** object of a cancellation
05662             # that the durable write met is retained: the owner of the
05663             # phase re-raises **it** and it is never converted into an
05664             # ordinary assertion of the evidence.
05665             if isinstance(error, (KeyboardInterrupt, SystemExit)):
05666                 self.barrier_interruption = error
05667             # CORRECTION 19 (M3): the **exact** object of a cancellation
05668             # that the durable write met is retained: it is re-raised by
05669             # the owner of the phase and it is never reduced to a type
05670             # and a detail.
05671             _note_interruption(getattr(self, 'interrupts', None), error)
05672             return None
05673         # CORRECTION 18 (M2): the fact of the durable write is recorded
05674         # and it is **consumed** by the phase: a barrier whose document
05675         # was not written never certifies the barrier.
05676         self.barrier_persisted = True
05677         return path
05678 
05679     def close_handle(self, handle):
05680         with self.lock:
05681             self.close_ledger.append(handle)
05682             self.closed.append(handle)
05683         if self.hold_the_worker:
05684             # The worker is held inside its own release: it is really
05685             # alive and the bounded join of the coordination fails.
05686             self.release_the_worker.wait(20.0)
05687 
05688     def read_stream(self, handle, size):
05689         with self.lock:
05690             if self.frames:
05691                 return self.frames.pop(0)
05692         self.read_waiting.set()
05693         try:
05694             kind, payload = self._outcomes.get(timeout=30.0)
05695         except queue.Empty:
05696             self.eof_observed.set()
05697             return b''
05698         if kind == 'FRAME':
05699             return payload
05700         if kind == 'FAULT':
05701             self.fault_observed.set()
05702             raise payload
05703         self.eof_observed.set()
05704         return b''
05705 
05706     def write_stream(self, handle, data):
05707         with self.lock:
05708             final = self.final
05709             self.final = b''
05710         if final:
05711             self.publish_outcome('FRAME', final)
05712         return len(data)
05713 
05714 
05715 def _m09_timeout_phase(document, plan, tmp_path, *, phase, confirm_the_worker,
05716                         hold_the_worker, barrier_probe='EXACT',
05717                         expect_barrier='PASSED', inventory=None, ledger=None,
05718                         store=None):
05719     """The dynamic schedule of the M09 obligation of this group.
05720 
05721     The schedule is the **production** one: a real ordinary fault is
05722     published for the current generation of the read of the real chain,
05723     the effective timeout of the round arrives **before** the endpoint
05724     consumes the outcome, and the coordination of the round - a
05725     confirmed one or a failed one - preserves the exact original error
05726     object and its code.  The timeout and the failure of the
05727     coordination are **parallel** facts: they never replace the failure
05728     that was really published.
05729 
05730     Every fact of the phase is the fact that the production returned.
05731 
05732     RECONCILIATION (N1 correction 14, F5): the schedule of the **failed**
05733     coordination reached the selection point of the production without a
05734     published fault: the cancel primitive of the round enqueued the
05735     fault of the read and returned immediately, so the coordination
05736     could finish before the worker had published the immutable outcome
05737     of its generation.  The parallel fact of the timeout could then not
05738     be read at all.  The seam of the failed coordination now publishes
05739     the real primitive fault and waits, bounded, for the **immutable
05740     outcome of that specific generation** before the cancel primitive
05741     returns, while the worker of the round is held inside its own
05742     final close and release tail: the real bounded join of the
05743     coordination still FAILS and the coordination error stays a
05744     **parallel** fact of the original error of the round.  The
05745     confirmed coordination and the required order of the schedule
05746     (fault published before the timeout, consumed after it) stay
05747     separate and unchanged.
05748 
05749     N1 correction 15 (M2): the wait of the seam for the outcome of the
05750     generation is a **checked** barrier and it **fails closed**.  The
05751     barrier demands the immutable outcome of the **exact** generation,
05752     the expected kind of the ordinary failure and the **exact** original
05753     error object **before** the cancel primitive returns, and it stores
05754     the checked order of the three facts.  A missing outcome, an outcome
05755     of another generation, another kind of failure and another error
05756     object are an **explicit refusal** of the premise of the test: the
05757     primitive never returns ``REQUESTED`` as if the barrier had passed.
05758     The production catches the ordinary exceptions of the seam, so the
05759     outer test reads the premise from the record of the barrier itself
05760     and it captures that record **before** the main assertions of the
05761     phase.
05762 
05763     The two negative subcases of the barrier (``ABSENT`` and
05764     ``WRONG_GENERATION``) drive the **same** failed coordination with a
05765     bounded transparent seam that forces an absent outcome and an
05766     outcome of another generation.  Their premise is **refused** and
05767     they are never counted as a successful failed coordination.
05768 
05769     The **confirmed** coordination does not use the barrier at all: it
05770     waits for the real exit of the worker and the record of the barrier
05771     says that it never ran.  The expectation of the phase names the
05772     three cases: ``PASSED`` for the failed coordination, ``REFUSED`` for
05773     the two negative subcases and ``NOT_APPLICABLE`` for the confirmed
05774     coordination.
05775 
05776     N1 correction 17 (M2): the construction of the chain is **inside**
05777     the guard of the phase, the barrier of the publication is waited
05778     for **after** the cause of the coordination - never before it - and
05779     the facts of the barrier are read from the **persisted** snapshot of
05780     the seam.  A phase that was **executed** and that failed is a
05781     **FAIL** with its raw facts; only a phase that was never entered is
05782     a ``NOT_RUN``.  The result of the phase is written **last**, after
05783     the two real stops and after the cleanup of the phase.
05784     """
05785     clock = ManualClock(1000.0)
05786     api = _M09TimeoutApi(confirm_the_worker=confirm_the_worker,
05787                           hold_the_worker=hold_the_worker)
05788     api.barrier_probe = barrier_probe
05789     api.barrier_document = phase + '-barrier'
05790     api.barrier_expectation = expect_barrier
05791     # CORRECTION 18 (M2): the seam of the round knows the ledger of the
05792     # phase and the name of the barrier of this phase: the attempt of
05793     # the barrier is marked **in the seam** and never only in the phase
05794     # that waits for it.
05795     api.barrier_ledger = ledger
05796     api.barrier_name = phase + '-barrier'
05797     # CORRECTION 17 (M3): the owners of the round of this phase are
05798     # registered by the observer of the starts: the observation is armed
05799     # for the **whole** phase, the construction of the chain included,
05800     # and it is disarmed only after the salvage of the phase.
05801     registry = _OwnerRegistry()
05802     observer = _StartObserver(registry)
05803     fault = RuntimeError('read_fault')
05804     api.fault = fault
05805     api.fault_object = fault
05806     session = None
05807     holder = None
05808     channel_closes = None
05809     channel_facts = None
05810     worker = None
05811     generation = None
05812     written = set()
05813     # CORRECTION 20 (M2): the **shared** fact store of the group: it
05814     # holds the **last actual** facts of every document and it is the
05815     # store that the outer recovery reads.  A phase that could not
05816     # persist its own terminal document never loses its facts.
05817     raw = store if store is not None else {}
05818     # CORRECTION 18 (M2/M3): the errors of this phase are **lists** and
05819     # they are never overwritten: a cleanup error is never replaced by a
05820     # later one and the first error is never the only one.
05821     cleanup_errors = []
05822     write_errors = []
05823     # CORRECTION 18 (M3): the **exact** objects of the interruptions
05824     # that the cleanup of this phase met.
05825     interrupts = []
05826     # CORRECTION 19 (M3): the seam of the round retains the **exact**
05827     # object of an interruption that its durable write met: the
05828     # cancellation of the cleanup of the phase is never reduced to a
05829     # type and a detail.
05830     api.interrupts = interrupts
05831     # CORRECTION 18 (M2): the **original** failure of this phase.
05832     original_failure = {'type': None, 'detail': None}
05833     names = (phase, phase + '-construction', phase + '-barrier',
05834              phase + '-first', phase + '-second')
05835 
05836     def record(name, facts):
05837         """Write one document of the phase and mark it as **written**.
05838 
05839         CORRECTION 17 (M2): the document is **persisted first** and it
05840         is marked only after the persistence succeeded: a document that
05841         the inventory of the outer test names really exists on the disk.
05842         """
05843         path = write_phase(name, facts)
05844         written.add(name)
05845         if inventory is not None:
05846             inventory.add(name)
05847         if ledger is not None:
05848             ledger.attempt(name)
05849         return path
05850 
05851     def record_guarded(name, facts):
05852         """Write one document and **never** replace an original failure.
05853 
05854         CORRECTION 18 (M2): the writing of the evidence of a phase that
05855         is already failing must never replace the original failure: the
05856         error of the write is retained in ``write_errors`` and the phase
05857         fails **closed**.
05858         """
05859         try:
05860             return record(name, facts)
05861         except BaseException as error:  # noqa: BLE001 - it stays visible
05862             write_errors.append({'name': name,
05863                                  'type': type(error).__name__,
05864                                  'detail': str(error)})
05865             _note_interruption(interrupts, error)
05866             return None
05867 
05868     def barrier_identity():
05869         """The durable identity facts of the barrier of this phase.
05870 
05871         CORRECTION 18 (M2): every rewrite of the barrier document keeps
05872         the **boolean** of the identity of the original error object: the
05873         repr of an object never carries its identity.
05874         """
