05875         snapshot = api.barrier_snapshot or {}
05876         observed = snapshot.get('observed_object')
05877         return {
05878             'barrier_observed_object_is_original':
05879                 observed is api.fault_object,
05880             'barrier_observed_object_type': (type(observed).__name__
05881                                              if observed is not None else None),
05882             'barrier_observed_object_detail': (repr(observed)
05883                                                if observed is not None else None),
05884         }
05885 
05886     if ledger is not None:
05887         for name in names:
05888             ledger.plan(name)
05889         ledger.attempt(phase + '-construction')
05890         # CORRECTION 18 (M2): the overall phase is attempted **before**
05891         # the construction of the chain: a construction that failed half
05892         # way is an attempted construction **and** an attempted phase -
05893         # the phase is never ``NOT_RUN`` when its construction ran.
05894         ledger.attempt(phase)
05895     observer_patch = observer.install()
05896     observer_patch.start()
05897     observer.arm()
05898     try:
05899         try:
05900             # CORRECTION 17 (M2): the construction of the chain is
05901             # **inside** the guard: a construction that failed half way
05902             # is an attempted construction with a durable document.
05903             session, holder, channel_closes, channel_facts = _chain(
05904                 api, document, plan, tmp_path, clock=clock)
05905             # CORRECTION 18 (M2): a **successful** construction has its
05906             # own terminal document: the facts are written first and the
05907             # ``PASSED`` document is written only after them and the
05908             # ledger of the phase is updated only after that.
05909             construction_facts = {'construction': 'COMPLETED',
05910                                   'session_created': session is not None,
05911                                   'holder_keys': sorted(holder),
05912                                   'state': getattr(session, 'state', None)}
05913             raw[phase + '-construction'] = dict(construction_facts)
05914             record(phase + '-construction',
05915                    dict(construction_facts, status='EXECUTED'))
05916             record(phase + '-construction',
05917                    dict(construction_facts, status='PASSED'))
05918             if ledger is not None:
05919                 ledger.pass_(phase + '-construction')
05920             session.start()
05921             # The reader of the chain is really inside its read and the
05922             # generation of that read is the fact of the worker itself: it
05923             # is never a number that the group hard codes.
05924             assert api.read_waiting.wait(15.0), \
05925                 'the reader of the chain was never inside its read'
05926             worker = holder['read_endpoint']._reader
05927             assert worker is not None, 'the reader of the chain never started'
05928             api.worker_holder['worker'] = worker
05929             generation = worker._submitted
05930             assert generation >= 1, 'the reader of the chain never submitted'
05931             # The **specific** generation of the read that is in flight is
05932             # given to the seam of the primitive: the outcome that the
05933             # coordination waits for is the outcome of **that** generation
05934             # and never a generic event of the worker.
05935             api.worker_holder['generation'] = generation
05936             # The effective timeout of the round is tightened while the
05937             # read is really in flight: the absolute lease of the round
05938             # stays in the future and only the bound of the channel
05939             # expires.  **This** is the cause of the coordination of the
05940             # round and therefore of the barrier of the publication.
05941             holder['channel'].bind_deadline(clock.value + 0.5)
05942             clock.advance(1.0)
05943             # The fault of the read is published by the coordination of the
05944             # round and the endpoint finds it before it consumes it.
05945             assert api.fault_observed.wait(15.0), \
05946                 'the ordinary failure of the read never happened'
05947             assert worker.outcome_published.wait(15.0), \
05948                 'the outcome of the read was never published'
05949             outcome = worker.outcome(generation)
05950             assert outcome is not None, 'the outcome of the generation is gone'
05951             assert outcome['generation'] == generation, \
05952                 'the outcome of another generation was read'
05953             assert outcome['error'] == 'RuntimeError', \
05954                 'the published failure of the round is not the ordinary one'
05955             assert outcome['error_object'] is fault, \
05956                 'the outcome of the generation does not keep the original error'
05957             # The publication of the outcome of the worker is **not** the
05958             # consumption of it by the endpoint: the endpoint attaches the
05959             # timeout to the original error object when it raises that
05960             # object, and the publication happens before it.  The group
05961             # waits for the **real** termination of the reader of the
05962             # channel: that is the barrier of the consumption and it is
05963             # never a sleep that proves it.
05964             reader_thread = getattr(holder['channel'], '_thread', None)
05965             assert reader_thread is not None, \
05966                 'the reader thread of the channel does not exist'
05967             reader_thread.join(15.0)
05968             assert not reader_thread.is_alive(), \
05969                 'the reader of the channel did not consume the outcome'
05970             # N1 correction 17 (M2): the barrier of the publication is
05971             # **completed** by the seam at its finalization, and the
05972             # finalization happened **after** the cause above.  The
05973             # phase waits for the completion - a bounded wait that never
05974             # precedes its cause - and it reads the **persisted** snapshot
05975             # of the seam.  The seam wrote the document durably at its
05976             # finalization, on the successful path and on the refused
05977             # path.
05978             # The phase attempts the barrier as well: the **seam** is the
05979             # authority of the attempt and this call is idempotent.
05980             if ledger is not None:
05981                 ledger.attempt(phase + '-barrier')
05982             if expect_barrier in ('PASSED', 'REFUSED'):
05983                 if not api.barrier_completed.wait(15.0):
05984                     barrier_failed_facts = dict(
05985                         barrier_identity(),
05986                         status='FAIL',
05987                         executed=True,
05988                         premise='BARRIER_NOT_COMPLETED',
05989                         timeout_seconds=15.0,
05990                         barrier_completed=False,
05991                         barrier_expectation=expect_barrier,
05992                         barrier_probe=barrier_probe,
05993                         state=session.state)
05994                     # CORRECTION 20 (M2): the **full intended** facts of
05995                     # the barrier are stored **before** the terminal write
05996                     # is attempted: a write that fails never erases them.
05997                     raw[phase + '-barrier'] = dict(barrier_failed_facts)
05998                     written_result = record_guarded(phase + '-barrier',
05999                                                     barrier_failed_facts)
06000                     # CORRECTION 19 (M2): the terminal state of the ledger
06001                     # follows the **successful** terminal write: a failed
06002                     # write leaves the barrier ATTEMPTED and the outer
06003                     # inventory recovers it, best effort and bounded.
06004                     if ledger is not None and written_result is not None:
06005                         ledger.fail(phase + '-barrier', 'BARRIER_NOT_COMPLETED')
06006                     # CORRECTION 20 (M3): the **first** exact pending
06007                     # interruption of the round wins over the ordinary
06008                     # assertion of the evidence.
06009                     if interrupts:
06010                         raise interrupts[0]
06011                     raise AssertionError(
06012                         'the publication barrier of the round never completed')
06013                 # CORRECTION 18 (M2): the error of the durable write of
06014                 # the barrier is **consumed** here: a barrier whose
06015                 # document was not written never certifies the barrier
06016                 # and it never leaves the evidence of the phase as if it
06017                 # had passed.
06018                 if api.barrier_write_error is not None:
06019                     barrier_failed_facts = dict(
06020                         barrier_identity(),
06021                         status='FAIL',
06022                         executed=True,
06023                         premise='BARRIER_DOCUMENT_NOT_WRITTEN',
06024                         barrier_write_error=api.barrier_write_error,
06025                         barrier_persisted=bool(api.barrier_persisted),
06026                         barrier_completed=True,
06027                         barrier_expectation=expect_barrier,
06028                         barrier_probe=barrier_probe,
06029                         state=session.state)
06030                     # CORRECTION 20 (M2): the **full intended** facts of
06031                     # the barrier are stored **before** the terminal write
06032                     # is attempted: a write that fails never erases them
06033                     # and the outer recovery reuses them.
06034                     raw[phase + '-barrier'] = dict(barrier_failed_facts)
06035                     written_result = record_guarded(phase + '-barrier',
06036                                                     barrier_failed_facts)
06037                     # CORRECTION 19 (M2): the terminal state of the ledger
06038                     # follows the **successful** terminal write.
06039                     if ledger is not None and written_result is not None:
06040                         ledger.fail(phase + '-barrier',
06041                                     'BARRIER_DOCUMENT_NOT_WRITTEN')
06042                     # CORRECTION 20 (M3): the **exact** cancellation of
06043                     # the durable write of the barrier is never converted
06044                     # into an ordinary assertion: it is raised as
06045                     # **itself**, so that the priority of the phase gives
06046                     # it the place of the **first** exact pending
06047                     # interruption of the cleanup and it is never
06048                     # suppressed by a synthesised assertion.
06049                     if api.barrier_interruption is not None:
06050                         raise api.barrier_interruption
06051                     raise AssertionError(
06052                         'the durable barrier document of the round was not '
06053                         'written: %s' % (api.barrier_write_error,))
06054                 barrier = dict(api.barrier_snapshot or {})
06055                 raw[phase + '-barrier'] = dict(
06056                     barrier_identity(),
06057                     barrier=barrier,
06058                     barrier_expectation=expect_barrier,
06059                     barrier_probe=barrier_probe,
06060                     barrier_completed=True,
06061                     barrier_persisted=bool(api.barrier_persisted),
06062                     barrier_return_state=barrier.get('return_state'),
06063                     confirm_the_worker=bool(confirm_the_worker),
06064                     hold_the_worker=bool(hold_the_worker),
06065                     cancel_calls=api.cancel_calls,
06066                     expected_generation=api.worker_holder.get('generation'),
06067                     published_outcome=bool(
06068                         api.worker_holder.get('published_outcome')),
06069                     state=session.state)
06070             else:
06071                 # The confirmed coordination does not use the barrier: the
06072                 # document is written immediately with the empty snapshot
06073                 # and **no wait** is performed.
06074                 barrier = dict(api.barrier)
06075                 raw[phase + '-barrier'] = dict(
06076                     barrier_identity(),
06077                     barrier=barrier,
06078                     barrier_expectation=expect_barrier,
06079                     barrier_probe=barrier_probe,
06080                     barrier_completed=False,
06081                     barrier_persisted=bool(api.barrier_persisted),
06082                     barrier_return_state=barrier.get('return_state'),
06083                     confirm_the_worker=bool(confirm_the_worker),
06084                     hold_the_worker=bool(hold_the_worker),
06085                     cancel_calls=api.cancel_calls,
06086                     expected_generation=api.worker_holder.get('generation'),
06087                     published_outcome=bool(
06088                         api.worker_holder.get('published_outcome')),
06089                     state=session.state)
06090                 record(phase + '-barrier',
06091                        dict(raw[phase + '-barrier'], status='EXECUTED'))
06092             if expect_barrier == 'PASSED':
06093                 assert barrier['failure'] is None, \
06094                     'the publication barrier of the round failed: %s' \
06095                     % barrier['failure']
06096                 assert barrier['passed'] is True, \
06097                     'the publication barrier of the round did not pass'
06098                 assert barrier['returned'] == 'REQUESTED', \
06099                     'the cancel primitive did not return after the barrier'
06100                 assert barrier['observed_generation'] == generation, \
06101                     'the barrier observed the outcome of another generation'
06102                 assert barrier['observed_error'] == 'RuntimeError', \
06103                     'the barrier did not observe the ordinary failure'
06104                 assert barrier['observed_object'] is fault, \
06105                     'the barrier did not observe the original error object'
06106                 # CORRECTION 18 (M2): the **durable** document of the
06107                 # barrier keeps the identity of the original error object
06108                 # as a **boolean**: the repr of an object never carries
06109                 # its identity.
06110                 assert raw[phase + '-barrier'][
06111                     'barrier_observed_object_is_original'] is True, \
06112                     'the durable document of the barrier does not keep the ' \
06113                     'identity of the original error object'
06114                 assert barrier['entered_at'] <= barrier['observed_at'] \
06115                     <= barrier['left_at'], \
06116                     'the order of the barrier of the publication was not checked'
06117                 assert barrier['return_state'] == 'PASSED', \
06118                     'the barrier of the publication did not finalise its state'
06119             elif expect_barrier == 'REFUSED':
06120                 # The negative subcase: the barrier **failed closed** and
06121                 # the premise of the test is refused explicitly.  It is
06122                 # never counted as a successful failed coordination.
06123                 assert barrier['failure'] is not None, \
06124                     'the publication barrier of the round did not fail closed'
06125                 assert barrier['passed'] is False, \
06126                     'the publication barrier of the round reported a success'
06127                 assert barrier['returned'] is None, \
06128                     'the cancel primitive returned as if the barrier had passed'
06129                 assert barrier['refused'] == barrier['failure'], \
06130                     'the refusal of the barrier of the publication was not recorded'
06131                 assert barrier['return_state'] == 'REFUSED', \
06132                     'the refused barrier of the publication did not finalise ' \
06133                     'its state'
06134             else:
06135                 # The **confirmed** coordination: the coordination of the
06136                 # round waits for the real exit of the worker and the
06137                 # barrier of the publication is **not** used by it.  The
06138                 # record of the barrier says so and it is never read as a
06139                 # premise that the schedule did not establish.
06140                 assert barrier['entered_at'] is None, \
06141                     'the barrier of the publication ran in a schedule that ' \
06142                     'does not use it'
06143                 assert barrier['passed'] is False, \
06144                     'the barrier of the publication reported a success in a ' \
06145                     'schedule that does not use it'
06146             # CORRECTION 18 (M2): the durable document of the barrier is
06147             # written **before** the terminal state of the ledger is
06148             # updated.
06149             record(phase + '-barrier',
06150                    dict(raw[phase + '-barrier'], status='PASSED'))
06151             if ledger is not None:
06152                 ledger.pass_(phase + '-barrier')
06153             # The endpoint raised the **original** error object and the
06154             # timeout is a **parallel** fact of it.
06155             raised = outcome['error_object']
06156             parallel = getattr(raised, 'lvw_parallel_timeout', None)
06157             assert isinstance(parallel, dict), \
06158                 'the timeout did not stay a parallel fact of the failure'
06159             assert parallel.get('code') == 'BROKER_PIPE_TIMEOUT'
06160             assert parallel.get('generation') == generation
06161             coordination = parallel.get('coordination_error')
06162             if confirm_the_worker:
06163                 # The coordination of the round **succeeded**: the worker
06164                 # really ended and the failure of the coordination is not
06165                 # a fact of the round.
06166                 assert coordination is None, \
06167                     'a confirmed coordination was named as a failed one'
06168             else:
06169                 # The coordination of the round **failed** and the failure
06170                 # is a parallel fact: the original error of the round is
06171                 # still the error that leaves.
06172                 assert coordination == 'BrokerError', \
06173                     'the failed coordination was not named as a parallel fact'
06174             raw[phase] = {'generation': generation,
06175                           'outcome_error': outcome['error'],
06176                           'outcome_object': type(outcome['error_object']).__name__,
06177                           'parallel_timeout': parallel,
06178                           'cancel_calls': api.cancel_calls,
06179                           'published_before_cancel_return': bool(
06180                               api.worker_holder.get('published_outcome')),
06181                           'barrier': barrier,
06182                           'barrier_expectation': expect_barrier,
06183                           'state': session.state}
06184             record(phase, dict(raw[phase], status='EXECUTED'))
06185             # The error of the reader of the channel is the error of the
06186             # round: the history of the round keeps it and the two public
06187             # stops of the round keep it.
06188             if ledger is not None:
06189                 ledger.attempt(phase + '-first')
06190             first = session.stop()
06191             first_facts = first['broker_close_facts'] or {}
06192             raw[phase + '-first'] = {
06193                 'reader_error': first_facts.get('channel_reader_error'),
06194                 'state': first['state'],
06195                 'retained': first['broker_retained']}
06196             record(phase + '-first',
06197                    dict(raw[phase + '-first'], status='EXECUTED'))
06198             assert first_facts.get('channel_reader_error') == 'RuntimeError', \
06199                 'the real fault of the round was replaced by a cancellation'
06200             assert first['broker_retained'] is True
06201             assert first['state'] == 'UNKNOWN_RETAINED'
06202             # CORRECTION 18 (M2): the durable document is written
06203             # **before** the terminal state of the ledger is updated.
06204             record(phase + '-first',
06205                    dict(raw[phase + '-first'], status='PASSED'))
06206             if ledger is not None:
06207                 ledger.pass_(phase + '-first')
06208             if ledger is not None:
06209                 ledger.attempt(phase + '-second')
06210             second = session.stop()
06211             second_facts = second['broker_close_facts'] or {}
06212             raw[phase + '-second'] = {
06213                 'reader_error': second_facts.get('channel_reader_error'),
06214                 'source': second_facts.get('channel_reader_error_source'),
06215                 'state': second['state']}
06216             record(phase + '-second',
06217                    dict(raw[phase + '-second'], status='EXECUTED'))
06218             assert second_facts.get('channel_reader_error') == 'RuntimeError', \
06219                 'the second attempt lost the error of the reader'
06220             assert second_facts.get('channel_reader_error_source') \
06221                 == 'REPEATED_CLOSE'
06222             assert second['state'] == 'UNKNOWN_RETAINED'
06223             # CORRECTION 18 (M2): the durable document is written
06224             # **before** the terminal state of the ledger is updated.
06225             record(phase + '-second',
06226                    dict(raw[phase + '-second'], status='PASSED'))
06227             if ledger is not None:
06228                 ledger.pass_(phase + '-second')
06229         except BaseException as error:
06230             # N1 correction 17 (M2): a phase that was **executed** and
06231             # that failed is a **FAIL** with the facts that it really
06232             # observed.  Only a phase that was **never entered** is left
06233             # to the inventory of the outer test, which writes it as
06234             # ``NOT_RUN`` without a fact of the phase.
06235             #
06236             # CORRECTION 18 (M2): the **original** failure is retained
06237             # and it is the one that leaves the phase.  The result
06238             # document of the phase is written **after** the cleanup and
06239             # it carries the original failure **and** the diagnostics of
06240             # the cleanup.  The writing of the evidence never replaces
06241             # the original failure: it is recorded and the phase fails
06242             # **closed**.
06243             original_failure['type'] = type(error).__name__
06244             original_failure['detail'] = str(error)
06245             for name in names:
06246                 if name == phase:
06247                     # the result of the phase is written after the
06248                     # cleanup of the phase
06249                     continue
06250                 if ledger is not None:
06251                     state = ledger.state(name)
06252                 else:
06253                     state = 'ATTEMPTED' if name in written else 'PLANNED'
06254                 if state != 'ATTEMPTED':
06255                     continue
06256                 # the facts that the phase really observed are kept: the
06257                 # barrier of the publication stays in the document of the
06258                 # phase that failed
06259                 observed = dict(raw.get(name, {}))
06260                 if name == phase + '-construction':
06261                     observed.setdefault('construction', 'FAILED')
06262                 if name.endswith('-barrier'):
06263                     if api.barrier_snapshot:
06264                         observed.setdefault('barrier',
06265                                             dict(api.barrier_snapshot))
06266                     observed.update(barrier_identity())
06267                     # CORRECTION 19 (M2): the rewrite of the barrier
06268                     # document keeps the **durable** facts of the
06269                     # barrier: the fact of its persistence and the error
06270                     # of its persistence.  An assertion of the phase that
06271                     # fails after the seam ran never loses the very
06272                     # evidence fault that the contract requires.
06273                     observed['barrier_persisted'] = bool(api.barrier_persisted)
06274                     observed['barrier_write_error'] = api.barrier_write_error
06275                 failed_facts = dict(observed,
06276                                     status='FAIL',
06277                                     executed=True,
06278                                     reason=type(error).__name__,
06279                                     detail=str(error))
06280                 # CORRECTION 20 (M2): the **full intended** facts of the
06281                 # document are stored **before** the terminal write is
06282                 # attempted: a write that fails leaves the name
06283                 # ATTEMPTED and the outer recovery reuses these facts
06284                 # instead of a generic ``FAIL``.
06285                 raw[name] = dict(failed_facts)
06286                 written_result = record_guarded(name, failed_facts)
06287                 # CORRECTION 19 (M2): the terminal state of the ledger
06288                 # follows the **successful** terminal write: a failed
06289                 # write leaves the phase ATTEMPTED and the outer
06290                 # inventory recovers it, best effort and bounded.
06291                 if ledger is not None and written_result is not None:
06292                     ledger.fail(name, type(error).__name__)
06293             raise
06294         finally:
06295             # The teardown of the owner of the test is bounded and it is
06296             # unconditional: the gate is released, the read of the round
06297             # is ended, the production wait of the worker is restored and
06298             # the worker is stopped and joined.
06299             #
06300             # CORRECTION 18 (M2/M3): every step of the teardown is
06301             # guarded and every error is **retained** in a list: one
06302             # exception never skips the owners that follow it and it
06303             # never skips the result document of the phase.  The join and
06304             # the query of the worker happen **before** the drain and
06305             # they are guarded themselves.
06306             # CORRECTION 20 (M3): the **initial** release of the round is
06307             # guarded **on its own**: a cancellation of it never skips the
06308             # stop, the join, the drain, the proof and the evidence that
06309             # follow it and it never replaces the failure that is already
06310             # unwinding.
06311             _release_gate(api.release_the_worker.set, cleanup_errors,
06312                           interrupts)
06313             if session is not None:
06314                 try:
06315                     session.stop()
06316                 except BaseException as error:  # noqa: BLE001
06317                     cleanup_errors.append({'kind': 'STOP',
06318                                            'type': type(error).__name__,
06319                                            'detail': str(error)})
06320                     _note_interruption(interrupts, error)
06321             if worker is not None:
06322                 try:
06323                     worker.join_bounded(10.0)
06324                 except BaseException as error:  # noqa: BLE001
06325                     cleanup_errors.append({'kind': 'WORKER_JOIN',
06326                                            'type': type(error).__name__,
06327                                            'detail': str(error)})
06328                     _note_interruption(interrupts, error)
06329                 try:
06330                     if worker.is_alive():
06331                         cleanup_errors.append(
06332                             {'kind': 'WORKER', 'type': 'AssertionError',
06333                              'detail': 'the worker of the read was left '
06334                                        'behind'})
06335                 except BaseException as error:  # noqa: BLE001
06336                     cleanup_errors.append({'kind': 'WORKER_LIVENESS',
06337                                            'type': type(error).__name__,
06338                                            'detail': str(error)})
06339                     _note_interruption(interrupts, error)
06340             # CORRECTION 17 (M3): the owners of the round are drained
06341             # **boundedly** and the quiescence is asserted **after** the
06342             # drain: an owner that is still alive fails the proof of the
06343             # phase and every owner is still attempted.
06344             drain = {'salvage': [], 'attempts': [], 'proof': None,
06345                      'quiescent': False, 'errors': [], 'all_salvage': []}
06346             try:
06347                 drain = _drain_owners(
06348                     registry, session=session,
06349                     channel=(holder or {}).get('channel'), prefix=phase,
06350                     release=lambda: api.release_the_worker.set(),
06351                     interrupts=interrupts)
06352             except BaseException as error:  # noqa: BLE001 - it stays visible
06353                 cleanup_errors.append({'kind': 'DRAIN',
06354                                        'type': type(error).__name__,
06355                                        'detail': str(error)})
06356                 _note_interruption(interrupts, error)
06357             # CORRECTION 18 (M2): the result of the phase is written
06358             # **after** the cleanup and it carries the original failure
06359             # **and** every diagnostic of the cleanup.  The phase is
06360             # ``PASSED`` only when the proof held, the round was
06361             # quiescent and no unexpected cleanup or evidence error
06362             # happened.
06363             cleanup_detail = _cleanup_detail(cleanup_errors, write_errors)
06364             # CORRECTION 19 (M2): the result of the phase is decided
06365             # **after** the cleanup and it is written **before** the
06366             # terminal state of the ledger is advanced.
06367             clean = (original_failure['type'] is None
06368                      and cleanup_detail is None
06369                      and drain['proof'] is None
06370                      and drain['quiescent'] is True)
06371             phase_failure_detail = None
06372             # CORRECTION 19 (M2): the durable facts of the barrier are
06373             # part of **every** final document of the phase: the
06374             # snapshot, the rewrite and the final phase keep the fact of
06375             # the persistence of the barrier, the error of its
06376             # persistence and the identity of the original object.
06377             barrier_facts = dict(barrier_identity())
06378             barrier_facts['barrier_persisted'] = bool(api.barrier_persisted)
06379             barrier_facts['barrier_write_error'] = api.barrier_write_error
06380             if clean:
06381                 final_facts = dict(raw.get(phase, {}), status='PASSED',
06382                                    cleanup_errors=[],
06383                                    evidence_write_errors=[],
06384                                    proof_failure=None,
06385                                    quiescent=True,
06386                                    drain=drain['attempts'],
06387                                    interrupts=_interruption_facts(interrupts),
06388                                    **barrier_facts)
06389                 # CORRECTION 20 (M2): the **full intended** facts of the
06390                 # phase are stored **before** the terminal persistence is
06391                 # attempted: a write that fails leaves the phase
06392                 # ATTEMPTED and the outer recovery reuses these facts.
06393                 raw[phase] = dict(final_facts)
06394                 written_result = record_guarded(phase, final_facts)
06395                 if written_result is None:
06396                     # the evidence of the phase could not be written:
06397                     # the phase fails **closed** and it is never
06398                     # reported as a success.  Its terminal state of the
06399                     # ledger is **not** advanced: the outer inventory
06400                     # recovers it, best effort and bounded.
06401                     phase_failure_detail = (
06402                         'the result document of the phase could not be '
06403                         'written: %s' % (write_errors,))
06404                     # CORRECTION 20 (M2): the store keeps the diagnostic
06405                     # of the failed persistence: the retry of the outer
06406                     # recovery carries it and it is never reduced to a
06407                     # generic ``FAIL``.
06408                     raw[phase] = dict(final_facts,
06409                                       status='FAIL',
06410                                       evidence_write_errors=list(
06411                                           write_errors),
06412                                       detail=_join_details(
06413                                           final_facts.get('detail'),
06414                                           phase_failure_detail))
06415                 elif ledger is not None:
06416                     ledger.pass_(phase)
06417             else:
06418                 reason = (original_failure['type'] or 'CleanupError')
06419                 detail = _join_details(original_failure['detail'],
06420                                        cleanup_detail, drain['proof'])
06421                 if drain['quiescent'] is not True:
06422                     detail = _join_details(
06423                         detail, 'the owners of the phase were not quiescent')
06424                     if original_failure['type'] is None:
06425                         reason = 'NOT_QUIESCENT'
06426                 failed_phase_facts = dict(raw.get(phase, {}),
06427                                           status='FAIL',
06428                                           executed=True,
06429                                           reason=reason,
06430                                           detail=detail,
06431                                           original_failure=dict(
06432                                               original_failure),
06433                                           cleanup_errors=list(cleanup_errors),
06434                                           evidence_write_errors=list(
06435                                               write_errors),
06436                                           proof_failure=drain['proof'],
06437                                           quiescent=drain['quiescent'],
06438                                           drain=drain['attempts'],
06439                                           interrupts=_interruption_facts(
06440                                               interrupts),
06441                                           **barrier_facts)
06442                 # CORRECTION 20 (M2): the **full intended** facts of the
06443                 # phase are stored **before** the terminal persistence is
06444                 # attempted.
06445                 raw[phase] = dict(failed_phase_facts)
06446                 written_result = record_guarded(phase, failed_phase_facts)
06447                 # CORRECTION 19 (M2): the terminal state of the ledger
06448                 # follows the **successful** terminal write: a failed
06449                 # write leaves the phase ATTEMPTED and the outer
06450                 # inventory recovers it, best effort and bounded.
06451                 if written_result is not None:
06452                     if ledger is not None:
06453                         ledger.fail(phase, reason)
06454                 else:
06455                     phase_failure_detail = (
06456                         'the durable FAIL of the phase was not written: %s'
06457                         % (write_errors,))
06458                     cleanup_detail = _join_details(cleanup_detail,
06459                                                    phase_failure_detail)
06460                     # CORRECTION 20 (M2): the store keeps the diagnostic
06461                     # of the failed persistence of the ``FAIL``.
06462                     raw[phase] = dict(failed_phase_facts,
06463                                       evidence_write_errors=list(write_errors),
06464                                       detail=_join_details(
06465                                           failed_phase_facts.get('detail'),
06466                                           phase_failure_detail))
06467                 if original_failure['type'] is None:
06468                     # CORRECTION 19 (M2): the diagnostic of a failed
06469                     # write is **joined** with the diagnostic of the
06470                     # phase: neither of them replaces the other.
06471                     phase_failure_detail = _join_details(
06472                         phase_failure_detail,
06473                         'the phase %s failed: %s' % (phase, detail))
06474             # CORRECTION 19 (M2/M3): the priority of the failures is
06475             # fixed: the failure that is already unwinding wins, then
06476             # the **exact** interruption of the cleanup and only then the
06477             # ordinary assertion of the proof or of the evidence.  An
06478             # exact interruption is never replaced by a proof assertion
06479             # and the rethrow of it is never unreachable.
06480             if interrupts and sys.exc_info()[0] is None:
06481                 raise interrupts[0]
06482             # CORRECTION 19 (M2): a durable FAIL of the phase never
06483             # returns a successful test: the phase fails **with** its
06484             # own document.
06485             if phase_failure_detail is not None \
06486                     and sys.exc_info()[0] is None:
06487                 raise AssertionError(phase_failure_detail)
06488             if cleanup_detail is not None and sys.exc_info()[0] is None:
06489                 raise AssertionError(cleanup_detail)
06490             if drain['proof'] is not None and sys.exc_info()[0] is None:
06491                 # the owners of the round were not released: the proof of
06492                 # the cleanup fails truthfully and the phase fails with it
06493                 raise AssertionError(drain['proof'])
06494     finally:
06495         observer.disarm()
06496         observer_patch.stop()
06497 class _M09Gate:
06498     """The transparent gate of the wait of the worker.
06499 
06500     The gate is a **wrapper** of the production wait of the worker: it
06501     calls the **original** wait and it holds the endpoint **after** that
06502     wait returned and **before** the endpoint rechecks or consumes the
06503     outcome of its own generation.
06504 
06505     The gate never decides the outcome, never rewrites the history of
06506     the error, never touches the selection of the error of the endpoint
06507     and never returns a fact of its own: it only decides **when** the
06508     endpoint continues.  Every value that the node asserts afterwards is
06509     a value that the production returned.
06510 
06511     The gate holds **one named worker** and it holds it only while the
06512     read of the round is really in flight: the frames of the start of
06513     the round are never held and the write of the round is never held.
06514     """
06515 
06516     def __init__(self, api):
06517         self.api = api
06518         self.entered = threading.Event()
06519         self.release = threading.Event()
06520         self.calls = []
06521         self.target = None
06522         self.armed = True
06523 
06524     def install(self, worker_class):
06525         """Wrap the production wait of the worker class."""
06526         original = worker_class.wait_done
06527         gate = self
06528 
06529         def gated_wait_done(worker, generation, timeout):
06530             result = original(worker, generation, timeout)
06531             gate.calls.append({'generation': generation,
06532                                'timeout': timeout,
06533                                'result': bool(result)})
06534             if (gate.armed and not result and worker is gate.target
06535                     and gate.api.read_waiting.is_set()):
06536                 # The endpoint is held **after** the production wait and
06537                 # **before** it rechecks its generation.
06538                 gate.entered.set()
06539                 gate.release.wait(30.0)
06540             return result
06541 
06542         worker_class.wait_done = gated_wait_done
06543         return original
06544 
06545 
06546 def _wait_for_outcome(worker, generation, seconds):
06547     """The immutable outcome of **that** generation of the worker.
06548 
06549     The generic event of the worker may be stale: the event is only a
06550     wake up and the **outcome of the named generation** is the fact that
06551     the wait is really about.
06552     """
06553     deadline = time.monotonic() + seconds
06554     while time.monotonic() < deadline:
06555         outcome = worker.outcome(generation)
06556         if outcome is not None and outcome.get('error') is not None:
06557             return outcome
06558         worker.outcome_published.wait(0.05)
06559     return None
06560 
06561 
06562 def _wait_for_missing_outcome(worker, generation, seconds):
06563     """Wait, bounded, for the outcome of a generation that never existed.
06564 
06565     The wait is the **negative** of the barrier of the publication: the
06566     outcome of that generation is never published, the wait ends with
06567     ``None`` and it never decides an outcome, never writes one and
06568     never replaces the error of the round.
06569     """
06570     deadline = time.monotonic() + seconds
06571     while time.monotonic() < deadline:
06572         outcome = worker.outcome(generation)
06573         if outcome is not None:
06574             return outcome
06575         time.sleep(0.005)
06576     return None
06577 
06578 
06579 def _m09_published_fault_phase(document, plan, tmp_path, *, phase,
06580                                inventory=None, ledger=None, store=None):
06581     """The schedule that the contract requires: the fault comes first.
06582 
06583     The ordinary failure of the read is published **before** the
06584     effective timeout of the round arrives and the endpoint consumes the
06585     outcome of its generation only **after** the timeout was decided.
06586 
06587     The order that is driven and recorded is therefore
06588 
06589         the publication of the fault -> the immutable outcome of that
06590         generation exists -> the effective timeout arrives -> the
06591         endpoint consumes the outcome.
06592 
06593     The gate of the schedule is a transparent wrapper of the production
06594     wait of the worker: it calls the original wait and it holds the
06595     endpoint between the wait and the recheck of its generation.  The
06596     fault is published **independently** of the timeout, the outcome of
06597     the **named** generation is waited for (the generic event of the
06598     worker may be stale) and the timeout of the round is advanced only
06599     after the outcome really exists.
06600 
06601     The error of the round is the **exact original error object** and
06602     the timeout is a **parallel** fact of it.  The history of the error
06603     keeps it across the channel, the handle of the broker and the two
06604     public stops of the round.
06605 
06606     N1 correction 17 (M2): the construction of the chain is **inside**
06607     the guard of the phase, a phase that was **executed** and that
06608     failed is a **FAIL** with the facts that it really observed, and
06609     the result of the phase is written **last**, after the two real
06610     stops and after the cleanup of the phase.  Only a phase that was
06611     never entered is left to the inventory of the outer test.
06612     """
06613     clock = ManualClock(1000.0)
06614     api = _M09TimeoutApi()
06615     fault = RuntimeError('read_fault')
06616     api.fault = fault
06617     api.fault_object = fault
06618     gate = _M09Gate(api)
06619     registry = _OwnerRegistry()
06620     observer = _StartObserver(registry)
06621     session = None
06622     holder = None
06623     channel_closes = None
06624     channel_facts = None
06625     worker = None
06626     generation = None
06627     trace = []
06628     written = set()
06629     # CORRECTION 20 (M2): the **shared** fact store of the group: it
06630     # holds the **last actual** facts of every document and it is the
06631     # store that the outer recovery reads.
06632     raw = store if store is not None else {}
06633     # CORRECTION 18 (M2/M3): the errors of this phase are **lists** and
06634     # they are never overwritten: a cleanup error is never replaced by a
06635     # later one and the first error is never the only one.
06636     cleanup_errors = []
06637     write_errors = []
06638     # CORRECTION 18 (M3): the **exact** objects of the interruptions
06639     # that the cleanup of this phase met.
06640     interrupts = []
06641     # CORRECTION 19 (M3): the seam of the round retains the **exact**
06642     # object of an interruption that its durable write met.
06643     api.interrupts = interrupts
06644     # CORRECTION 18 (M2): the **original** failure of this phase.
06645     original_failure = {'type': None, 'detail': None}
06646     names = (phase, phase + '-construction', phase + '-first',
06647              phase + '-second')
06648 
06649     def record(name, facts):
06650         """Write one document of the phase and mark it as **written**.
06651 
06652         CORRECTION 17 (M2): the document is **persisted first** and it
06653         is marked only after the persistence succeeded.
06654         """
06655         path = write_phase(name, facts)
06656         written.add(name)
06657         if inventory is not None:
06658             inventory.add(name)
06659         if ledger is not None:
06660             ledger.attempt(name)
06661         return path
06662 
06663     def record_guarded(name, facts):
06664         """Write one document and **never** replace an original failure.
06665 
06666         CORRECTION 18 (M2): the writing of the evidence of a phase that
06667         is already failing must never replace the original failure: the
06668         error of the write is retained in ``write_errors`` and the phase
06669         fails **closed**.
06670         """
06671         try:
06672             return record(name, facts)
06673         except BaseException as error:  # noqa: BLE001 - it stays visible
06674             write_errors.append({'name': name,
06675                                  'type': type(error).__name__,
06676                                  'detail': str(error)})
06677             _note_interruption(interrupts, error)
06678             return None
06679 
06680     if ledger is not None:
06681         for name in names:
06682             ledger.plan(name)
06683         ledger.attempt(phase + '-construction')
06684         # CORRECTION 18 (M2): the overall phase is attempted **before**
06685         # the construction of the chain: a construction that failed half
06686         # way is an attempted construction **and** an attempted phase.
06687         ledger.attempt(phase)
06688     original_wait_done = gate.install(_WindowsPipeWorker)
06689     observer_patch = observer.install()
06690     observer_patch.start()
06691     observer.arm()
06692     try:
06693         try:
06694             # CORRECTION 17 (M2): the construction of the chain is
06695             # **inside** the guard of the phase.
06696             session, holder, channel_closes, channel_facts = _chain(
06697                 api, document, plan, tmp_path, clock=clock)
06698             # CORRECTION 18 (M2): a **successful** construction has its
06699             # own terminal document: the facts are written first and the
06700             # ``PASSED`` document is written only after them.
06701             construction_facts = {'construction': 'COMPLETED',
06702                                   'session_created': session is not None,
06703                                   'holder_keys': sorted(holder),
06704                                   'state': getattr(session, 'state', None)}
06705             raw[phase + '-construction'] = dict(construction_facts)
06706             record(phase + '-construction',
06707                    dict(construction_facts, status='EXECUTED'))
06708             record(phase + '-construction',
06709                    dict(construction_facts, status='PASSED'))
06710             if ledger is not None:
06711                 ledger.pass_(phase + '-construction')
06712             session.start()
06713             # The reader of the chain is really inside its read and the
06714             # generation of that read is the fact of the worker itself: it
06715             # is never a number that the group hard codes.
06716             assert api.read_waiting.wait(15.0), \
06717                 'the reader of the chain was never inside its read'
06718             worker = holder['read_endpoint']._reader
06719             assert worker is not None, 'the reader of the chain never started'
06720             gate.target = worker
06721             generation = worker._submitted
06722             assert generation >= 1, 'the reader of the chain never submitted'
06723             # The bound of the round is a real deadline: the timeout that
06724             # arrives later is the expiry of **this** bound and the absolute
06725             # lease of the round stays in the future.
06726             holder['channel'].bind_deadline(clock.value + 0.5)
06727             # The endpoint is held after its production wait and before it
06728             # rechecks its generation.
06729             assert gate.entered.wait(15.0), \
06730                 'the endpoint was never held at the wait of its generation'
06731             trace.append({'step': 'GATE_ENTERED', 'generation': generation,
06732                           'clock': clock.value})
06733             # The ordinary failure of the read is published
06734             # **independently** of the timeout of the round.
06735             api.publish_read_fault()
06736             assert api.fault_observed.wait(15.0), \
06737                 'the ordinary failure of the read never happened'
06738             # The **specific** outcome of that generation is waited for: the
06739             # generic event of the worker may be stale.
06740             outcome = _wait_for_outcome(worker, generation, 15.0)
06741             assert outcome is not None, \
06742                 'the outcome of the generation was never published'
06743             trace.append({'step': 'FAULT_PUBLISHED', 'generation': generation,
06744                           'sequence': outcome['sequence'],
06745                           'error': outcome['error'],
06746                           'clock': clock.value})
06747             assert outcome['generation'] == generation, \
06748                 'the outcome of another generation was read'
06749             assert outcome['error'] == 'RuntimeError', \
06750                 'the published failure of the round is not the ordinary one'
06751             assert outcome['error_object'] is fault, \
06752                 'the outcome of the generation does not keep the original error'
06753             # The effective timeout of the round arrives **now**: the fault
06754             # is already published and the endpoint has not consumed it.
06755             clock.advance(1.0)
06756             trace.append({'step': 'TIMEOUT_ARRIVED', 'generation': generation,
06757                           'clock': clock.value,
06758                           'effective': holder['channel']._effective_deadline()})
06759             # The endpoint continues: it rechecks the timeout and it finds
06760             # the published outcome of its own generation.
06761             gate.release.set()
06762             trace.append({'step': 'RELEASED', 'generation': generation,
06763                           'clock': clock.value})
06764             # The endpoint consumed the outcome: the original error object
06765             # carries the timeout as a **parallel** fact.
06766             deadline = time.monotonic() + 15.0
06767             parallel = None
06768             while time.monotonic() < deadline:
06769                 parallel = getattr(fault, 'lvw_parallel_timeout', None)
06770                 if parallel is not None:
06771                     break
06772                 time.sleep(0.005)
06773             assert isinstance(parallel, dict), \
06774                 'the endpoint did not consume the published outcome'
06775             trace.append({'step': 'OUTCOME_CONSUMED', 'generation': generation,
06776                           'clock': clock.value, 'parallel': parallel})
06777             assert parallel.get('code') == 'BROKER_PIPE_TIMEOUT', \
06778                 'the timeout did not stay a parallel fact of the failure'
06779             assert parallel.get('generation') == generation, \
06780                 'the parallel fact names another generation'
06781             # The order of the three facts is the order of the contract:
06782             # the publication of the fault, the arrival of the timeout and
06783             # the consumption of the outcome.
06784             steps = [item['step'] for item in trace]
06785             assert steps.index('FAULT_PUBLISHED') < steps.index('TIMEOUT_ARRIVED'), \
06786                 'the timeout of the round arrived before the fault was published'
06787             assert steps.index('TIMEOUT_ARRIVED') < steps.index('OUTCOME_CONSUMED'), \
06788                 'the outcome was consumed before the timeout of the round arrived'
06789             raw[phase] = {'generation': generation, 'trace': trace,
06790                           'outcome_error': outcome['error'],
06791                           'outcome_object': type(outcome['error_object']).__name__,
06792                           'parallel_timeout': parallel,
06793                           'gate_calls': gate.calls,
06794                           'state': session.state}
06795             record(phase, dict(raw[phase], status='EXECUTED'))
06796             # The error of the reader of the channel is the error of the
06797             # round: the history of the round keeps it and the two public
06798             # stops of the round keep it.
06799             if ledger is not None:
06800                 ledger.attempt(phase + '-first')
06801             first = session.stop()
06802             first_facts = first['broker_close_facts'] or {}
06803             raw[phase + '-first'] = {
06804                 'reader_error': first_facts.get('channel_reader_error'),
06805                 'state': first['state'],
06806                 'retained': first['broker_retained']}
06807             record(phase + '-first',
06808                    dict(raw[phase + '-first'], status='EXECUTED'))
06809             assert first_facts.get('channel_reader_error') == 'RuntimeError', \
06810                 'the real fault of the round was replaced by a cancellation'
06811             assert first['broker_retained'] is True
06812             assert first['state'] == 'UNKNOWN_RETAINED'
06813             # CORRECTION 18 (M2): the durable document is written
06814             # **before** the terminal state of the ledger is updated.
06815             record(phase + '-first',
06816                    dict(raw[phase + '-first'], status='PASSED'))
06817             if ledger is not None:
06818                 ledger.pass_(phase + '-first')
06819             if ledger is not None:
06820                 ledger.attempt(phase + '-second')
06821             second = session.stop()
06822             second_facts = second['broker_close_facts'] or {}
06823             raw[phase + '-second'] = {
06824                 'reader_error': second_facts.get('channel_reader_error'),
06825                 'source': second_facts.get('channel_reader_error_source'),
06826                 'state': second['state']}
06827             record(phase + '-second',
06828                    dict(raw[phase + '-second'], status='EXECUTED'))
06829             assert second_facts.get('channel_reader_error') == 'RuntimeError', \
06830                 'the second attempt lost the error of the reader'
06831             assert second_facts.get('channel_reader_error_source') \
06832                 == 'REPEATED_CLOSE'
06833             assert second['state'] == 'UNKNOWN_RETAINED'
06834             # CORRECTION 18 (M2): the durable document is written
06835             # **before** the terminal state of the ledger is updated.
06836             record(phase + '-second',
06837                    dict(raw[phase + '-second'], status='PASSED'))
06838             if ledger is not None:
06839                 ledger.pass_(phase + '-second')
06840         except BaseException as error:
06841             # N1 correction 17 (M2): a phase that was **executed** and
06842             # that failed is a **FAIL** with the facts that it really
06843             # observed.  Only a phase that was **never entered** is left
06844             # to the inventory of the outer test.
06845             #
06846             # CORRECTION 18 (M2): the **original** failure is retained
06847             # and it is the one that leaves the phase; the result
06848             # document of the phase is written **after** the cleanup and
06849             # it carries the original failure **and** the diagnostics of
06850             # the cleanup.  The writing of the evidence never replaces
06851             # the original failure.
06852             original_failure['type'] = type(error).__name__
06853             original_failure['detail'] = str(error)
06854             for name in names:
06855                 if name == phase:
06856                     # the result of the phase is written after the
06857                     # cleanup of the phase
06858                     continue
06859                 if ledger is not None:
06860                     state = ledger.state(name)
06861                 else:
06862                     state = 'ATTEMPTED' if name in written else 'PLANNED'
06863                 if state != 'ATTEMPTED':
06864                     continue
06865                 failed_facts = dict(raw.get(name, {}),
06866                                     status='FAIL',
06867                                     executed=True,
06868                                     reason=type(error).__name__,
06869                                     detail=str(error),
06870                                     trace=list(trace),
06871                                     gate_calls=list(gate.calls),
06872                                     cancel_calls=api.cancel_calls)
06873                 # CORRECTION 20 (M2): the **full intended** facts of the
06874                 # document are stored **before** the terminal write is
06875                 # attempted: the outer recovery reuses them.
06876                 raw[name] = dict(failed_facts)
06877                 written_result = record_guarded(name, failed_facts)
06878                 # CORRECTION 19 (M2): the terminal state of the ledger
06879                 # follows the **successful** terminal write: a failed
06880                 # write leaves the phase ATTEMPTED and the outer
06881                 # inventory recovers it, best effort and bounded.
06882                 if ledger is not None and written_result is not None:
06883                     ledger.fail(name, type(error).__name__)
06884             raise
06885         finally:
06886             # The teardown of the owner of the test is bounded and it is
06887             # unconditional: the gate is released, the read of the round is
06888             # ended, the production wait of the worker is restored and the
06889             # worker is stopped and joined.
06890             #
06891             # CORRECTION 18 (M2/M3): every step of the teardown is
06892             # guarded and every error is **retained** in a list: one
06893             # exception never skips the owners that follow it and it
06894             # never skips the result document of the phase.
06895             # CORRECTION 20 (M3): every **initial** release of a gate is
06896             # guarded **on its own**: a cancellation of the first gate
06897             # never skips the second one, never skips the stop, the
06898             # joins, the drain, the proof and the evidence that follow it
06899             # and it never replaces the failure that is already
06900             # unwinding.
06901             _release_gate(gate.release.set, cleanup_errors, interrupts)
06902             _release_gate(api.release_the_worker.set, cleanup_errors,
06903                           interrupts)
06904             _release_gate(lambda: api.publish_outcome('EOF'), cleanup_errors,
06905                           interrupts)
06906             _WindowsPipeWorker.wait_done = original_wait_done
06907             if session is not None:
06908                 try:
06909                     session.stop()
06910                 except BaseException as error:  # noqa: BLE001
06911                     cleanup_errors.append({'kind': 'STOP',
06912                                            'type': type(error).__name__,
06913                                            'detail': str(error)})
06914                     _note_interruption(interrupts, error)
06915             if worker is not None:
06916                 # The worker is stopped explicitly: the coordination of the
06917                 # round was never reached in this schedule and the worker of
06918                 # the read would otherwise wait for a request that never
06919                 # comes.
06920                 try:
06921                     worker.stop_accepting()
06922                 except BaseException as error:  # noqa: BLE001
06923                     cleanup_errors.append({'kind': 'WORKER_STOP',
06924                                            'type': type(error).__name__,
06925                                            'detail': str(error)})
06926                     _note_interruption(interrupts, error)
06927                 try:
06928                     worker.join_bounded(10.0)
06929                 except BaseException as error:  # noqa: BLE001
06930                     cleanup_errors.append({'kind': 'WORKER_JOIN',
06931                                            'type': type(error).__name__,
06932                                            'detail': str(error)})
06933                     _note_interruption(interrupts, error)
06934                 try:
06935                     if worker.is_alive():
06936                         cleanup_errors.append(
06937                             {'kind': 'WORKER', 'type': 'AssertionError',
06938                              'detail': 'the worker of the read was left '
06939                                        'behind'})
06940                 except BaseException as error:  # noqa: BLE001
06941                     cleanup_errors.append({'kind': 'WORKER_LIVENESS',
06942                                            'type': type(error).__name__,
06943                                            'detail': str(error)})
06944                     _note_interruption(interrupts, error)
06945             drain = {'salvage': [], 'attempts': [], 'proof': None,
06946                      'quiescent': False, 'errors': [], 'all_salvage': []}
06947             try:
06948                 drain = _drain_owners(
06949                     registry, session=session,
06950                     channel=(holder or {}).get('channel'), prefix=phase,
06951                     release=(lambda: gate.release.set(),
06952                              lambda: api.release_the_worker.set()),
06953                     interrupts=interrupts)
06954             except BaseException as error:  # noqa: BLE001 - it stays visible
06955                 cleanup_errors.append({'kind': 'DRAIN',
06956                                        'type': type(error).__name__,
06957                                        'detail': str(error)})
06958                 _note_interruption(interrupts, error)
06959             # CORRECTION 18 (M2): the result of the phase is written
06960             # **after** the cleanup and it carries the original failure
06961             # **and** every diagnostic of the cleanup.  The phase is
06962             # ``PASSED`` only when the proof held, the round was
06963             # quiescent and no unexpected cleanup or evidence error
06964             # happened.
06965             cleanup_detail = _cleanup_detail(cleanup_errors, write_errors)
06966             # CORRECTION 19 (M2): the result of the phase is decided
06967             # **after** the cleanup and it is written **before** the
06968             # terminal state of the ledger is advanced.
06969             clean = (original_failure['type'] is None
06970                      and cleanup_detail is None
06971                      and drain['proof'] is None
06972                      and drain['quiescent'] is True)
06973             phase_failure_detail = None
06974             if clean:
06975                 final_facts = dict(raw.get(phase, {}), status='PASSED',
06976                                    cleanup_errors=[],
06977                                    evidence_write_errors=[],
06978                                    proof_failure=None,
06979                                    quiescent=True,
06980                                    drain=drain['attempts'],
06981                                    interrupts=_interruption_facts(interrupts))
06982                 # CORRECTION 20 (M2): the **full intended** facts of the
06983                 # phase are stored **before** the terminal persistence is
06984                 # attempted.
06985                 raw[phase] = dict(final_facts)
06986                 written_result = record_guarded(phase, final_facts)
06987                 if written_result is None:
06988                     # the evidence of the phase could not be written:
06989                     # the phase fails **closed** and it is never
06990                     # reported as a success.  Its terminal state of the
06991                     # ledger is **not** advanced: the outer inventory
06992                     # recovers it, best effort and bounded.
06993                     phase_failure_detail = (
06994                         'the result document of the phase could not be '
06995                         'written: %s' % (write_errors,))
06996                     # CORRECTION 20 (M2): the store keeps the diagnostic
06997                     # of the failed persistence: the retry of the outer
06998                     # recovery carries it and it is never reduced to a
06999                     # generic ``FAIL``.
07000                     raw[phase] = dict(final_facts,
07001                                       status='FAIL',
07002                                       evidence_write_errors=list(
07003                                           write_errors),
07004                                       detail=_join_details(
07005                                           final_facts.get('detail'),
07006                                           phase_failure_detail))
07007                 elif ledger is not None:
07008                     ledger.pass_(phase)
07009             else:
07010                 reason = (original_failure['type'] or 'CleanupError')
07011                 detail = _join_details(original_failure['detail'],
07012                                        cleanup_detail, drain['proof'])
07013                 if drain['quiescent'] is not True:
07014                     detail = _join_details(
07015                         detail, 'the owners of the phase were not quiescent')
07016                     if original_failure['type'] is None:
07017                         reason = 'NOT_QUIESCENT'
07018                 failed_phase_facts = dict(raw.get(phase, {}),
07019                                           status='FAIL',
07020                                           executed=True,
07021                                           reason=reason,
07022                                           detail=detail,
07023                                           original_failure=dict(
07024                                               original_failure),
07025                                           cleanup_errors=list(cleanup_errors),
07026                                           evidence_write_errors=list(
07027                                               write_errors),
07028                                           proof_failure=drain['proof'],
07029                                           quiescent=drain['quiescent'],
07030                                           drain=drain['attempts'],
07031                                           interrupts=_interruption_facts(
07032                                               interrupts))
07033                 # CORRECTION 20 (M2): the **full intended** facts of the
07034                 # phase are stored **before** the terminal persistence is
07035                 # attempted.
07036                 raw[phase] = dict(failed_phase_facts)
07037                 written_result = record_guarded(phase, failed_phase_facts)
07038                 # CORRECTION 19 (M2): the terminal state of the ledger
07039                 # follows the **successful** terminal write: a failed
07040                 # write leaves the phase ATTEMPTED and the outer
07041                 # inventory recovers it, best effort and bounded.
07042                 if written_result is not None:
07043                     if ledger is not None:
07044                         ledger.fail(phase, reason)
07045                 else:
07046                     phase_failure_detail = (
07047                         'the durable FAIL of the phase was not written: %s'
07048                         % (write_errors,))
07049                     cleanup_detail = _join_details(cleanup_detail,
07050                                                    phase_failure_detail)
07051                     # CORRECTION 20 (M2): the store keeps the diagnostic
07052                     # of the failed persistence of the ``FAIL``.
07053                     raw[phase] = dict(failed_phase_facts,
07054                                       evidence_write_errors=list(write_errors),
07055                                       detail=_join_details(
07056                                           failed_phase_facts.get('detail'),
07057                                           phase_failure_detail))
07058                 if original_failure['type'] is None:
07059                     # CORRECTION 19 (M2): the diagnostic of a failed
07060                     # write is **joined** with the diagnostic of the
07061                     # phase: neither of them replaces the other.
07062                     phase_failure_detail = _join_details(
07063                         phase_failure_detail,
07064                         'the phase %s failed: %s' % (phase, detail))
07065             # CORRECTION 19 (M2/M3): the priority of the failures is
07066             # fixed: the failure that is already unwinding wins, then
07067             # the **exact** interruption of the cleanup and only then the
07068             # ordinary assertion of the proof or of the evidence.  An
07069             # exact interruption is never replaced by a proof assertion
07070             # and the rethrow of it is never unreachable.
07071             if interrupts and sys.exc_info()[0] is None:
07072                 raise interrupts[0]
07073             # CORRECTION 19 (M2): a durable FAIL of the phase never
07074             # returns a successful test: the phase fails **with** its
07075             # own document.
07076             if phase_failure_detail is not None \
07077                     and sys.exc_info()[0] is None:
07078                 raise AssertionError(phase_failure_detail)
07079             if cleanup_detail is not None and sys.exc_info()[0] is None:
07080                 raise AssertionError(cleanup_detail)
07081             if drain['proof'] is not None and sys.exc_info()[0] is None:
07082                 # the owners of the round were not released: the proof of
07083                 # the cleanup fails truthfully and the phase fails with it
07084                 raise AssertionError(drain['proof'])
07085     finally:
07086         observer.disarm()
07087         observer_patch.stop()
07088 def test_n10_r10_the_outcome_of_the_generation_wins_over_the_cancellation(tmp_path):
07089     """N10-R10 (M09): a real fault of the round is never a benign cancel.
07090 
07091     Obligation: the worker of an endpoint keeps the **outcome** of
07092     every generation it runs: the result, the error, the original error
07093     object and the order of the completion.  A failure that was really
07094     published **before** the cancellation of the round is the first
07095     error of the round: a close that sets the cancel flag afterwards
07096     may not replace it and it may not turn it into a benign
07097     cancellation.  The identity of the token of the cancel is not a
07098     proof: the order of the two facts is the proof.  The error of the
07099     reader is a **history**: two real stops of the round keep it and
07100     the round stays unknown.
07101 
07102     Entry: ``_WindowsPipeWorker.outcome``,
07103     ``_WindowsPipeWorker.published_before_cancel``,
07104     ``_WindowsPipeEndpoint._raise_cancellation``,
07105     ``BrokerProcess._keep_reader_error_history`` and
07106     ``NativeCustodySession.stop``.  Trigger: the read of the
07107     generation fails with an ordinary error, the outcome of the
07108     generation is really published and the close of the round asks for
07109     the cancellation of the same generation.  Oracle: the original
07110     error object is the one that leaves, the code of the error is the
07111     code of the real fault and the two stops of the round keep it.
07112     Negative: a cancellation may never rewrite a published failure.
07113     Returned state: the outcome of the generation is named with its
07114     order and its error object.  Salvage: the owner of the test stops
07115     the round in every case.
07116 
07117     CORRECTION 11 (M09): the outcome of the **same** generation is
07118     inspected **again** after the coordination of the cancellation, and
07119     it is inspected **before** a new timeout is decided.  The published
07120     failure of the generation remains the primary error of the round and
07121     the failure of the coordination and a new timeout are **parallel**
07122     facts: they never replace it and they never hide it.  The group
07123     drives the coordination of a cancellation whose fault of the round
07124     is published while the coordination runs, and it proves the order
07125     of the two facts with the auxiliary static proof of the group.
07126 
07127     CORRECTION 12 (M09): the auxiliary static proof is **replaced** by
07128     the dynamic schedule of the obligation.  The real chain of the round
07129     publishes a real ordinary fault for the **current** generation of
07130     its read, the effective timeout of the round arrives **before** the
07131     endpoint consumes the outcome, and both a **confirmed** and a
07132     **failed** coordination of the round preserve the exact original
07133     error object and its code: the timeout and the failure of the
07134     coordination are parallel facts and they never replace the failure
07135     that was really published.  The history of the error keeps it
07136     across the channel, the handle of the broker and the two public
07137     stops of the round, and the round stays unknown.  The generation of
07138     the blocked read is the fact of the worker itself and it is never a
07139     number that the group hard codes.
07140 
07141     CORRECTION 12 (root preflight PF12-01): the **required order** is
07142     driven as well and it is driven **distinctly** from the two
07143     coordination subcases above.  The ordinary failure of the read is
07144     published **first** and the immutable outcome of **that** generation
07145     really exists before the effective timeout of the round arrives;
07146     the endpoint consumes the outcome only after the timeout was
07147     decided.  The gate of that schedule is a transparent wrapper of the
07148     production wait of the worker: it calls the original wait and it
07149     holds the endpoint between that wait and the recheck of its
07150     generation.  The publication, the timeout and the consumption are
07151     recorded in their order and the current generation is named with
07152     them.  The coordination subcases above remain: they are the ones
07153     that drive a **confirmed** and a **failed** coordination of the
07154     round, and the failure of the coordination is a parallel fact there.
07155 
07156     RECONCILIATION (N1 correction 14, F5): the schedule of the **failed**
07157     coordination reached the selection point of the production without a
07158     published fault.  The cancel primitive of the round enqueued the
07159     fault of the read and returned immediately, so the coordination
07160     could finish before the worker had published the immutable outcome
