07161     of its generation, and the parallel fact of the timeout could not
07162     be read at all (the phase document of the failed batch of the
07163     correction 13 stopped there).
07164 
07165     The seam of the failed coordination now publishes the **real**
07166     primitive fault and waits, bounded, for the **immutable outcome of
07167     that specific generation** before the cancel primitive returns,
07168     while the worker of the round is held inside its own final close
07169     and release tail: the real bounded join of the coordination still
07170     FAILS and the coordination error stays a **parallel** fact of the
07171     original error of the round.  The confirmed coordination, the
07172     required order of the schedule (fault published before the timeout,
07173     consumed after it) and the two public stops of the round stay
07174     separate and unchanged, no outcome, no history, no selection and
07175     no public stop of the production is patched, and no assertion of
07176     the group is relaxed.
07177 
07178     N1 correction 15 (M2): the wait of the seam for the outcome is a
07179     **checked** barrier and it **fails closed**.  The barrier demands
07180     the immutable outcome of the **exact** generation, the expected kind
07181     of the ordinary failure and the **exact** original error object
07182     **before** the cancel primitive returns, and it stores the checked
07183     monotonic order of the three facts.  A missing outcome, an outcome
07184     of another generation, another kind of failure and another error
07185     object are an **explicit refusal** of the premise of the test: the
07186     primitive never returns ``REQUESTED`` as if the barrier had passed.
07187     The production catches the ordinary exceptions of the seam, so the
07188     outer test reads the premise from the record of the barrier itself
07189     and it captures that record **before** the main assertions of the
07190     phase.
07191 
07192     The two negative subcases of the barrier drive the **same** failed
07193     coordination with a bounded transparent seam that forces an absent
07194     outcome and an outcome of another generation.  Their premise is
07195     **refused** and they are never counted as a successful failed
07196     coordination.  The confirmed coordination, the required order of the
07197     schedule and the two public stops of the round stay separate and
07198     unchanged.
07199 
07200     N1 correction 16 (M2): the facts of the barrier are written
07201     **before** the main assertions of the phase, so a barrier that
07202     failed closed keeps its failure, its order and its object even when
07203     the main assertions of the phase fail afterwards.  A phase that was
07204     **executed** and that failed is a **FAIL** and it keeps the facts
07205     that it really observed; only the documents that were genuinely
07206     not reached are ``NOT_RUN``.  The **outer** inventory of the group
07207     names every main schedule and both negative subcases before they
07208     run: the failure of an earlier schedule may never leave the
07209     evidence of a later one ambiguous.
07210     """
07211     # -- N1 correction 17 (M2): the **outer** inventory of the phases of
07212     #    this group is installed **before** any setup and before any
07213     #    assertion of the group, and it is **complete**: every name that
07214     #    a schedule really writes is named here and every name that is
07215     #    never entered is written as ``NOT_RUN`` without a fact.
07216     #
07217     #    The arithmetic of the inventory is derived from the operations
07218     #    that are really instrumented:
07219     #
07220     #    * four timeout schedules with five names each - the result of
07221     #      the phase, the construction of the chain, the barrier of the
07222     #      publication, the first stop and the second stop - that is 20;
07223     #    * the published-before-timeout schedule with four names - the
07224     #      result of the phase, the construction of the chain, the first
07225     #      stop and the second stop - because its construction is
07226     #      instrumented like the construction of the other schedules;
07227     #    * the three documents of the earlier work of the group - the
07228     #      first stop, the second stop and the coordination;
07229     #    * the final verdict of the earlier work of the group - the
07230     #      salvage of that round - because its terminal document is
07231     #      written **after** its cleanup, exactly like the terminal
07232     #      document of every later schedule;
07233     #
07234     #    that is 28 names, and every one of them is unique.
07235     ledger = _PhaseLedger()
07236     r10_documents = []
07237     for r10_name in ('r10-timeout-confirmed', 'r10-timeout-failed',
07238                       'r10-barrier-absent', 'r10-barrier-wrong-generation'):
07239         r10_documents.extend([r10_name, r10_name + '-construction',
07240                                r10_name + '-barrier', r10_name + '-first',
07241                                r10_name + '-second'])
07242     r10_documents.extend(['r10-published-before-timeout',
07243                            'r10-published-before-timeout-construction',
07244                            'r10-published-before-timeout-first',
07245                            'r10-published-before-timeout-second'])
07246     r10_documents.extend(['r10-first', 'r10-second', 'r10-coordination',
07247                            'r10-early-salvage'])
07248     assert len(r10_documents) == len(set(r10_documents)), \
07249         'the inventory of the group names a document twice'
07250     for r10_name in r10_documents:
07251         ledger.plan(r10_name)
07252     r10_inventory = set()
07253     r10_raw = {}
07254     first_failure = {'type': None}
07255     # CORRECTION 19 (M3): the **exact** objects of the interruptions
07256     # that the inventory of the group met.
07257     outer_interrupts = []
07258     # CORRECTION 17 (M3): the observer of the starts of the round of the
07259     # earlier work of the group.
07260     early_registry = _OwnerRegistry()
07261     early_observer = _StartObserver(early_registry)
07262     try:
07263         document = _live_plan_document()
07264         plan = validate_launch_plan(document, now=time.time())
07265         api = _F5Api(read_fault=RuntimeError('read_fault'))
07266         # the observation of the starts covers **only** the round of the
07267         # earlier work: it is armed before the chain is built and it is
07268         # disarmed after the salvage of that round.
07269         early_observer_patch = early_observer.install()
07270         early_observer_patch.start()
07271         early_observer.arm()
07272         try:
07273             # CORRECTION 18 (M3): the construction of the chain is
07274             # **inside** the guard of the round: a construction that
07275             # failed half way still owns its teardown and its proof.
07276             session = None
07277             holder = None
07278             channel_closes = None
07279             channel_facts = None
07280             early_cleanup_errors = []
07281             # CORRECTION 19 (M3): the **exact** objects of the
07282             # interruptions that the cleanup of the earlier work met.
07283             early_interrupts = []
07284             # CORRECTION 19 (M2): the **original** failure of the earlier
07285             # work: it is durable and it never disappears behind a
07286             # diagnostic of the cleanup.
07287             early_original = {'type': None, 'detail': None}
07288             # CORRECTION 19 (M2): the attempt of the final verdict of the
07289             # earlier work is marked **before** the round is built.
07290             ledger.attempt('r10-early-salvage')
07291             try:
07292                 session, holder, channel_closes, channel_facts = _chain(
07293                     api, document, plan, tmp_path)
07294                 session.start()
07295                 reader = holder['read_endpoint']._reader
07296                 assert reader is not None, 'the reader of the chain never started'
07297                 # The owner of the test publishes the ordinary failure of the
07298                 # read and waits for the reader to be really inside its read.
07299                 assert api.read_waiting.wait(15.0), \
07300                     'the reader of the chain was never inside its read'
07301                 # The generation of the read that is really in flight is the
07302                 # fact of the worker itself: the two frames of the round were
07303                 # read before it and the generation of the blocked read is
07304                 # never a number that the group hard codes.
07305                 generation = reader._submitted
07306                 assert generation >= 1, 'the reader of the chain never submitted'
07307                 api.publish_read_fault()
07308                 assert api.fault_observed.wait(15.0), \
07309                     'the ordinary failure of the read never happened'
07310                 # The barrier: the outcome of the generation is really published
07311                 # and it carries the **original** error object.
07312                 assert reader.outcome_published.wait(15.0), \
07313                     'the outcome of the read was never published'
07314                 outcome = reader.outcome(generation)
07315                 assert outcome is not None, 'the outcome of the generation is gone'
07316                 assert outcome['generation'] == generation, \
07317                     'the outcome of another generation was read'
07318                 assert outcome['error'] == 'RuntimeError'
07319                 assert isinstance(outcome['error_object'], RuntimeError)
07320                 # The close of the round asks for the cancellation of the same
07321                 # generation **after** the failure was published.
07322                 holder['channel']._cancel.set()
07323                 ledger.attempt('r10-first')
07324                 first = session.stop()
07325                 first_facts = first['broker_close_facts'] or {}
07326                 r10_raw['r10-first'] = {
07327                     'generation': generation,
07328                     'outcome_error': outcome['error'],
07329                     'outcome_object':
07330                     type(outcome['error_object']).__name__,
07331                     'sequence': outcome['sequence'],
07332                     'reader_error':
07333                     first_facts.get('channel_reader_error'),
07334                     'state': first['state']}
07335                 write_phase('r10-first', dict(r10_raw['r10-first'],
07336                                               status='EXECUTED'))
07337                 r10_inventory.add('r10-first')
07338                 assert first_facts.get('channel_reader_error') == 'RuntimeError', \
07339                     'the real fault of the round was replaced by a cancellation'
07340                 assert first_facts.get('close_cancellation') is None
07341                 assert first['broker_retained'] is True
07342                 assert first['state'] == 'UNKNOWN_RETAINED'
07343                 # CORRECTION 18 (M2): the durable document is written
07344                 # **before** the terminal state of the ledger is updated.
07345                 write_phase('r10-first', dict(r10_raw['r10-first'],
07346                                               status='PASSED'))
07347                 ledger.pass_('r10-first')
07348 
07349                 # The second public stop of the round: the error of the reader is
07350                 # a history and it is never lost.
07351                 ledger.attempt('r10-second')
07352                 second = session.stop()
07353                 second_facts = second['broker_close_facts'] or {}
07354                 r10_raw['r10-second'] = {
07355                     'reader_error': second_facts.get('channel_reader_error'),
07356                     'source': second_facts.get('channel_reader_error_source'),
07357                     'state': second['state']}
07358                 write_phase('r10-second', dict(r10_raw['r10-second'],
07359                                                status='EXECUTED'))
07360                 r10_inventory.add('r10-second')
07361                 assert second_facts.get('channel_reader_error') == 'RuntimeError', \
07362                     'the second attempt lost the error of the reader'
07363                 assert second_facts.get('channel_reader_error_source') \
07364                     == 'REPEATED_CLOSE'
07365                 assert second['broker_retained'] is True
07366                 assert second['state'] == 'UNKNOWN_RETAINED'
07367                 write_phase('r10-second', dict(r10_raw['r10-second'],
07368                                                status='PASSED'))
07369                 ledger.pass_('r10-second')
07370             except BaseException as error:  # noqa: BLE001 - it stays visible
07371                 # CORRECTION 19 (M2): the **original** failure of the
07372                 # earlier work is retained and it is durable: the final
07373                 # verdict of the cleanup carries it next to the errors of
07374                 # the cleanup.
07375                 early_original['type'] = type(error).__name__
07376                 early_original['detail'] = str(error)
07377                 raise
07378             finally:
07379                 # CORRECTION 18 (M3): every step of the teardown is
07380                 # guarded and every error is **retained** in a list: one
07381                 # exception never skips the drain of the owners and it
07382                 # never skips the proof of the cleanup.
07383                 #
07384                 # CORRECTION 19 (M3): the **exact** object of a
07385                 # cancellation of the cleanup is retained and it is
07386                 # re-raised by the owner of the earlier work.
07387                 if session is not None:
07388                     try:
07389                         session.stop()
07390                     except BaseException as error:  # noqa: BLE001
07391                         early_cleanup_errors.append(
07392                             {'kind': 'STOP',
07393                              'type': type(error).__name__,
07394                              'detail': str(error)})
07395                         _note_interruption(early_interrupts, error)
07396                 # CORRECTION 17 (M3): the owners of the round of the earlier
07397                 # work are drained **boundedly** and the quiescence is
07398                 # asserted **after** the drain.
07399                 early_drain = {'salvage': [], 'attempts': [], 'proof': None,
07400                                'quiescent': False, 'errors': [],
07401                                'all_salvage': []}
07402                 try:
07403                     early_drain = _drain_owners(
07404                         early_registry, session=session,
07405                         channel=(holder or {}).get('channel'),
07406                         prefix='r10-early',
07407                         interrupts=early_interrupts)
07408                 except BaseException as error:  # noqa: BLE001
07409                     early_cleanup_errors.append(
07410                         {'kind': 'DRAIN',
07411                          'type': type(error).__name__,
07412                          'detail': str(error)})
07413                     _note_interruption(early_interrupts, error)
07414                 # CORRECTION 19 (M2): the **final verdict** of the
07415                 # earlier work is computed **after** the cleanup and it
07416                 # is written **before** the terminal state of the ledger
07417                 # is advanced.  The passed subchecks keep their own
07418                 # documents and they are never rewritten by it.
07419                 early_failure = None
07420                 if early_original['type'] is not None:
07421                     # CORRECTION 20 (M2): the **final verdict** of the
07422                     # earlier work is a **FAIL** when its **original**
07423                     # failure is not None: a cleanup that is clean never
07424                     # certifies a round that really failed and the
07425                     # verdict of the operation is never the verdict of
07426                     # its subchecks.
07427                     early_failure = ('the earlier work of the group failed: '
07428                                      '%s: %s' % (early_original['type'],
07429                                                  early_original['detail']))
07430                 elif early_drain['proof'] is not None:
07431                     early_failure = early_drain['proof']
07432                 elif early_drain['quiescent'] is not True:
07433                     early_failure = ('the owners of the earlier work of the '
07434                                      'group were not quiescent')
07435                 elif early_cleanup_errors:
07436                     early_failure = ('the cleanup of the earlier work of the '
07437                                      'group met %s'
07438                                      % (early_cleanup_errors,))
07439                 early_document = {
07440                     'salvage': early_drain['salvage'],
07441                     'all_salvage': early_drain.get('all_salvage', []),
07442                     'drain': early_drain['attempts'],
07443                     'quiescent': early_drain['quiescent'],
07444                     'proof_failure': early_drain['proof'],
07445                     'cleanup_errors': list(early_cleanup_errors),
07446                     'original_failure': dict(early_original),
07447                     'interrupts': _interruption_facts(early_interrupts),
07448                     'status': ('PASSED' if early_failure is None else 'FAIL')}
07449                 if early_failure is not None:
07450                     early_document['executed'] = True
07451                     early_document['reason'] = (early_original['type']
07452                                                 or 'EarlyCleanup')
07453                     early_document['detail'] = (early_failure
07454                                                 or early_original['detail'])
07455                 # CORRECTION 20 (M2): the **full intended** facts of the
07456                 # final verdict are stored **before** the terminal write is
07457                 # attempted and they are the facts that the outer
07458                 # recovery reuses.
07459                 r10_raw['r10-early-salvage'] = dict(early_document)
07460                 early_write_failure = None
07461                 try:
07462                     write_phase('r10-early-salvage', early_document)
07463                 except BaseException as write_error:  # noqa: BLE001
07464                     early_cleanup_errors.append(
07465                         {'kind': 'EVIDENCE',
07466                          'type': type(write_error).__name__,
07467                          'detail': str(write_error)})
07468                     _note_interruption(early_interrupts, write_error)
07469                     # CORRECTION 20 (M2): the evidence of the final
07470                     # verdict **failed closed**: the diagnostic of the
07471                     # failure of the write is retained next to the
07472                     # original failure and the store keeps it.
07473                     early_write_failure = (
07474                         'the evidence of the final verdict of the earlier '
07475                         'work could not be written: %s' % (write_error,))
07476                     r10_raw['r10-early-salvage'] = dict(
07477                         early_document,
07478                         status='FAIL',
07479                         evidence_write_errors=list(early_cleanup_errors),
07480                         detail=_join_details(early_document.get('detail'),
07481                                              early_write_failure))
07482                 else:
07483                     r10_inventory.add('r10-early-salvage')
07484                     # CORRECTION 19 (M2): the terminal state of the ledger
07485                     # follows the **successful** terminal write.
07486                     if early_failure is None:
07487                         ledger.pass_('r10-early-salvage')
07488                     else:
07489                         ledger.fail('r10-early-salvage', 'EarlyCleanup')
07490                 # CORRECTION 19 (M3): the priority of the failures is
07491                 # fixed: the failure that is already unwinding wins, then
07492                 # the **exact** interruption of the cleanup and only then
07493                 # the ordinary assertion of the cleanup.
07494                 if early_interrupts and sys.exc_info()[0] is None:
07495                     raise early_interrupts[0]
07496                 if early_write_failure is not None \
07497                         and sys.exc_info()[0] is None:
07498                     # CORRECTION 20 (M2): the evidence of the final
07499                     # verdict is **closed** after the cleanup: the failure
07500                     # of the write is a real failure of the group and it
07501                     # is never reduced to a late incomplete ledger.
07502                     raise AssertionError(early_write_failure)
07503                 if early_failure is not None and sys.exc_info()[0] is None:
07504                     raise AssertionError(early_failure)
07505         finally:
07506             early_observer.disarm()
07507             early_observer_patch.stop()
07508 
07509         # -- the coordination of the cancellation (N1 correction 11, M09):
07510         #    the outcome of the **same** generation is inspected **again**
07511         #    after the coordination of the cancellation.  The original error
07512         #    object of the generation remains the error that leaves and the
07513         #    failure of the coordination is a **parallel** fact: it never
07514         #    replaces the published failure and it never hides it.
07515         gate = threading.Event()
07516         entered = threading.Event()
07517 
07518         class CoordinationApi:
07519             """The primitives of a read whose fault is published late."""
07520 
07521             def duplicate_current_thread_handle(self):
07522                 return 705
07523 
07524             def cancel_synchronous_io(self, handle):
07525                 # The request of the cancellation releases the fault of the
07526                 # round: the original error of the generation is really
07527                 # published **while** the coordination of the cancellation
07528                 # runs.
07529                 gate.set()
07530                 return 'NOT_FOUND'
07531 
07532             def close_handle(self, handle):
07533                 return None
07534 
07535             def read_stream(self, handle, size):
07536                 entered.set()
07537                 gate.wait(5.0)
07538                 raise RuntimeError('read_fault')
07539 
07540         # CORRECTION 18 (M2): the attempt of the coordination is marked
07541         # **before** the endpoint is created, before the reader is started
07542         # and before the first assertion of the coordination: an
07543         # operation that really ran is never inventoried as ``NOT_RUN``.
07544         ledger.attempt('r10-coordination')
07545         # CORRECTION 18 (M3): the coordination round has its **own**
07546         # observer and its **own** registry: the reader that it starts is
07547         # an owner of that round and it is joined with a bounded wait.
07548         coord_registry = _OwnerRegistry()
07549         coord_observer = _StartObserver(coord_registry)
07550         coord_observer_patch = coord_observer.install()
07551         coord_observer_patch.start()
07552         coord_observer.arm()
07553         endpoint = None
07554         reader = None
07555         coord_cleanup_errors = []
07556         coord_interrupts = []
07557         coord_original = {'type': None, 'detail': None}
07558         coord_passed = False
07559         try:
07560             # CORRECTION 19 (M3): the **cleanup owning** try starts
07561             # **before** the endpoint is constructed and **before** the
07562             # reader is started.  A start that took effect and that then
07563             # raised still obtains the release of the gates, the close of
07564             # the endpoint, the join of the observed threads and the
07565             # proof of the round: a comment is not a boundary.
07566             try:
07567                 endpoint = _WindowsPipeEndpoint(
07568                     file_object=_FakeFile('coordination'),
07569                     name='coordination',
07570                     api=CoordinationApi(),
07571                     clock=time.monotonic, handle=706)
07572                 cancel = _ChannelCancel()
07573                 holder = {}
07574 
07575                 def run_read():
07576                     try:
07577                         endpoint.read(None, cancel)
07578                     except BaseException as error:  # noqa: BLE001
07579                         holder['error'] = error
07580 
07581                 reader = threading.Thread(target=run_read)
07582                 reader.start()
07583                 assert entered.wait(5.0), \
07584                     'the read never entered the operation'
07585                 # The cancellation of the round is published while the read is
07586                 # really inside its operation.
07587                 cancel.set()
07588                 reader.join(10.0)
07589                 assert not reader.is_alive(), \
07590                     'the read of the round was left behind'
07591                 error = holder.get('error')
07592                 assert isinstance(error, RuntimeError), \
07593                     'the original error object of the generation was replaced'
07594                 assert str(error) == 'read_fault'
07595                 outcome = endpoint._reader.outcome(
07596                     endpoint._reader._submitted)
07597                 assert outcome is not None, \
07598                     'the outcome of the generation is gone'
07599                 assert outcome['error'] == 'RuntimeError'
07600                 assert isinstance(outcome['error_object'], RuntimeError)
07601                 r10_raw['r10-coordination'] = {
07602                     'error': type(error).__name__,
07603                     'detail': str(error),
07604                     'outcome': outcome['error'],
07605                     'parallel': getattr(
07606                         error, 'lvw_parallel_cancellation', None) is not None}
07607                 write_phase('r10-coordination',
07608                             dict(r10_raw['r10-coordination'],
07609                                  status='EXECUTED'))
07610                 r10_inventory.add('r10-coordination')
07611                 coord_passed = True
07612             except BaseException as error:  # noqa: BLE001 - it stays visible
07613                 coord_original['type'] = type(error).__name__
07614                 coord_original['detail'] = str(error)
07615                 raise
07616             finally:
07617                 # CORRECTION 18 (M3): every step of the cleanup is
07618                 # guarded: one exception never skips the close of the
07619                 # endpoint and it never skips the owners of the round.
07620                 #
07621                 # CORRECTION 19 (M3): the **exact** object of a
07622                 # cancellation of the cleanup is retained and it is
07623                 # re-raised by the owner of the coordination.
07624                 # CORRECTION 20 (M3): the **initial** release of the gate
07625                 # of the coordination is guarded **on its own**: a
07626                 # cancellation of it never skips the join of the reader,
07627                 # the close of the endpoint, the drain, the proof and the
07628                 # evidence that follow it and it never replaces the
07629                 # failure that is already unwinding.
07630                 _release_gate(gate.set, coord_cleanup_errors, coord_interrupts)
07631                 if reader is not None:
07632                     try:
07633                         reader.join(10.0)
07634                     except BaseException as error:  # noqa: BLE001
07635                         coord_cleanup_errors.append(
07636                             {'kind': 'READER_JOIN',
07637                              'type': type(error).__name__,
07638                              'detail': str(error)})
07639                         _note_interruption(coord_interrupts, error)
07640                 if endpoint is not None:
07641                     try:
07642                         endpoint.close()
07643                     except BaseException as error:  # noqa: BLE001
07644                         coord_cleanup_errors.append(
07645                             {'kind': 'ENDPOINT_CLOSE',
07646                              'type': type(error).__name__,
07647                              'detail': str(error)})
07648                         _note_interruption(coord_interrupts, error)
07649                 coord_drain = {'salvage': [], 'attempts': [], 'proof': None,
07650                                'quiescent': False, 'errors': [],
07651                                'all_salvage': []}
07652                 try:
07653                     coord_drain = _drain_owners(
07654                         coord_registry, prefix='r10-coordination',
07655                         interrupts=coord_interrupts)
07656                 except BaseException as error:  # noqa: BLE001
07657                     coord_cleanup_errors.append(
07658                         {'kind': 'DRAIN',
07659                          'type': type(error).__name__,
07660                          'detail': str(error)})
07661                     _note_interruption(coord_interrupts, error)
07662                 # CORRECTION 19 (M2): the **final** result of the
07663                 # coordination is computed **after** the cleanup and it
07664                 # is written **before** the terminal state of the ledger
07665                 # is advanced.  There is no terminal PASS **before** the
07666                 # cleanup.
07667                 coord_failure = None
07668                 if coord_drain['proof'] is not None:
07669                     coord_failure = coord_drain['proof']
07670                 elif coord_drain['quiescent'] is not True:
07671                     coord_failure = ('the owners of the coordination of the '
07672                                      'round were not quiescent')
07673                 elif coord_cleanup_errors:
07674                     coord_failure = ('the cleanup of the coordination of the '
07675                                      'round met %s'
07676                                      % (coord_cleanup_errors,))
07677                 # the diagnostics of the cleanup are **durable** and they
07678                 # stay in the raw facts of the coordination: a retry of
07679                 # the outer inventory carries them too.
07680                 r10_raw.setdefault('r10-coordination', {})
07681                 r10_raw['r10-coordination']['cleanup_errors'] = \
07682                     list(coord_cleanup_errors)
07683                 r10_raw['r10-coordination']['quiescent'] = \
07684                     coord_drain['quiescent']
07685                 r10_raw['r10-coordination']['proof_failure'] = \
07686                     coord_drain['proof']
07687                 # CORRECTION 20 (M2): the evidence of the coordination has
07688                 # its **own** name: the **original launch document** of
07689                 # the group keeps its own binding and it reaches every
07690                 # real schedule unchanged.  The coordination never
07691                 # replaces the plan of the round with its own evidence.
07692                 coord_document = dict(r10_raw['r10-coordination'],
07693                                       original_failure=dict(coord_original),
07694                                       interrupts=_interruption_facts(
07695                                           coord_interrupts),
07696                                       drain=coord_drain['attempts'])
07697                 if coord_failure is None and coord_passed \
07698                         and coord_original['type'] is None:
07699                     coord_document['status'] = 'PASSED'
07700                 else:
07701                     coord_document['status'] = 'FAIL'
07702                     coord_document['executed'] = True
07703                     coord_document['reason'] = (coord_original['type']
07704                                                 or 'CoordinationCleanup')
07705                     coord_document['detail'] = (coord_failure
07706                                                 or coord_original['detail'])
07707                 # CORRECTION 20 (M2): the **full intended** facts of the
07708                 # coordination are stored **before** the terminal write is
07709                 # attempted.
07710                 r10_raw['r10-coordination'] = dict(coord_document)
07711                 coord_write_failure = None
07712                 try:
07713                     write_phase('r10-coordination', coord_document)
07714                 except BaseException as write_error:  # noqa: BLE001
07715                     coord_cleanup_errors.append(
07716                         {'kind': 'EVIDENCE',
07717                          'type': type(write_error).__name__,
07718                          'detail': str(write_error)})
07719                     _note_interruption(coord_interrupts, write_error)
07720                     # CORRECTION 20 (M2): the evidence of the coordination
07721                     # **failed closed** and the store keeps the
07722                     # diagnostic of the failure of the write next to the
07723                     # original failure.
07724                     coord_write_failure = (
07725                         'the evidence of the coordination could not be '
07726                         'written: %s' % (write_error,))
07727                     r10_raw['r10-coordination'] = dict(
07728                         coord_document,
07729                         status='FAIL',
07730                         evidence_write_errors=list(coord_cleanup_errors),
07731                         detail=_join_details(coord_document.get('detail'),
07732                                              coord_write_failure))
07733                 else:
07734                     r10_inventory.add('r10-coordination')
07735                     # CORRECTION 19 (M2): the terminal state of the ledger
07736                     # follows the **successful** terminal write.
07737                     if coord_document['status'] == 'PASSED':
07738                         ledger.pass_('r10-coordination')
07739                     else:
07740                         ledger.fail('r10-coordination', coord_document['reason'])
07741                 # CORRECTION 19 (M3): the priority of the failures is
07742                 # fixed: the failure that is already unwinding wins, then
07743                 # the **exact** interruption of the cleanup and only then
07744                 # the ordinary assertion of the cleanup.
07745                 if coord_interrupts and sys.exc_info()[0] is None:
07746                     raise coord_interrupts[0]
07747                 if coord_write_failure is not None \
07748                         and sys.exc_info()[0] is None:
07749                     # CORRECTION 20 (M2): the evidence of the coordination
07750                     # is **closed** after the cleanup: the failure of the
07751                     # write is a real failure of the group and it is never
07752                     # reduced to a late incomplete ledger.
07753                     raise AssertionError(coord_write_failure)
07754                 if coord_failure is not None and sys.exc_info()[0] is None:
07755                     raise AssertionError(coord_failure)
07756         finally:
07757             coord_observer.disarm()
07758             coord_observer_patch.stop()
07759 
07760         # -- THE DYNAMIC SCHEDULE OF THE M09 OBLIGATION (N1 correction 12):
07761         #    a real ordinary fault is published for the current generation
07762         #    of the real chain, the effective timeout of the round arrives
07763         #    **before** the endpoint consumes the outcome and the original
07764         #    error object survives both a **confirmed** and a **failed**
07765         #    coordination of the round.
07766         #
07767         #    N1 correction 16 (M2): the **outer** inventory of the phases of
07768         #    this group.  Every main schedule and both negative barrier
07769         #    subcases are named **before** they run and every document that
07770         #    a schedule really wrote is marked in the inventory.  A schedule
07771         #    that was never reached is ``NOT_RUN`` and it is never
07772         #    ambiguous: the failure of an earlier schedule may never leave
07773         #    the evidence of a later one undefined.
07774         #
07775         #    N1 correction 17 (M2): every schedule of this group writes its
07776         #    documents through the ledger of the group: a schedule that was
07777         #    executed and that failed is a ``FAIL`` and the schedules that
07778         #    were never entered are ``NOT_RUN``.
07779         _m09_timeout_phase(document, plan, tmp_path,
07780                             phase='r10-timeout-confirmed',
07781                             confirm_the_worker=True, hold_the_worker=False,
07782                             expect_barrier='NOT_APPLICABLE',
07783                             inventory=r10_inventory, ledger=ledger,
07784                             store=r10_raw)
07785         _m09_timeout_phase(document, plan, tmp_path,
07786                             phase='r10-timeout-failed',
07787                             confirm_the_worker=False, hold_the_worker=True,
07788                             expect_barrier='PASSED',
07789                             inventory=r10_inventory, ledger=ledger,
07790                             store=r10_raw)
07791         # -- N1 correction 15 (M2): the barrier of the publication is
07792         #    **checked** and it **fails closed**.  The two negative
07793         #    subcases below drive the **same** failed coordination of the
07794         #    round with a bounded transparent seam that forces an
07795         #    **absent** outcome and an outcome of **another generation**.
07796         #    The premise of the test is then **refused** explicitly and
07797         #    the subcase is never counted as a successful failed
07798         #    coordination: the cancel primitive of the round never
07799         #    returns ``REQUESTED`` as if the barrier had passed.
07800         _m09_timeout_phase(document, plan, tmp_path,
07801                             phase='r10-barrier-absent',
07802                             confirm_the_worker=False, hold_the_worker=True,
07803                             barrier_probe='ABSENT', expect_barrier='REFUSED',
07804                             inventory=r10_inventory, ledger=ledger,
07805                             store=r10_raw)
07806         _m09_timeout_phase(document, plan, tmp_path,
07807                             phase='r10-barrier-wrong-generation',
07808                             confirm_the_worker=False, hold_the_worker=True,
07809                             barrier_probe='WRONG_GENERATION',
07810                             expect_barrier='REFUSED',
07811                             inventory=r10_inventory, ledger=ledger,
07812                             store=r10_raw)
07813 
07814         # -- THE REQUIRED ORDER (N1 correction 12, root preflight
07815         #    PF12-01): the ordinary failure of the read is published
07816         #    **before** the effective timeout of the round arrives and
07817         #    the endpoint consumes the outcome of its own generation
07818         #    only **after** the timeout was decided.  The gate is a
07819         #    transparent wrapper of the production wait of the worker:
07820         #    it calls the original wait and it holds the endpoint
07821         #    between that wait and the recheck of its generation.
07822         _m09_published_fault_phase(document, plan, tmp_path,
07823                                    phase='r10-published-before-timeout',
07824                                    inventory=r10_inventory, ledger=ledger,
07825                                    store=r10_raw)
07826     except BaseException as error:
07827         # CORRECTION 17 (M2): a document of a schedule that was entered
07828         # and that failed is a ``FAIL`` with the facts that it really
07829         # observed; the schedules that were never entered are ``NOT_RUN``
07830         # and they carry no fact.
07831         #
07832         # CORRECTION 18 (M2): the writing of the evidence **never**
07833         # replaces the original failure: the error of the write is
07834         # retained and the original failure is the one that leaves.
07835         #
07836         # CORRECTION 19 (M2): the recovery of the terminal documents that
07837         # a failed write left ATTEMPTED is **bounded and best effort**:
07838         # one attempt per name, no retry, no success on a missing
07839         # evidence and the terminal state of the ledger follows the
07840         # **successful** terminal write.
07841         first_failure['type'] = type(error).__name__
07842         first_failure['detail'] = str(error)
07843         for r10_name in r10_documents:
07844             if ledger.state(r10_name) != 'ATTEMPTED':
07845                 continue
07846             # CORRECTION 20 (M2): the recovery reuses the **shared fact
07847             # store** of the group: the retry carries the **full facts**
07848             # that the phase really observed - the barrier identity, the
07849             # persistence of the barrier, the error of its persistence,
07850             # the original failure, every diagnostic of the cleanup, the
07851             # quiescence and the attempts of the drain - and it is never
07852             # a generic ``FAIL`` over an empty document.
07853             recovered = dict(r10_raw.get(r10_name, {}))
07854             recovered['status'] = 'FAIL'
07855             recovered['executed'] = True
07856             recovered['reason'] = type(error).__name__
07857             recovered['detail'] = _join_details(recovered.get('detail'),
07858                                                 str(error))
07859             recovered['recovered_from'] = 'ATTEMPTED'
07860             try:
07861                 write_phase(r10_name, recovered)
07862             except BaseException as write_error:  # noqa: BLE001
07863                 first_failure.setdefault('write_errors', []).append(
07864                     {'name': r10_name,
07865                      'type': type(write_error).__name__,
07866                      'detail': str(write_error)})
07867                 _note_interruption(outer_interrupts, write_error)
07868                 continue
07869             r10_inventory.add(r10_name)
07870             ledger.fail(r10_name, type(error).__name__)
07871         raise
07872     finally:
07873         # The schedules that were **not** reached are ``NOT_RUN``: they
07874         # carry no fact of a schedule that was not established and they
07875         # are never ambiguous.  A document that a schedule really wrote
07876         # is never overwritten here.
07877         #
07878         # CORRECTION 18 (M2): a write that fails here never replaces the
07879         # original failure of the group: it is retained and it is
07880         # reported in the reason of the group.
07881         #
07882         # CORRECTION 19 (M2): a name is finalised as ``NOT_RUN`` **only
07883         # after** its durable write succeeded and the terminal state of
07884         # the ledger follows that write: a failed write leaves the name
07885         # PLANNED and it is reported as a persistence diagnostic.
07886         # the state of the unwinding is read **before** the writes: inside
07887         # the handler of a write the state of the frame is the write
07888         # error itself and the original failure would never be seen.
07889         outer_unwinding = sys.exc_info()[0] is not None
07890         for r10_name in r10_documents:
07891             if ledger.state(r10_name) != 'PLANNED':
07892                 continue
07893             try:
07894                 write_phase(r10_name, {'status': 'NOT_RUN',
07895                                        'reason': first_failure['type']})
07896             except BaseException as write_error:  # noqa: BLE001
07897                 first_failure.setdefault('write_errors', []).append(
07898                     {'name': r10_name,
07899                      'type': type(write_error).__name__,
07900                      'detail': str(write_error)})
07901                 _note_interruption(outer_interrupts, write_error)
07902                 # CORRECTION 20 (M3): an **exact** cancellation of the
07903                 # write of the inventory is **never** raised in the middle
07904                 # of the inventory: it is retained and it is re-raised
07905                 # **after** the durable summary of the group, as the
07906                 # **first** exact pending interruption of the group.
07907                 continue
07908             r10_inventory.add(r10_name)
07909             ledger.finish(r10_name, 'NOT_RUN', first_failure['type'])
07910         # CORRECTION 20 (M2): the **final accounting** of the group is
07911         # **durable**.  It is written **once** and **after** every action
07912         # of the inventory: it carries the intended state of every name,
07913         # the names that were not persisted, the full facts of every
07914         # document, the original failure of the group and **every**
07915         # additional error of a write.  The summary never replaces the
07916         # original failure and the group never claims that the failure
07917         # log is durable when its own write failed.
07918         summary_states = {name: ledger.state(name) for name in r10_documents}
07919         summary_incomplete = [name for name in r10_documents
07920                               if summary_states[name]
07921                               not in ('PASSED', 'FAIL', 'NOT_RUN')
07922                               or name not in r10_inventory]
07923         summary = {
07924             'status': ('FAIL' if (first_failure['type']
07925                                   or first_failure.get('write_errors')
07926                                   or summary_incomplete)
07927                        else 'PASSED'),
07928             'original_failure': dict(first_failure),
07929             'write_errors': list(first_failure.get('write_errors', [])),
07930             'states': summary_states,
07931             'written': sorted(r10_inventory),
07932             'not_persisted': [item['name']
07933                               for item in first_failure.get('write_errors', [])
07934                               if item.get('name') in r10_documents],
07935             'incomplete': summary_incomplete,
07936             'facts': {name: r10_raw.get(name, {}) for name in r10_documents},
07937             'interrupts': _interruption_facts(outer_interrupts),
07938             'inventory_size': len(r10_documents),
07939             'unwinding': bool(outer_unwinding),
07940         }
07941         try:
07942             write_phase('r10-final-summary', summary)
07943         except BaseException as write_error:  # noqa: BLE001
07944             first_failure.setdefault('write_errors', []).append(
07945                 {'name': 'r10-final-summary',
07946                  'type': type(write_error).__name__,
07947                  'detail': str(write_error)})
07948             _note_interruption(outer_interrupts, write_error)
07949     # CORRECTION 19 (M3): the **exact** object of a cancellation that the
07950     # inventory of the group met is re-raised by the owner of the group:
07951     # the priority is the failure that is already unwinding, then the
07952     # exact interruption and only then the ordinary assertion.
07953     if outer_interrupts:
07954         raise outer_interrupts[0]
07955     # CORRECTION 19 (M2): the terminal accounting of the group is
07956     # **complete** and it is checked **only** when the group really
07957     # passed: every name of the inventory has a terminal state of the
07958     # ledger and a document that was really written, and a passing test
07959     # carries **no** persistence diagnostic at all.
07960     assert first_failure['type'] is None, \
07961         'the group of the M09 obligation failed: %s' % (first_failure,)
07962     assert not first_failure.get('write_errors'), \
07963         'the evidence of the group carried a persistence diagnostic: %s' \
07964         % (first_failure.get('write_errors'),)
07965     for r10_name in r10_documents:
07966         assert ledger.state(r10_name) in ('PASSED', 'FAIL', 'NOT_RUN'), \
07967             'the terminal state of %s is %s' \
07968             % (r10_name, ledger.state(r10_name))
07969         assert r10_name in r10_inventory, \
07970             'the document of %s was never written' % (r10_name,)
07971 
07972 # -- N10-R11: the overflow of the channel is a named failure -------------
07973 
07974 def test_n10_r11_the_overflow_of_the_channel_is_a_named_failure(tmp_path):
07975     """N10-R11 (M09): the overflow of the channel is named where it is.
07976 
07977     Obligation: a complete frame with a newline that is too long and a
07978     frame that is one byte beyond the limit are recorded at the place
07979     where they really happen, as a **named** sticky error of the
07980     channel with the original facts of the overflow.  A close that
07981     happens before the read consumes the frame carries that error: the
07982     channel that overflowed is never a channel that ended normally and
07983     the broker is never released as if there were no error.  The limits
07984     of the package do not move: the exact frame fits and the frame that
07985     is one byte longer does not.
07986 
07987     Entry: ``StreamChannel._drain``, ``StreamChannel._record_overflow``,
07988     ``StreamChannel.close``, ``BrokerProcess._close_channel`` and
07989     ``NativeCustodySession.stop``.  Trigger: the channel of the round
07990     reads a frame that does not fit and the round is stopped twice.
07991     Oracle: the error of the reader is the named overflow, the facts of
07992     the overflow travel with the close and the broker is kept.  Negative:
07993     the exact frame still fits and the one byte beyond the limit does
07994     not.  Returned state: the facts of the two stops name the overflow.
07995     Salvage: the owner of the test stops the round in every case.
07996     """
07997     document = _live_plan_document()
07998     plan = validate_launch_plan(document, now=time.time())
07999     limit = 32 * 1024
08000     # The exact frame and the frame that is one byte longer are strictly
08001     # adjacent and the limit of the package does not move.
08002     exact = b'y' * (limit - 1) + b'\n'
08003     one_over = b'x' * limit + b'\n'
08004     assert len(exact) == limit
08005     assert len(one_over) == limit + 1
08006 
08007     api = _F5Api()
08008     session, holder, channel_closes, channel_facts = _chain(
08009         api, document, plan, tmp_path)
08010     try:
08011         session.start()
08012         # The owner of the test publishes the frame that does not fit
08013         # into the channel of the round: the frame is a **complete**
08014         # frame with a newline that is one byte beyond the limit.
08015         assert api.read_waiting.wait(15.0), \
08016             'the reader of the chain was never inside its read'
08017         api.publish_outcome('FRAME', one_over)
08018         # The channel of the round reads the frame that does not fit.
08019         deadline = time.monotonic() + 15.0
08020         while holder['channel']._error is None and time.monotonic() < deadline:
08021             time.sleep(0.02)
08022         channel = holder['channel']
08023         facts = {'error': channel._error, 'overflow': channel.overflow,
08024                  'overflow_facts': channel._overflow_facts}
08025         write_phase('r11-oversize', facts)
08026         assert channel.overflow is True
08027         assert channel._error == 'BROKER_CHANNEL_OVERFLOW', \
08028             'the overflow was not recorded as a named failure'
08029         assert channel._overflow_facts.get('reason') == 'FRAME_TOO_LARGE'
08030         # The two real stops of the round carry the named error.
08031         first = session.stop()
08032         first_facts = first['broker_close_facts'] or {}
08033         write_phase('r11-first', {'reader_error':
08034                                   first_facts.get('channel_reader_error'),
08035                                   'overflow': first_facts.get('channel_overflow'),
08036                                   'state': first['state']})
08037         assert first_facts.get('channel_reader_error') \
08038             == 'BROKER_CHANNEL_OVERFLOW', \
08039             'the close of the round lost the overflow'
08040         assert first_facts.get('channel_overflow') is True
08041         assert first['broker_retained'] is True
08042         assert first['state'] == 'UNKNOWN_RETAINED'
08043         second = session.stop()
08044         second_facts = second['broker_close_facts'] or {}
08045         write_phase('r11-second', {'reader_error':
08046                                     second_facts.get('channel_reader_error'),
08047                                     'source':
08048                                     second_facts.get('channel_reader_error_source'),
08049                                     'state': second['state']})
08050         assert second_facts.get('channel_reader_error') \
08051             == 'BROKER_CHANNEL_OVERFLOW', \
08052             'the second attempt lost the overflow'
08053         assert second['broker_retained'] is True
08054         assert second['state'] == 'UNKNOWN_RETAINED'
08055     finally:
08056         try:
08057             session.stop()
08058         except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 - the evidence keeps the failure
08059             pass
08060 
08061     # The exact frame still fits: the limits of the package did not move.
08062     blocks = [exact]
08063     channel = StreamChannel(None, None, read_endpoint=_BlockReader(blocks),
08064                             write_endpoint=_BlockWriter(), clock=time.monotonic)
08065     try:
08066         deadline = time.monotonic() + 15.0
08067         while channel._count == 0 and time.monotonic() < deadline:
08068             time.sleep(0.02)
08069         write_phase('r11-exact', {'count': channel._count,
08070                                   'overflow': channel.overflow,
08071                                   'error': channel._error})
08072         assert channel._count == 1, \
08073             'the exact frame did not fit into the channel'
08074         assert channel.overflow is False
08075         assert channel._error is None
08076     finally:
08077         channel.close()
08078 
08079     # The frame count of the channel: the frame that is one beyond the
08080     # number of the frames of the package is refused with the same named
08081     # error.  The parent of the round may never deliver that many
08082     # frames - its own budget is smaller - and the channel alone is the
08083     # owner that may prove it.
08084     blocks = [b'z\n'] * 1025
08085     channel = StreamChannel(None, None, read_endpoint=_BlockReader(blocks),
08086                             write_endpoint=_BlockWriter(), clock=time.monotonic)
08087     try:
08088         deadline = time.monotonic() + 15.0
08089         while channel._error is None and time.monotonic() < deadline:
08090             time.sleep(0.02)
08091         write_phase('r11-frames', {'error': channel._error,
08092                                    'overflow': channel.overflow,
08093                                    'overflow_facts': channel._overflow_facts,
08094                                    'frames': channel.frames})
08095         assert channel.overflow is True
08096         assert channel._error == 'BROKER_CHANNEL_OVERFLOW'
08097         assert channel._overflow_facts.get('reason') == 'TOO_MANY_FRAMES'
08098     finally:
08099         channel.close()
08100 
08101 
08102 class _BlockReader:
08103     """A read end of the channel that returns the blocks it was given."""
08104 
08105     def __init__(self, blocks):
08106         self.blocks = list(blocks)
08107 
08108     def read(self, deadline=None, cancel=None):
08109         if self.blocks:
08110             return self.blocks.pop(0)
08111         return b''
08112 
08113     def close(self):
08114         return {'closed': True, 'retained_workers': 0,
08115                 'worker_thread_handle_released': True}
08116 
08117 
08118 class _BlockWriter:
08119     """A write end of the channel that counts its own closes."""
08120 
08121     def __init__(self):
08122         self.closes = 0
08123 
08124     def write(self, raw, deadline=None, cancel=None):
08125         return len(raw)
08126 
08127     def close(self):
08128         self.closes += 1
08129         return {'closed': True, 'retained_workers': 0,
08130                 'worker_thread_handle_released': True}
08131 
08132 
08133 # -- N10-R12: the causal order of the reader ------------------------------
08134 
08135 def test_n10_r12_the_reader_publishes_its_wait_before_it_waits(tmp_path):
08136     """N10-R12 (test causality): the reader publishes its real state.
08137 
08138     Obligation: the reader of the chain publishes the event that it is
08139     really inside its read **before** it waits, so that the owner of
08140     the test never waits for an event that the reader publishes only
08141     after a timeout.  The external owner waits for that event, it sets
08142     the **same** cancel flag of the **same** channel without naming an
08143     owner and it publishes an explicit confirmation; the public stop of
08144     the round is driven only after that confirmation.  The code of the
08145     cancellation is the one that the **real** observation layer raised
08146     - the read endpoint of the channel or the wait of the channel
08147     between two reads - and the origin of the cancellation stays
08148     unknown: the close of the channel may never be taken for the owner
08149     of this cancellation.
08150 
08151     Entry: ``_F5Api.read_stream`` and
08152     ``_F5ExternalCancelOwner.run``.  Trigger: the round is started and
08153     the reader of the chain enters its read.  Oracle: the event of the
08154     reader was published before the owner set the flag, the origin of
08155     the cancellation is really unknown and the reader of the chain
08156     keeps the error of the cancellation of the other owner.  Negative:
08157     the owner never sets the flag before the reader is really inside
08158     its read.  Returned state: the confirmation of the owner is
08159     published and the facts of the close name the error of the reader.
08160     Salvage: the exact thread of the owner is joined by the owner of
08161     the test, in every case and with a bounded wait.
08162     """
08163     document = _live_plan_document()
08164     plan = validate_launch_plan(document, now=time.time())
08165     api = _F5Api()
08166     session, holder, channel_closes, channel_facts = _chain(
08167         api, document, plan, tmp_path)
08168     owner = _F5ExternalCancelOwner(api, holder)
08169     thread = threading.Thread(target=owner.run, name='lvw-n10-r12-owner')
08170     thread.start()
08171     try:
08172         session.start()
08173         # The confirmation of the external owner is published in every
08174         # case and the failure of the owner is the failure of the test.
08175         assert owner.published.wait(30.0), \
08176             'the external owner never confirmed its cancellation'
08177         if owner.error is not None:
08178             raise owner.error
08179         channel = holder['channel']
08180         facts = {'read_waiting': bool(api.read_waiting.is_set()),
08181                  'flag_set': owner.flag_set,
08182                  'flag_owner': owner.flag_owner,
08183                  'frames': len(api.frames)}
08184         write_phase('r12-owner', facts)
08185         # The reader of the chain published its wait before it waited.
08186         assert api.read_waiting.is_set()
08187         # The flag of the cancel is really set and its origin is really
08188         # unknown: the close of the channel may never be taken for the
08189         # owner of this cancellation.
08190         assert owner.flag_set is True
08191         assert channel._cancel.is_set()
08192         assert channel._cancel.owner() is None
08193         assert owner.flag_owner is None
08194         # The public stop of the round is driven only after the
08195         # confirmation of the owner.
08196         first = session.stop()
08197         close_facts = first['broker_close_facts'] or {}
08198         write_phase('r12-stop', {'state': first['state'],
08199                                   'reader_error':
08200                                   close_facts.get('channel_reader_error'),
08201                                   'retained': first['broker_retained']})
08202         # The code of the cancellation is the one that the real
08203         # observation layer raised and it is one of the two codes that
08204         # the production names for the cancellation of the channel.
08205         assert close_facts.get('channel_reader_error') in CANCELLATION_CODES, \
08206             'the cancellation of the other owner was not classified'
08207         # It is **not** the cancellation of the close of the channel.
08208         assert close_facts.get('close_cancellation') is None, \
08209             'the cancellation of another owner was taken for the close'
08210         assert first['broker_retained'] is True
08211         assert first['state'] == 'UNKNOWN_RETAINED'
08212     finally:
08213         # The exact thread of the owner of the test is joined in every
08214         # case and with a bounded wait.
08215         thread.join(30.0)
08216         assert not thread.is_alive(), 'the owner of the round was left behind'
08217         try:
08218             session.stop()
08219         except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 - the evidence keeps the failure
08220             pass
08221 
08222 
08223 # -- N10-R13: the fault is unlocked by the owner of the test ---------------
08224 
08225 def test_n10_r13_the_fault_is_unlocked_by_the_owner_and_observed_first(tmp_path):
08226     """N10-R13 (M09): the fault of the read is observed before the stop.
08227 
08228     Obligation: the ordinary failure of the read, the frame that never
08229     ends and the end of the stream are published by the **owner of the
08230     test** and they are never unlocked by the write of the stop: the
08231     owner waits for the real outcome - the error, the frame or the end
08232     of the stream - and it observes it **before** it drives the public
08233     stop of the round.  Every phase of the round writes its bounded
08234     document and every value of it is a value that the production
08235     returned.
08236 
08237     Entry: ``_F5Api.publish_read_fault``,
08238     ``_F5Api.publish_partial_frame`` and
08239     ``NativeCustodySession.stop``.  Trigger: the ordinary failure of
08240     the read of the chain, the frame that never ends and the end of the
08241     stream.  Oracle: the failure was really published before the stop,
08242     the error of the reader is the error of the round and the partial
08243     frame keeps its own name.  Negative: the write of the stop never
08244     unlocks a failure that should have happened first.  Returned state:
08245     the phase documents are on the disk.  Salvage: the owner of the test
08246     stops the round in every case.
08247     """
08248     document = _live_plan_document()
08249     plan = validate_launch_plan(document, now=time.time())
08250 
08251     # -- PHASE ONE: the ordinary failure of the read --------------------
08252     api = _F5Api(read_fault=RuntimeError('read_fault'))
08253     session, holder, channel_closes, channel_facts = _chain(
08254         api, document, plan, tmp_path)
08255     try:
08256         session.start()
08257         assert api.read_waiting.wait(15.0), \
08258             'the reader of the chain was never inside its read'
08259         api.publish_read_fault()
08260         assert api.fault_observed.wait(15.0), \
08261             'the ordinary failure of the read never happened'
08262         reader = holder['read_endpoint']._reader
08263         assert reader is not None
08264         assert reader.outcome_published.wait(15.0), \
08265             'the outcome of the read was never published'
08266         first = session.stop()
08267         facts = first['broker_close_facts'] or {}
08268         write_phase('r13-fault', {'reader_error':
08269                                    facts.get('channel_reader_error'),
08270                                    'state': first['state'],
08271                                    'retained': first['broker_retained']})
08272         assert facts.get('channel_reader_error') == 'RuntimeError', \
08273             'the failure of the read was swallowed by the stop'
08274         assert first['broker_retained'] is True
08275         assert first['state'] == 'UNKNOWN_RETAINED'
08276     finally:
08277         try:
08278             session.stop()
08279         except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 - the evidence keeps the failure
08280             pass
08281 
08282     # -- PHASE TWO: the frame that never ends and the end of the stream --
08283     api = _F5Api(partial_frame=b'{"schema": "lvw.s3-native-custody-channel/1"')
08284     session, holder, channel_closes, channel_facts = _chain(
08285         api, document, plan, tmp_path)
08286     try:
08287         session.start()
08288         assert api.read_waiting.wait(15.0), \
08289             'the reader of the chain was never inside its read'
08290         api.publish_partial_frame()
08291         assert api.eof_observed.wait(15.0), \
08292             'the end of the stream was never observed'
08293         channel = holder['channel']
08294         deadline = time.monotonic() + 15.0
08295         while channel._error is None and time.monotonic() < deadline:
08296             time.sleep(0.02)
08297         first = session.stop()
08298         facts = first['broker_close_facts'] or {}
08299         write_phase('r13-partial', {'reader_error':
08300                                     facts.get('channel_reader_error'),
08301                                     'state': first['state'],
08302                                     'retained': first['broker_retained']})
08303         assert facts.get('channel_reader_error') \
08304             == 'BROKER_CHANNEL_PARTIAL_FRAME', \
08305             'the partial frame was dropped as a normal end'
08306         assert first['broker_retained'] is True
08307         assert first['state'] == 'UNKNOWN_RETAINED'
08308     finally:
08309         try:
08310             session.stop()
08311         except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 - the evidence keeps the failure
08312             pass

08315 # -- N10-R14: the compatibility of the new candidate ----------------------
08316 
08317 def test_n10_r14_the_closed_obligations_of_the_package_do_not_regress(tmp_path):
08318     """N10-R14 (compatibility): the closed obligations stay closed.
08319 
08320     Obligation: the candidate of this correction keeps every closed
08321     obligation of the package: the unknown state is latched under one
08322     lock, the budget of the parent and of the child is one budget in
08323     both directions, the reservation of the terminal exchange is
08324     consumed by the terminal frame itself, a cancellation of a normal
08325     close keeps its source and a first error survives a retry.  The
08326     identity and the order of the 175 frozen nodes are untouched: the
08327     new groups are **appended** and they never replace an old one.
08328 
08329     Entry: ``NativeCustodySession._latch_unknown_state``,
08330     ``NativeCustodySession._check_frame_budget``,
08331     ``NativeCustodySession._send`` and ``_ChannelCancel``.  Trigger: a
08332     round that is stopped and a round whose reader failed.  Oracle: the
08333     latches and the budgets keep their facts.  Negative: a later frame
08334     never borrows the reservation of the terminal one.  Returned state:
08335     the facts of the round are named.  Salvage: the owner of the test
08336     stops the round in every case.
08337     """
08338     document = _live_plan_document()
08339     plan = validate_launch_plan(document, now=time.time())
08340     clock = ManualClock(1000.0)
08341     api = _F5Api()
08342     session, holder, channel_closes, channel_facts = _chain(
08343         api, document, plan, tmp_path)
08344     try:
08345         session.start()
08346         # The unknown state is latched under one lock and it is never
08347         # cleared.  The latch is checked on a **separate** session, so
08348         # that the round that is stopped below keeps its own state.
08349         probe = NativeCustodySession(
08350             plan=document, run_root=tmp_path,
08351             parent_child_remaining_seconds=60,
08352             launcher=lambda *args, **kwargs: None, clock=clock,
08353             wall=time.time, nonce=NONCE)
08354         assert probe._latch_unknown_state() is True
08355         assert probe._latch_unknown_state() is False
08356         assert probe._context.is_unknown() is True
08357         # The reservation of the terminal exchange is consumed by the
08358         # terminal frame itself and a normal frame never borrows it.
08359         session._check_frame_budget(terminal=False)
08360         session._check_frame_budget(terminal=True)
08361         # The cancel flag of a normal close keeps its source.
08362         cancel = _ChannelCancel()
08363         source = broker_module._CloseSource(1)
08364         cancel.set(source)
08365         assert cancel.owner() is source
08366         assert cancel.owner() is not broker_module._CloseSource(1)
08367         facts = {'unknown': probe._context.is_unknown(),
08368                  'frames_sent': session._frames_sent,
08369                  'frames_received': session._frames_received,
08370                  'cancel_owner': cancel.owner().owner}
08371         write_phase('r14-compatibility', facts)
08372         first = session.stop()
08373         assert first['state'] in ('UNKNOWN_RETAINED', 'RELEASED')
08374     finally:
08375         try:
08376             session.stop()
08377         except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 - the evidence keeps the failure
08378             pass
